> - Fire: hitscan raycast from camera through muzzle; spread cone; tracer visual; muzzle flash light+particles; shell eject; recoil; ammo 30 + reserve; reload with animation timing and SFX; fire modes single/auto.
> - Damage/health: 100 HP, regen-ish? no — use health packs / chapter checkpoint restore. Damage vignette + hit direction indicator.
> - Interaction: raycast forward, detect `Interactable`, show prompt "[E] Inspect". Hold to open radial menu for the tavern NPC.

> - Death: fade, "You died", respawn at checkpoint.

> ### Enemy AI
> - `CharacterBody3D` + `NavigationAgent3D`.
> - NavigationRegion3D baked at runtime from procedural navmesh — hmm, baking at runtime is possible via `NavigationMeshGenerator`? In Godot 4, `NavigationRegion3D.bake_navigation_mesh()` works at runtime. But procedural geometry built in code needs to be in the tree and parsed by the navmesh source geometry. It works with `NavigationMeshSourceGeometryData3D` or by setting `geometry_parsed_geometry_type` to mesh instances and calling bake. Doable but risky/slow.
> 
> Simpler & robust: use a hand-authored navigation graph / waypoint graph for patrol routes, plus simple steering with obstacle avoidance (raycast whiskers). Given a flat-ish city ground plane, steering + waypoints is totally adequate and avoids navmesh pitfalls. I'll do waypoint-graph patrol + direct chase with local avoidance + "lost player → search last known position". This is robust and I control it fully. 
> 
> - FSM: IDLE → PATROL → ALERT (heard something / partial sight) → COMBAT (advance to cover range, fire in bursts) → SEARCH → DEAD.
> - Vision: cone test (dot product + distance) + raycast LOS + light-level modifier? Keep it: cone + LOS + a `visibility` bonus if player crouched (reduced detection range) and if player is in the locker (never).
> - Hearing: gunshots emit noise events with radius; enemies within radius go to ALERT/last-heard-position.
> - Combat: burst fire with accuracy falloff + reaction delay, tracer, muzzle flash, damage to player. Health bar above enemy when damaged. Death: fall over (procedural ragdoll-ish rotate to floor), drop ammo.
> - Squad coordination: when one enemy spots the player, notify others within radius.
>

> 2. **THE TAVERN** — Safehouse interior; talk to NPC (radial menu: Inspect / Talk / Wait / Leave), dialogue tree gives objective and lore. Chapter card.
> 3. **THE HUNT** — Combat escalation in the flooded market; squad fight; ammo pickups; checkpoint.
> 4. **THE DARK** — Stealth: interior, guards with flashlights patrolling, lockers, crouch, avoid vision cones. Reach the archive.
> 5. **THE MEADOW** — Exit into sunlit meadow (completely different lighting palette — bright, green, warm with god rays), walk to the light, credits roll.
> 
> That's a real game. Each chapter = a built area. That's a lot of world building but procedural helpers make it feasible: `WorldBuilder` with functions like `build_district()`, `build_plaza()`, `build_tavern()`, `build_market()`, `build_interior()`, `build_meadow()`.
> 
> To keep it manageable: each chapter is a separate area, and I place them at different positions in the world, teleporting the player between them (or better: build each area as a separate subscene/Node3D added/removed per chapter). That's clean: `Chapter` nodes with `build()` and `spawn_point`. Only one chapter exists at a time → performance is fine.

> Careful with Godot 4.7 specifics. Let me recall key API (Godot 4.x):
> - `CharacterBody3D`: `velocity`, `move_and_slide()`, `is_on_floor()`.
> - `Input.get_vector`, `Input.is_action_pressed`.
> - `NavigationAgent3D`: `target_position`, `get_next_path_position()`, signals `velocity_computed`. I'm using waypoints manually, so maybe skip NavigationAgent3D entirely and just steer. Yes — simpler and robust. I'll use raycast whiskers for avoidance.
> - `RayCast3D`, `ShapeCast3D`.
> - `GPUParticles3D` with `ParticleProcessMaterial`.
> - `WorldEnvironment` with `Environment`: 
>   - `environment.background_mode = Environment.BG_SKY` with `sky = Sky.new()` and `SkyMaterial` — for Godot 4.7 the sky material is `ProceduralSkyMaterial`. 
>   - `fog_enabled`, `fog_light_color`, `fog_density`, `fog_aerial_perspective`, `fog_sky_affect`.
>   - `volumetric_fog_enabled`, `volumetric_fog_density`, `volumetric_fog_albedo`, `volumetric_fog_emission`, `volumetric_fog_length`, `volumetric_fog_gi_inject`, `volumetric_fog_anisotropy`.
>   - `ssao_enabled`, `ssao_radius`, `ssao_intensity`, `ssao_power`, `ssao_detail`.
>   - `ssil_enabled`.
>   - `ssr_enabled`, `ssr_max_steps`, `ssr_fade_in`, `ssr_fade_out`.
>   - `glow_enabled`, `glow_intensity`, `glow_strength`, `glow_bloom`, `glow_hdr_threshold`, `glow_blend_mode`, `glow_levels/1..7`.
>   - `tonemap_mode` = Environment.TONE_MAPPER_ACES, `tonemap_white`.
>   - `adjustment_enabled`, `adjustment_brightness`, `adjustment_contrast`, `adjustment_saturation`.
>   - `ambient_light_source`, `ambient_light_color`, `ambient_light_energy`, `ambient_light_sky_contribution`.

> Let me carefully write gen_audio.py.
> 
> ```python
> #!/usr/bin/env python3
> """Procedural audio generator for MONOLITH: A Century of Ash."""
> import numpy as np, wave, os, subprocess, math
> 
> SR = 44100
> OUT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "assets", "audio")
> RAW = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", ".raw_audio")
> ```

> Let me write full DSP.
> 
> ```python
> def rng(seed): return np.random.default_rng(seed)
> 
> def nz(n, seed=0):
>     return rng(seed).standard_normal(n).astype(np.float32)
> 
> # biquad
> def biquad(x, b, a):
>     y = np.zeros_like(x)
>     x1=x2=y1=y2=0.0
>     for i in range(len(x)):
>         ...
> ```
> Python loop over 44100*60 samples = 2.6M iterations per filter → way too slow in pure Python. Need vectorized filtering.
> 
> Options: use `scipy.signal.lfilter` — is scipy available? Unknown. Let me check. Alternatively implement IIR via `np.convolve` with an FIR approximation — easy and vectorized: design FIR via windowed sinc or just convolve with an exponential-decay impulse response (that gives a one-pole lowpass: y = x * h where h[k] = (1-a) a^k). One-pole lowpass as FIR convolution is exact for causal one-pole: y[n] = (1-a) * sum_{k>=0} a^k x[n-k]. Truncate h at ~ 8 time constants. That's exact (up to truncation) and uses FFT convolution → fast. 
> 
> So:
> ```python
> def onepole_lp(x, cutoff):
>     a = math.exp(-2*math.pi*cutoff/SR)
>     n = int(min(len(x), 8/(-math.log(a)) if a>0 else 64))
>     h = (1-a) * a**np.arange(n)
>     return np.convolve(x, h)[:len(x)].astype(np.float32)
> ```
> Convolve with FFT? `np.convolve` is direct; for n=100 and len=2.6M that's 260M ops — too slow. Use FFT: `scipy.signal.fftconvolve` needed... or implement with `np.fft`:
> ```python
> def fftconv(x, h):
>     n = len(x)+len(h)-1
>     nfft = 1<<int(math.ceil(math.log2(n)))
>     return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(x, nfft)*np.fft.rfft(h, nfft), nfft)[:len(x)]
> ```
> Good, fully vectorized with numpy. 
> 
> Highpass = x - lowpass(x, cutoff). Bandpass = lp(hp(x)) style.
> 
> Resonant filters: I can approximate with a two-pole resonator FIR: h[k] = r^k * sin(w0 k) → impulse response of a resonator. Convolution with the truncation gives a resonant bandpass. 
> 
> ```python
> def resonator(x, freq, decay):  # decay = time in seconds to -60dB
>     r = math.exp(-6.907755/(decay*SR))
>     n = int(min(len(x), max(16, 8*decay*SR)))
>     k = np.arange(n)
>     h = (r**k) * np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*k/SR)
>     return fftconv(x, h)
> ```
> This is a bandpass-ish resonant filter — great for metallic ring/snare body. 
> 
> Better: for general bandpass, apply lp then hp (each as one-pole FIR). Fine.
> 
> Reverb (Schroeder): comb filters = feedback delay lines; implement via `lfilter`-like recursion — again needs a loop. Alternative reverb: convolve with a synthetic noise impulse response that has exponential decay and progressive lowpass. FFT convolution handles it fast, and it sounds like a real room.
> 
> ```python
> def make_ir(decay=1.2, seed=0, damp=6000, sr=SR):
>     n = int(decay*sr)
>     t = np.arange(n)/sr
>     ir = nz(n, seed) * np.exp(-6.9*t/decay)
>     # progressive damping by chunk-wise lowpass
>     ...
> ```
> Damping: apply a lowpass whose cutoff decreases with time — approximate by filtering the IR with a fixed lowpass (fine) plus early-reflection spikes. Good enough for a game.
> 
> Add early reflections: spikes at ~ 7ms, 11ms, 19ms, 29ms with decreasing amplitude.
> 
> ```python
> def reverb(x, decay=1.2, mix=0.3, damp=5000, seed=1):
>     ir = make_ir(decay, seed, damp)
>     w = fftconv(x, ir)
>     w = normalize(w, 1.0)
>     return (1-mix)*x + mix*w
> ```
> 
> Env: ADSR as a vectorized envelope builder.
> 
> ```python
> def env_ad(n, a, d, sr=SR):
>     t = np.arange(n)/sr
>     return np.where(t<a, t/max(a,1e-6), np.exp(-(t-a)/max(d,1e-6)))
> ```
> "AD" percussive envelope: linear attack then exponential decay. Great for impacts/guns.
> 
> `env_adsr(n, a, d, s, r)` for pads.
> 
> Sweep frequency for kick: sine with instantaneous frequency f(t) = f1 + (f0-f1)*exp(-t/tau). Phase = integral. Vectorized: phase = 2π*(f1*t + (f0-f1)*tau*(1-exp(-t/tau))).
> 
> Good.

> def pad(freqs, dur, amp=0.18, detune=0.006, attack=1.4, release=2.0, cutoff=1400):

> Bell/piano (FM):
> ```python
> def bell(f, dur, amp=0.3, ratio=3.01, index=1.6, decay=2.2):

>     idx = index*np.exp(-t/ (decay*0.12))
>     ph_c = 2*np.pi*f*t
>     ph_m = 2*np.pi*f*ratio*t
>     y = np.sin(ph_c + idx*np.sin(ph_m))
>     return y*np.exp(-t/decay)*amp
> ```

> Sub bass:

> ```
> 
> Percussion: kick(f0=140,f1=48,decay 0.35), snare(noise + resonator 190Hz), hat(highpass noise 0.03s).

> Noise/pulse layers for tension: filtered noise with tremolo.

> Then reverb + soft clip + normalize + fade edges for seamless looping (crossfade the tail into the head).
> 
> Loop trick: render the track, then create a loop by crossfading the last L samples with the first L samples:
> ```python
> def make_loop(x, xfade=1.5):
>     L = int(xfade*SR)
>     head = x[:L]; tail = x[-L:]
>     w = np.linspace(0,1,L)
>     x = x[:-L].copy()
>     x[:L] = head*w + tail*(1-w)
>     return x
> ```
> Hmm, that's not quite right — the standard approach: `x[:L] = x[:L]*w + x[-L:]*(1-w)` then drop the last L. The head fades in from the tail. Yes as written. Good.
> 
> Track length must be an exact number of bars so rhythmic content loops (crossfade at non-zero-crossing is fine, it's smooth).
> 
> OK, and for the loop to be rhythmically exact I should compose exactly N bars and then do the crossfade only over the reverb tail... Actually crossfading the *beginning with the end* of rhythmic material will smear the beat. Better: compose N bars + tail_extra (reverb ring-out), then wrap the extra tail back onto the start (add it), producing an exact loop:
> ```python
> def wrap_tail(x, bars_len):
>     n = bars_len
>     extra = len(x)-n
>     y = x[:n].copy()
>     y[:extra] += x[n:]
>     return y
> ```
> That's the correct "tail wrap" technique for seamless loops.

{
  "path": "tools/gen_audio.py",
  "instructions": "Create the procedural audio generator that synthesizes all SFX and music for the game.",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nMONOLITH: A Century of Ash\nProcedural audio pipeline.\n\nSynthesizes the entire soundtrack and SFX library from scratch with numpy --\nno sample packs, no external assets.  Output is converted to Ogg Vorbis by\nffmpeg so the game ships with compressed, streaming-friendly audio.\n\n    python3 tools/gen_audio.py\n\nDesign notes\n------------\n* All IIR filtering is expressed as truncated-FIR + FFT convolution so it is\n  fully vectorised.  A causal one-pole lowpass y[n] = (1-a) * sum a^k x[n-k]\n  is exact once the impulse response is truncated past ~8 time constants, and\n  a two-pole resonator is h[k] = r^k sin(w0 k).  No python-level sample loops.\n* Music is composed on a bar grid, rendered into a buffer that is longer than\n  the loop by a reverb tail, then the tail is *wrapped back onto the head*.\n  That produces a mathematically seamless loop without crossfade smearing.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport shutil\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nSR = 44100\nROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))\nOUT_DIR = os.path.join(ROOT, \"assets\", \"audio\")\nRAW_DIR = os.path.join(ROOT, \".raw_audio\")\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# DSP primitives\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef nz(n, seed=0):\n    \"\"\"Gaussian white noise.\"\"\"\n    return np.random.default_rng(seed).standard_normal(n)\n\n\ndef fftconv(x, h):\n    \"\"\"Fast linear convolution, trimmed back to len(x).\"\"\"\n    if len(h) == 0 or len(x) == 0:\n        return np.zeros_like(x)\n    n = len(x) + len(h) - 1\n    nfft = 1 << int(math.ceil(math.log2(max(n, 2))))\n    y = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(x, nfft) * np.fft.rfft(h, nfft), nfft)\n    return y[: len(x)]\n\n\ndef lp(x, cutoff, order=1):\n    \"\"\"One-pole lowpass (per pole) via exact exponential impulse response.\"\"\"\n    cutoff = max(10.0, min(cutoff, SR * 0.49))\n    a = math.exp(-2.0 * math.pi * cutoff / SR)\n    n = int(min(len(x) - 1, max(2, 8.0 / max(1e-9, -math.log(a)))))\n    h = (1.0 - a) * a ** np.arange(n)\n    for _ in range(max(1, order)):\n        x = fftconv(x, h)\n    return x\n\n\ndef hp(x, cutoff, order=1):\n    \"\"\"Complementary highpass: x - lowpass(x).\"\"\"\n    y = x\n    for _ in range(max(1, order)):\n        y = y - lp(y, cutoff)\n    return y\n\n\ndef bp(x, low, high, order=1):\n    return lp(hp(x, low, order), high, order)\n\n\ndef reson(x, freq, decay, amp=1.0):\n    \"\"\"Two-pole resonant bandpass.  `decay` = seconds to -60 dB.\"\"\"\n    r = math.exp(-6.907755 / (decay * SR))\n    n = int(min(len(x) - 1, max(16, 8 * decay * SR)))\n    k = np.arange(n)\n    h = (r ** k) * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * freq * k / SR) * amp\n    return fftconv(x, h)\n\n\ndef env_exp(n, attack=0.002, decay=0.2):\n    \"\"\"Percussive attack/exponential-decay envelope.\"\"\"\n    t = np.arange(n) / SR\n    a = max(attack, 1e-5)\n    return np.where(t < a, t / a, np.exp(-(t - a) / max(decay, 1e-5)))\n\n\ndef env_adsr(n, attack=0.01, decay=0.1, sustain=0.7, release=0.3):\n    t = np.arange(n) / SR\n    total = n / SR\n    rel_start = max(attack + decay, total - release)\n    e = np.ones(n)\n    a_end = min(n, int(attack * SR))\n    if a_end > 0:\n        e[:a_end] = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a_end)\n    d_end = min(n, a_end + int(decay * SR))\n    if d_end > a_end:\n        e[a_end:d_end] = np.linspace(1.0, sustain, d_end - a_end)\n    e[d_end:] = sustain\n    r_start = int(rel_start * SR)\n    if r_start < n:\n        e[r_start:] *= np.linspace(1.0, 0.0, n - r_start) ** 0.6\n    e[t >= total] = 0.0\n    return np.clip(e, 0.0, 1.0)\n\n\ndef sweep_sine(n, f0, f1, tau=0.06, phase0=0.0):\n    \"\"\"Sine with an exponential frequency glide f0 -> f1.\"\"\"\n    t = np.arange(n) / SR\n    ph = 2.0 * math.pi * (f1 * t + (f0 - f1) * tau * (1.0 - np.exp(-t / tau)))\n    return np.sin(ph + phase0)\n\n\ndef soft_clip(x, drive=1.6):\n    return np.tanh(x * drive) / np.tanh(drive)\n\n\ndef norm(x, peak=0.92):\n    m = float(np.max(np.abs(x))) if len(x) else 0.0\n    if m < 1e-9:\n        return x\n    return x * (peak / m)\n\n\ndef fade_edges(x, ms=3.0):\n    n = int(SR * ms / 1000.0)\n    if n * 2 >= len(x):\n        return x\n    x = x.copy()\n    x[:n] *= np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)\n    x[-n:] *= np.linspace(1.0, 0.0, n)\n    return x\n\n\n# --- reverb ---------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef make_ir(decay=1.0, damp=5200, seed=7, pre=0.012):\n    \"\"\"Synthetic room impulse response: damped noise + early reflections.\"\"\"\n    n = int(decay * SR)\n    t = np.arange(n) / SR\n    ir = nz(n, seed) * np.exp(-6.907755 * t / decay)\n    ir = lp(ir, damp, order=2)\n    ir = hp(ir, 120)\n    # early reflections give the room a size\n    for dt, g in ((0.007, 0.55), (0.011, 0.42), (0.019, 0.3), (0.029, 0.22), (0.041, 0.14)):\n        i = int((pre + dt) * SR)\n        if i < n:\n            ir[i] += g\n    return norm(ir, 1.0)\n\n\ndef reverb(x, decay=0.9, mix=0.3, damp=5200, seed=7):\n    w = norm(fftconv(x, make_ir(decay, damp, seed)), 1.0)\n    return (1.0 - mix) * x + mix * w\n\n\ndef stereo_wide(x, width=0.6, ms=0.35):\n    \"\"\"Cheap but effective width: tiny decorrelating delay + side energy.\"\"\"\n    d = int(SR * ms / 1000.0)\n    l = x\n    r = np.concatenate([np.zeros(d), x[:-d]]) if d > 0 else x\n    mid = (l + r) * 0.5\n    side = (l - r) * 0.5\n    l2 = mid + side * (1.0 + width)\n    r2 = mid - side * (1.0 + width)\n    return np.stack([l2, r2], axis=1)\n\n\ndef wrap_tail(x, loop_len):\n    \"\"\"Wrap the post-loop reverb tail back onto the head -> seamless loop.\"\"\"\n    if len(x) <= loop_len:\n        return x\n    y = x[:loop_len].copy()\n    extra = min(len(x) - loop_len, loop_len)\n    y[:extra] += x[loop_len : loop_len + extra]\n    return y\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Musical helpers\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n_SEMI = {\"C\": 0, \"D\": 2, \"E\": 4, \"F\": 5, \"G\": 7, \"A\": 9, \"B\": 11}\n\n\ndef pf(tok):\n    \"\"\"Parse a note token such as 'D3', 'Bb2', 'F#4' into a frequency.\"\"\"\n    name = tok[0].upper()\n    i = 1\n    semi = _SEMI[name]\n    while i < len(tok) and tok[i] in \"#b\":\n        semi += 1 if tok[i] == \"#\" else -1\n        i += 1\n    octave = int(tok[i:])\n    midi = 12 * (octave + 1) + semi\n    return 440.0 * 2.0 ** ((midi - 69) / 12.0)\n\n\n_PAD_CACHE = {}\n\n\ndef pad_chord(chord, dur, cutoff=1200, detune=0.005, seed=3, bright=0.3):\n    \"\"\"Warm detuned-saw pad.  Cached because chords repeat across bars.\"\"\"\n    key = (tuple(chord), round(dur, 4), cutoff, detune, seed, bright)\n    if key in _PAD_CACHE:\n        return _PAD_CACHE[key]\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    t = np.arange(n) / SR\n    out = np.zeros(n)\n    r = np.random.default_rng(seed)\n    voices = 0\n    for tok in chord:\n        f = pf(tok)\n        for d in (-detune, 0.0, detune):\n            ph = 2.0 * math.pi * f * (1.0 + d) * t + r.uniform(0, 6.283)\n            out += np.sin(ph) + bright * 0.45 * np.sin(2 * ph) + bright * 0.2 * np.sin(3 * ph)\n            voices += 1\n    out /= max(1, voices)\n    out = lp(out, cutoff, order=2)\n    out = lp(out, cutoff * 1.6)\n    _PAD_CACHE[key] = out\n    return out\n\n\ndef bell(f, dur, amp=0.3, ratio=3.01, index=1.7, decay=2.4, mod_decay=None):\n    \"\"\"FM bell / music-box voice.\"\"\"\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    t = np.arange(n) / SR\n    md = mod_decay if mod_decay is not None else decay * 0.11\n    idx = index * np.exp(-t / md)\n    y = np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f * t + idx * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f * ratio * t))\n    return y * np.exp(-t / decay) * amp\n\n\ndef sub_note(f, dur, amp=0.5, attack=0.01, release=0.25, drive=1.2):\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    t = np.arange(n) / SR\n    y = np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f * t) + 0.25 * np.sin(4.0 * math.pi * f * t)\n    y = soft_clip(y * drive, 1.3)\n    return y * env_adsr(n, attack, 0.05, 0.85, release) * amp\n\n\ndef kick(dur=0.42, f0=150.0, f1=44.0, amp=1.0):\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    y = sweep_sine(n, f0, f1, tau=0.045) * env_exp(n, 0.001, dur * 0.34)\n    click = bp(nz(n, 11) * env_exp(n, 0.0002, 0.006), 1800, 7000) * 0.45\n    return norm(y + click, amp)\n\n\ndef snare(dur=0.3, amp=1.0, seed=5):\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    body = reson(nz(n, seed), 195.0, 0.055) * 0.5\n    noise = bp(nz(n, seed + 1) * env_exp(n, 0.0004, 0.11), 700, 8000)\n    return norm(body + noise, amp)\n\n\ndef hat(dur=0.055, amp=1.0, seed=9, open_=False):\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    x = hp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.0002, dur * (0.6 if open_ else 0.22)), 6500)\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef tamb(dur=0.12, amp=1.0, seed=13):\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    x = bp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.0004, dur * 0.5), 3000, 9000, order=2)\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\nclass Track:\n    \"\"\"Bar-grid composition buffer with L/R buses and a wrapped reverb tail.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, bpm, bars, beats_per_bar=4, tail=4.0, seed=0):\n        self.bpm = bpm\n        self.bars = bars\n        self.bpb = beats_per_bar\n        self.spb = 60.0 / bpm\n        self.bar = self.spb * beats_per_bar\n        self.loop_len = int(round(bars * self.bar * SR))\n        pad = int(tail * SR)\n        self.L = np.zeros(self.loop_len + pad)\n        self.R = np.zeros(self.loop_len + pad)\n        self.seed = seed\n        self._counter = 0\n\n    def _seed(self):\n        self._counter += 1\n        return self.seed * 977 + self._counter\n\n    def t(self, bar, beat=0.0):\n        return bar * self.bar + beat * self.spb\n\n    def add(self, sig, t, gain=1.0, pan=0.0):\n        i = int(t * SR)\n        if i < 0:\n            sig = sig[-i:]\n            i = 0\n        n = min(len(sig), len(self.L) - i)\n        if n <= 0:\n            return\n        gl = math.sqrt(max(0.0, 0.5 * (1.0 - pan)))\n        gr = math.sqrt(max(0.0, 0.5 * (1.0 + pan)))\n        self.L[i : i + n] += sig[:n] * gain * gl\n        self.R[i : i + n] += sig[:n] * gain * gr\n\n    def finish(self, rev_decay=2.4, rev_mix=0.34, rev_damp=4800, drive=1.15, peak=0.9):\n        out = []\n        for i, bus in enumerate((self.L, self.R)):\n            ch = reverb(bus, rev_decay, rev_mix, rev_damp, 21 + i)\n            ch = soft_clip(ch, drive)\n            ch = wrap_tail(ch, self.loop_len)\n            ch = fade_edges(ch, 1.5)\n            out.append(ch)\n        st = np.stack(out, axis=1)\n        st = norm(st, peak)\n        return st\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# SFX definitions\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef gunshot(seed=0, amp=1.0, body=0.14, low=0.55, tail=0.6):\n    n = int((tail + 0.02) * SR)\n    t = np.arange(n) / SR\n    crack = hp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.00015, 0.005), 2600)\n    mid = bp(nz(n, seed + 1) * env_exp(n, 0.0005, body), 190, 3200)\n    thump = np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * 74.0 * t) * env_exp(n, 0.0004, 0.075) * low\n    mech = bp(nz(n, seed + 5) * env_exp(n, 0.0001, 0.0035), 4000, 12000) * 0.35\n    x = crack * 1.0 + mid * 1.15 + thump + mech\n    x = reverb(x, tail, 0.4, 3600, seed + 2)\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef mechanical_click(seed=0, lo=900, hi=7000, dur=0.07, body_f=280.0, amp=1.0):\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    click = bp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.00015, 0.006), lo, hi)\n    body = reson(nz(n, seed + 1) * env_exp(n, 0.0004, dur * 0.4), body_f, 0.02) * 0.6\n    return norm(click + body, amp)\n\n\ndef impact_concrete(seed=0, amp=1.0):\n    n = int(0.4 * SR)\n    x = lp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.0004, 0.05), 1700, order=2)\n    x += reson(nz(n, seed + 1), 620.0, 0.03) * 0.25\n    x = reverb(x, 0.5, 0.35, 3000, seed + 2)\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef impact_metal(seed=0, amp=1.0):\n    n = int(0.55 * SR)\n    x = np.zeros(n)\n    for f, d, g in ((1240, 0.22, 1.0), (1830, 0.16, 0.7), (2670, 0.11, 0.5), (3910, 0.07, 0.3)):\n        x += reson(nz(n, seed), f, d) * g\n    x += bp(nz(n, seed + 3) * env_exp(n, 0.0002, 0.02), 2500, 9000) * 0.7\n    x = reverb(x, 0.6, 0.3, 6000, seed + 4)\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef impact_flesh(seed=0, amp=1.0):\n    n = int(0.28 * SR)\n    x = lp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.0006, 0.055), 900, order=2)\n    x += np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * 130.0 * np.arange(n) / SR) * env_exp(n, 0.0005, 0.04)\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef impact_water(seed=0, amp=1.0):\n    n = int(0.6 * SR)\n    x = bp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.002, 0.11), 500, 5200)\n    x += reson(nz(n, seed + 1) * env_exp(n, 0.001, 0.06), 900.0, 0.05) * 0.4\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef footstep(seed=0, kind=\"concrete\", amp=1.0):\n    n = int(0.3 * SR)\n    if kind == \"concrete\":\n        x = bp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.0006, 0.032), 260, 3400)\n        x += np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * 95.0 * np.arange(n) / SR) * env_exp(n, 0.0008, 0.028) * 0.5\n    elif kind == \"gravel\":\n        x = bp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.0003, 0.07), 900, 7000, order=2)\n    elif kind == \"water\":\n        x = bp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.001, 0.13), 420, 5600)\n        x += bp(nz(n, seed + 1) * env_exp(n, 0.004, 0.2), 300, 2600) * 0.6\n    elif kind == \"grass\":\n        x = bp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.0009, 0.05), 1400, 8000, order=2)\n        x += bp(nz(n, seed + 1) * env_exp(n, 0.002, 0.09), 500, 2200) * 0.35\n    else:  # metal / interior\n        x = bp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.0004, 0.03), 700, 6000)\n        x += reson(nz(n, seed + 2), 1500.0, 0.05) * 0.4\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef water_lap(seed=0, amp=1.0, dur=3.0):\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    x = bp(nz(n, seed), 300, 2800) * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 0.4 * np.arange(n) / SR))\n    x = lp(x, 2400)\n    # slow amplitude swells\n    lfo = 0.45 + 0.55 * np.abs(np.sin(2 * np.pi * 0.23 * np.arange(n) / SR + 0.7))\n    x *= lfo\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef ambience_noise(dur, seed=0, lo=180, hi=3800, smooth=2500, amp=1.0, brown=True):\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    if brown:\n        x = np.cumsum(nz(n, seed))\n        x = x / (np.max(np.abs(x)) + 1e-9)\n    else:\n        x = nz(n, seed)\n    x = bp(x, lo, hi)\n    x = lp(x, smooth, order=2)\n    lfo = 0.6 + 0.4 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 0.07 * np.arange(n) / SR + seed)\n    return norm(x * lfo, amp)\n\n\ndef rain_layer(dur, seed=0, amp=1.0, intensity=1.0):\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    hiss = bp(nz(n, seed), 900, 9000)\n    hiss = lp(hiss, 7000, order=2) * 0.7\n    # individual drop transients\n    drops = np.zeros(n)\n    r = np.random.default_rng(seed + 4)\n    count = int(dur * 90 * intensity)\n    for _ in range(count):\n        i = r.integers(0, max(1, n - 900))\n        d = bp(nz(900, int(r.integers(0, 9999))) * env_exp(900, 0.0002, 0.006), 2200, 11000)\n        drops[i : i + 900] += d * r.uniform(0.25, 0.9)\n    x = hiss + drops * 0.5\n    x = soft_clip(x, 1.2)\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef ui_blip(f=880.0, dur=0.09, amp=0.5, kind=\"sine\", seed=0):\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    t = np.arange(n) / SR\n    if kind == \"sine\":\n        y = np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * t) + 0.25 * np.sin(4 * np.pi * f * t)\n    elif kind == \"square\":\n        y = np.sign(np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * t)) * 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * t)\n    else:\n        y = nz(n, seed)\n    y *= env_exp(n, 0.0015, dur * 0.35)\n    y = lp(y, 6000)\n    return norm(y, amp)\n\n\ndef ui_sequence(freqs, dur_each=0.1, amp=0.5, kind=\"sine\"):\n    seg = [ui_blip(f, dur_each, 1.0, kind) for f in freqs]\n    x = np.concatenate(seg)\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef vocal(seed=0, base=180.0, dur=0.5, amp=1.0, kind=\"shout\"):\n    \"\"\"Formant-ish synthetic vocalisation for enemy barks.\"\"\"\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    t = np.arange(n) / SR\n    if kind == \"shout\":\n        f0 = base * (1.0 + 0.18 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 5.5 * t))\n        src = np.sign(np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(f0) / SR)) * 0.6\n        env = env_exp(n, 0.012, dur * 0.42)\n    else:  # grunt / pain\n        f0 = base * np.exp(-t / 0.22)\n        src = np.sign(np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(f0) / SR)) * 0.6\n        env = env_exp(n, 0.004, dur * 0.3)\n    x = np.zeros(n)\n    for f, g, q in ((520, 1.0, 90), (1180, 0.6, 120), (2650, 0.35, 160)):\n        x += reson(src, f, q / SR * 0.02 + 0.002) * g\n    x += src * 0.25\n    x = lp(x, 4200)\n    x = reverb(x, 0.45, 0.22, 3000, seed)\n    return norm(x * env, amp)\n\n\ndef door_sfx(seed=0, amp=1.0, close=False):\n    n = int(1.1 * SR)\n    x = bp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.004, 0.35), 220, 2400)\n    for i, g in enumerate((0.6, 0.4, 0.25)):\n        off = int((0.06 + i * 0.09) * SR)\n        x[off : off + n // 3] += reson(nz(n // 3, seed + i + 1), 340.0 + 90 * i, 0.02) * g\n    if close:\n        x = x[::-1] * 0.9\n    x = reverb(x, 0.7, 0.3, 3000, seed + 5)\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef locker_sfx(seed=0, amp=1.0, close=False):\n    n = int(0.7 * SR)\n    x = bp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.001, 0.09), 500, 6000)\n    x += reson(nz(n, seed + 1), 780.0, 0.09) * 0.7\n    x += reson(nz(n, seed + 2), 1620.0, 0.05) * 0.4\n    thud = np.sin(2 * np.pi * 88.0 * np.arange(n) / SR) * env_exp(n, 0.002, 0.06)\n    x += thud * (0.8 if close else 0.3)\n    x = reverb(x, 0.5, 0.28, 4200, seed + 3)\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef explosion(seed=0, amp=1.0, dur=2.2):\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    t = np.arange(n) / SR\n    boom = lp(nz(n, seed) * env_exp(n, 0.003, 0.5), 320, order=2)\n    crack = hp(nz(n, seed + 1) * env_exp(n, 0.0004, 0.02), 1800)\n    rumble = np.sin(2 * np.pi * 42.0 * t) * env_exp(n, 0.006, 0.7)\n    x = boom * 1.4 + crack * 0.8 + rumble * 0.9\n    x = reverb(x, 1.8, 0.4, 2200, seed + 2)\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef whoosh(seed=0, amp=1.0, dur=1.0):\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    t = np.arange(n) / SR\n    x = bp(nz(n, seed), 200, 3000)\n    env = np.sin(np.pi * np.clip(t / dur, 0, 1)) ** 1.5\n    x = lp(x * env, 2200)\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef heartbeat(seed=0, amp=1.0):\n    n = int(0.9 * SR)\n    x = np.zeros(n)\n    for off, g in ((0.0, 1.0), (0.30, 0.72)):\n        i = int(off * SR)\n        seg = np.sin(2 * np.pi * 58.0 * np.arange(n - i) / SR) * env_exp(n - i, 0.004, 0.09)\n        x[i:] += seg * g\n    return norm(lp(x, 260, order=2), amp)\n\n\ndef metal_stinger(seed=0, amp=1.0, dur=2.4):\n    n = int(dur * SR)\n    x = np.zeros(n)\n    for f, d, g in ((220, 1.4, 1.0), (330, 1.1, 0.6), (466, 0.9, 0.45), (622, 0.7, 0.3)):\n        x += reson(nz(n, seed), f, d) * g\n    x = reverb(x, 2.2, 0.45, 4000, seed + 3)\n    return norm(x, amp)\n\n\ndef fade_in_out(x, ms=20):\n    return fade_edges(x, ms)\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Composition\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef compose_menu():\n    \"\"\"Slow, awe-struck.  The monolith at dusk.\"\"\"\n    tr = Track(66, 16, tail=6.0, seed=2)\n    prog = [\n        ([\"D3\", \"F3\", \"A3\"], \"D2\"),\n        ([\"Bb2\", \"D3\", \"F3\"], \"Bb1\"),\n        ([\"F2\", \"A2\", \"C3\"], \"F1\"),\n        ([\"C3\", \"E3\", \"G3\"], \"C2\"),\n    ]\n    mel = [None, \"A4\", None, None, \"F4\", None, None, \"D4\",\n           None, \"C5\", None, None, \"A4\", None, None, None,\n           None, \"D5\", None, \"C5\", None, None, \"A4\", None,\n           None, \"G4\", None, None, \"E4\", None, None, None]\n    for bar in range(16):\n        chord, root = prog[(bar // 4) % 4]\n        if bar % 4 == 0:\n            tr.add(pad_chord(chord, tr.bar * 4.05, cutoff=1150, detune=0.007, seed=bar), tr.t(bar), 0.30)\n            tr.add(sub_note(pf(root), tr.bar * 4.05, amp=0.42, attack=0.6, release=1.2), tr.t(bar), 1.0)\n        # airy noise swell every 4 bars\n        if bar % 4 == 2:\n            n = int(tr.bar * 2 * SR)\n            swell = lp(nz(n, bar), 1400, order=2) * np.sin(np.pi * np.arange(n) / n) ** 2\n            tr.add(norm(swell, 0.3), tr.t(bar), 0.5, pan=0.4)\n        tok = mel[(bar * 2) % len(mel)] if bar < 16 else None\n        if tok:\n            tr.add(bell(pf(tok), 3.4, amp=0.30, index=1.5, decay=2.6), tr.t(bar, 0.0), 1.0, pan=-0.25)\n        tok2 = mel[(bar * 2 + 8) % len(mel)]\n        if tok2 and bar % 2 == 1:\n            tr.add(bell(pf(tok2), 2.8, amp=0.16, index=2.2, decay=1.9), tr.t(bar, 2.5), 1.0, pan=0.35)\n    return tr.finish(rev_decay=3.4, rev_mix=0.42, rev_damp=4200, peak=0.86)\n\n\ndef compose_explore():\n    \"\"\"Tense, patient, forward motion.  The drowned district.\"\"\"\n    tr = Track(74, 16, tail=5.0, seed=3)\n    prog = [\n        ([\"D3\", \"F3\", \"A3\"], \"D2\", \"D\"),\n        ([\"Bb2\", \"D3\", \"F3\"], \"Bb1\", \"Bb\"),\n        ([\"F2\", \"A2\", \"C3\"], \"F1\", \"F\"),\n        ([\"C3\", \"E3\", \"G3\"], \"C2\", \"C\"),\n    ]\n    for bar in range(16):\n        chord, root, _ = prog[(bar // 4) % 4]\n        if bar % 4 == 0:\n            tr.add(pad_chord(chord, tr.bar * 4.05, cutoff=950, detune=0.006, seed=bar + 40), tr.t(bar), 0.26)\n            tr.add(sub_note(pf(root), tr.bar * 4.05, amp=0.38, attack=0.35, release=1.0), tr.t(bar))\n        # pulsing low ostinato on the root\n        for beat in (0.0, 1.5, 2.5, 3.0):\n            tr.add(sub_note(pf(root), tr.spb * 0.9, amp=0.20, attack=0.006, release=0.18),\n                   tr.t(bar, beat), 1.0, pan=-0.1)\n        # frame-drum / taiko hits\n        if bar % 4 in (0, 2):\n            tr.add(kick(0.5, 120.0, 46.0) * 0.8, tr.t(bar, 0.0), 0.55)\n        if bar % 8 == 3:\n            tr.add(kick(0.5, 120.0, 46.0) * 0.8, tr.t(bar, 2.0), 0.5)\n        if bar % 4 == 3:\n            tr.add(snare(0.35, 0.6, seed=bar), tr.t(bar, 3.5), 0.35, pan=0.2)\n        # distant metal clangs as texture\n        if bar % 4 == 1:\n            tr.add(reson(nz(int(1.6 * SR), bar), 820.0, 0.5) * 0.25, tr.t(bar, 1.5), 0.4, pan=0.55)\n    return tr.finish(rev_decay=2.8, rev_mix=0.36, peak=0.88)\n\n\ndef compose_combat():\n    \"\"\"Driving, aggressive.  Suppressing fire in the flooded market.\"\"\"\n    bpm = 138\n    tr = Track(bpm, 16, tail=3.5, seed=4)\n    prog = [\n        ([\"A2\", \"C3\", \"E3\"], \"A1\"),\n        ([\"F2\", \"A2\", \"C3\"], \"F1\"),\n        ([\"C3\", \"E3\", \"G3\"], \"C2\"),\n        ([\"E2\", \"G#2\", \"B2\"], \"E1\"),\n    ]\n    for bar in range(16):\n        chord, root = prog[(bar // 4) % 4]\n        if bar % 4 == 0:\n            tr.add(pad_chord(chord, tr.bar * 4.05, cutoff=1400, detune=0.01, seed=bar + 80, bright=0.5),\n                   tr.t(bar), 0.24)\n        # 8th-note driving bass\n        for i in range(8):\n            g = 0.42 if i % 2 == 0 else 0.28\n            tr.add(sub_note(pf(root), tr.spb * 0.45, amp=g, attack=0.004, release=0.1),\n                   tr.t(bar, i * 0.5), 1.0)\n        # drums\n        for beat in (0.0, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):\n            tr.add(kick(0.36, 160.0, 46.0), tr.t(bar, beat), 0.85)\n        tr.add(kick(0.36, 160.0, 46.0) * 0.7, tr.t(bar, 2.75), 0.6)\n        for beat in (1.0, 3.0):\n            tr.add(snare(0.3, 0.95, seed=bar * 3 + int(beat)), tr.t(bar, beat), 0.7)\n        for i in range(8):\n            tr.add(hat(0.05, 0.35 if i % 2 else 0.5, seed=bar * 7 + i), tr.t(bar, i * 0.5 + 0.25), 1.0,\n                   pan=0.3 if i % 2 else -0.3)\n        tr.add(hat(0.13, 0.3, seed=bar + 500, open_=True), tr.t(bar, 3.75), 1.0)\n        # brass-ish stabs\n        if bar % 2 == 1:\n            stab = pad_chord([chord[0], chord[1]], tr.spb * 0.4, cutoff=2600, detune=0.014,\n                             seed=bar + 200, bright=0.8)\n            tr.add(soft_clip(stab, 2.0), tr.t(bar, 2.5), 0.5, pan=0.15)\n    return tr.finish(rev_decay=1.6, rev_mix=0.22, rev_damp=5200, drive=1.5, peak=0.92)\n\n\ndef compose_stealth():\n    \"\"\"Sparse, anxious.  Boots on wet tile in the dark.\"\"\"\n    tr = Track(58, 16, tail=6.0, seed=5)\n    prog = [([\"A2\", \"C3\", \"E3\"], \"A1\"), ([\"G#2\", \"B2\", \"E3\"], \"G#1\")]\n    for bar in range(16):\n        chord, root = prog[(bar // 4) % 2]\n        if bar % 4 == 0:\n            tr.add(pad_chord(chord, tr.bar * 4.05, cutoff=700, detune=0.004, seed=bar + 300), tr.t(bar), 0.22)\n            tr.add(sub_note(pf(root), tr.bar * 2.0, amp=0.34, attack=1.2, release=1.5), tr.t(bar))\n        # slow heartbeat-like pulse\n        tr.add(heartbeat(seed=bar) * 0.35, tr.t(bar, 0.0 if bar % 2 == 0 else 2.0), 0.5)\n        # high tension drone\n        n = int(tr.bar * SR)\n        drone = np.sin(2 * np.pi * pf(\"E6\") * np.arange(n) / SR) * 0.5\n        drone += np.sin(2 * np.pi * pf(\"B5\") * np.arange(n) / SR) * 0.4\n        trem = 0.35 + 0.65 * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 6.5 * np.arange(n) / SR))\n        tr.add(lp(drone * trem, 3000) * 0.10, tr.t(bar), 0.5, pan=0.5)\n        # metallic pings\n        if bar % 8 == 5:\n            tr.add(reson(nz(int(2.4 * SR), bar + 11), 1180.0, 1.2) * 0.3, tr.t(bar, 0.25), 0.4, pan=-0.6)\n        if bar % 4 == 2:\n            tr.add(whoosh(seed=bar, dur=1.6), tr.t(bar), 0.18, pan=0.0)\n    return tr.finish(rev_decay=4.0, rev_mix=0.46, rev_damp=3600, peak=0.82)\n\n\ndef compose_meadow():\n    \"\"\"Warm and hopeful.  Sunlight after the ash.\"\"\"\n    tr = Track(78, 16, tail=5.0, seed=6)\n    prog = [\n        ([\"F3\", \"A3\", \"C4\"], \"F2\"),\n        ([\"C3\", \"E3\", \"G3\"], \"C2\"),\n        ([\"G2\", \"B2\", \"D3\"], \"G1\"),\n        ([\"A2\", \"C3\", \"E3\"], \"A1\"),\n    ]\n    mel = [\"A4\", \"C5\", \"F4\", \"G4\", \"A4\", \"F4\", \"C5\", \"A4\",\n           \"G4\", \"E4\", \"G4\", \"C5\", \"D5\", \"C5\", \"A4\", \"G4\",\n           \"F4\", \"A4\", \"C5\", \"D5\", \"E5\", \"D5\", \"C5\", \"A4\",\n           \"G4\", \"A4\", \"C5\", \"G4\", \"F4\", \"C5\", \"A4\", None]\n    for bar in range(16):\n        chord, root = prog[(bar // 4) % 4]\n        if bar % 4 == 0:\n            tr.add(pad_chord(chord, tr.bar * 4.05, cutoff=2000, detune=0.009, seed=bar + 400, bright=0.6),\n                   tr.t(bar), 0.26)\n            tr.add(sub_note(pf(root), tr.bar * 4.05, amp=0.30, attack=0.5, release=2.0), tr.t(bar))\n        # gentle arpeggio\n        for i in range(4):\n            tok = chord[i % len(chord)]\n            tr.add(bell(pf(tok) * 2, 1.6, amp=0.10, index=1.2, decay=1.2), tr.t(bar, i * 1.0), 1.0,\n                   pan=-0.4 + 0.3 * i)\n        tok = mel[(bar * 2) % len(mel)]\n        if tok:\n            tr.add(bell(pf(tok), 3.0, amp=0.24, index=1.4, decay=2.2), tr.t(bar, 0.0), 1.0, pan=0.2)\n        tok2 = mel[(bar * 2 + 1) % len(mel)]\n        if tok2:\n            tr.add(bell(pf(tok2), 2.0, amp=0.12, index=2.0, decay=1.4), tr.t(bar, 2.0), 1.0, pan=-0.2)\n        # soft shaker and light kick for pulse\n        for i in range(8):\n            tr.add(tamb(0.1, 0.16, seed=bar * 13 + i), tr.t(bar, i * 0.5 + 0.25), 1.0,\n                   pan=0.45 if i % 2 else -0.45)\n        if bar % 4 in (0, 2):\n            tr.add(kick(0.3, 110.0, 48.0) * 0.5, tr.t(bar, 0.0), 0.4)\n    return tr.finish(rev_decay=3.6, rev_mix=0.4, rev_damp=5000, peak=0.88)\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Render manifest\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef build_manifest():\n    \"\"\"Return {filename: (signal_builder, loop)}.\"\"\"\n    m = {}\n\n    # --- UI ---------------------------------------------------------------\n    m[\"ui_hover\"] = (lambda: ui_blip(1320.0, 0.05, 0.22, \"sine\"), False)\n    m[\"ui_click\"] = (lambda: ui_blip(760.0, 0.07, 0.5, \"square\"), False)\n    m[\"ui_confirm\"] = (lambda: ui_sequence([660.0, 990.0], 0.07, 0.45), False)\n    m[\"ui_back\"] = (lambda: ui_sequence([520.0, 340.0], 0.08, 0.45), False)\n    m[\"ui_deny\"] = (lambda: ui_sequence([300.0, 220.0], 0.09, 0.5, \"square\"), False)\n    m[\"ui_tab\"] = (lambda: ui_blip(1050.0, 0.06, 0.3, \"sine\"), False)\n    m[\"ui_slider\"] = (lambda: ui_blip(1500.0, 0.03, 0.25, \"sine\"), False)\n\n    # --- combat -----------------------------------------------------------\n    for i in range(3):\n        m[\"rifle_fire_%d\" % (i + 1)] = (lambda i=i: gunshot(100 + i, 0.95, 0.13, 0.5), False)\n    m[\"rifle_dry\"] = (lambda: mechanical_click(7, 2000, 9000, 0.05, 900.0, 0.85), False)\n    m[\"mag_out\"] = (lambda: mechanical_click(11, 700, 5000, 0.12, 240.0, 0.9), False)\n    m[\"mag_in\"] = (lambda: mechanical_click(13, 500, 4200, 0.14, 190.0, 1.0), False)\n    m[\"bolt\"] = (lambda: mechanical_click(17, 1500, 9000, 0.1, 420.0, 0.95), False)\n    m[\"shell_drop\"] = (lambda: np.concatenate([\n        mechanical_click(19, 3000, 12000, 0.09, 2400.0, 0.5),\n        np.zeros(int(0.14 * SR)),\n        mechanical_click(23, 3500, 13000, 0.07, 2900.0, 0.32),\n    ]), False)\n    m[\"hit_marker\"] = (lambda: ui_blip(2100.0, 0.045, 0.4), False)\n    m[\"hit_crit\"] = (lambda: ui_sequence([2600.0, 3400.0], 0.04, 0.45), False)\n\n    # --- impacts ----------------------------------------------------------\n    for i in range(3):\n        m[\"impact_concrete_%d\" % (i + 1)] = (lambda i=i: impact_concrete(200 + i, 0.85), False)\n    for i in range(2):\n        m[\"impact_metal_%d\" % (i + 1)] = (lambda i=i: impact_metal(210 + i, 0.85), False)\n    m[\"impact_flesh\"] = (lambda: impact_flesh(220, 0.9), False)\n    m[\"impact_water\"] = (lambda: impact_water(230, 0.8), False)\n    m[\"impact_ricochet\"] = (lambda: np.concatenate([\n        reson(nz(int(0.5 * SR), 240), 3400.0, 0.25),\n        np.zeros(int(0.05 * SR)),\n        reson(nz(int(0.3 * SR), 241), 2100.0, 0.15) * 0.4,\n    ]), False)\n\n    # --- movement ---------------------------------------------------------\n    for kind, seed0 in ((\"concrete\", 300), (\"gravel\", 320), (\"water\", 340), (\"grass\", 360), (\"metal\", 380)):\n        for i in range(4):\n            m[\"step_%s_%d\" % (kind, i + 1)] = (lambda k=kind, s=seed0 + i: footstep(s, k, 0.7), False)\n    m[\"jump_grunt\"] = (lambda: vocal(400, 150.0, 0.35, 0.5, \"grunt\"), False)\n    m[\"land\"] = (lambda: footstep(402, \"concrete\", 1.0) * 1.2, False)\n    m[\"gear\"] = (lambda: bp(nz(int(0.35 * SR), 404) * env_exp(int(0.35 * SR), 0.003, 0.09), 800, 7000, 2) * 0.4, False)\n\n    # --- player state -----------------------------------------------------\n    m[\"player_hurt\"] = (lambda: vocal(410, 170.0, 0.4, 0.85, \"grunt\"), False)\n    m[\"player_death\"] = (lambda: vocal(412, 150.0, 1.4, 1.0, \"grunt\"), False)\n    m[\"heartbeat\"] = (lambda: heartbeat(414, 0.9), False)\n    m[\"heal\"] = (lambda: ui_sequence([660.0, 880.0, 1320.0], 0.09, 0.35), False)\n\n    # --- enemy ------------------------------------------------------------\n    m[\"enemy_alert\"] = (lambda: vocal(500, 190.0, 0.55, 1.0, \"shout\"), False)\n    m[\"enemy_shout\"] = (lambda: vocal(502, 205.0, 0.7, 0.9, \"shout\"), False)\n    m[\"enemy_pain\"] = (lambda: vocal(504, 175.0, 0.33, 0.9, \"grunt\"), False)\n    m[\"enemy_death\"] = (lambda: vocal(506, 160.0, 0.95, 1.0, \"grunt\"), False)\n    m[\"enemy_spot\"] = (lambda: ui_sequence([880.0, 1180.0], 0.05, 0.3), False)\n\n    # --- world ------------------------------------------------------------\n    m[\"door_open\"] = (lambda: door_sfx(600, 0.8, False), False)\n    m[\"door_close\"] = (lambda: door_sfx(602, 0.8, True), False)\n    m[\"locker_open\"] = (lambda: locker_sfx(610, 0.85, False), False)\n    m[\"locker_close\"] = (lambda: locker_sfx(612, 0.85, True), False)\n    m[\"switch\"] = (lambda: mechanical_click(620, 1400, 6000, 0.09, 520.0, 0.8), False)\n    m[\"pickup\"] = (lambda: ui_sequence([780.0, 1040.0, 1560.0], 0.06, 0.35), False)\n    m[\"objective\"] = (lambda: ui_sequence([660.0, 990.0, 1320.0], 0.13, 0.4), False)\n    m[\"checkpoint\"] = (lambda: np.concatenate([\n        ui_sequence([520.0, 780.0], 0.1, 0.35),\n        np.zeros(int(0.08 * SR)),\n        norm(bell(pf(\"D5\"), 2.0, 0.3, 1.4, 2.0), 0.35),\n    ]), False)\n    m[\"chapter\"] = (lambda: norm(np.concatenate([\n        np.zeros(int(0.3 * SR)),\n        metal_stinger(700, 0.8, 3.0),\n    ]), 0.8), False)\n    m[\"explosion\"] = (lambda: explosion(720, 0.95), False)\n    m[\"whoosh\"] = (lambda: whoosh(730, 0.6, 1.2), False)\n    m[\"flesh_fall\"] = (lambda: lp(nz(int(0.4 * SR), 740) * env_exp(int(0.4 * SR), 0.004, 0.09), 700, 2) * 0.7, False)\n\n    # --- ambience loops ---------------------------------------------------\n    m[\"amb_rain\"] = (lambda: fade_in_out(rain_layer(14.0, 800, 0.8, 1.0), 60.0), True)\n    m[\"amb_rain_light\"] = (lambda: fade_in_out(rain_layer(14.0, 802, 0.45, 0.45), 60.0), True)\n    m[\"amb_wind\"] = (lambda: fade_in_out(ambience_noise(16.0, 810, 120, 2600, 1800, 0.5), 60.0), True)\n    m[\"amb_city\"] = (lambda: fade_in_out(ambience_noise(18.0, 820, 60, 900, 700, 0.6), 60.0), True)\n    m[\"amb_water\"] = (lambda: fade_in_out(water_lap(830, 0.5, 18.0), 60.0), True)\n    m[\"amb_interior\"] = (lambda: fade_in_out(ambience_noise(16.0, 840, 40, 500, 400, 0.4) * 0.7, 60.0), True)\n    m[\"amb_meadow\"] = (lambda: fade_in_out(ambience_noise(18.0, 850, 200, 5000, 4000, 0.35, brown=False), 60.0), True)\n    m[\"amb_embers\"] = (lambda: fade_in_out(bp(nz(int(12 * SR), 860), 300, 1800) * 0.35, 60.0), True)\n\n    # --- music ------------------------------------------------------------\n    m[\"mus_menu\"] = (compose_menu, True)\n    m[\"mus_explore\"] = (compose_explore, True)\n    m[\"mus_combat\"] = (compose_combat, True)\n    m[\"mus_stealth\"] = (compose_stealth, True)\n    m[\"mus_meadow\"] = (compose_meadow, True)\n\n    return m\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# IO\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef save_wav(path, data):\n    import wave\n\n    st = np.asarray(data, dtype=np.float64)\n    if st.ndim == 1:\n        st = np.stack([st, st], axis=1)\n    st = np.clip(st, -1.0, 1.0)\n    pcm = (st * 32767.0).astype(\"<i2\")\n    with wave.open(path, \"wb\") as w:\n        w.setnchannels(2)\n        w.setsampwidth(2)\n        w.setframerate(SR)\n        w.writeframes(pcm.tobytes())\n\n\ndef find_encoder():\n    for enc, args in (\n        (\"libvorbis\", [\"-c:a\", \"libvorbis\", \"-q:a\", \"5\"]),\n        (\"libopus\", [\"-c:a\", \"libopus\", \"-b:a\", \"128k\"]),\n        (\"vorbis\", [\"-c:a\", \"vorbis\", \"-strict\", \"-2\", \"-q:a\", \"5\"]),\n    ):\n        try:\n            r = subprocess.run([\"ffmpeg\", \"-hide_banner\", \"-h\", \"encoder=%s\" % enc],\n                               capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)\n            if r.returncode == 0:\n                return enc, args\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n    return None, None\n\n\ndef main():\n    only = set(sys.argv[1:])\n    os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    os.makedirs(RAW_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    enc, enc_args = find_encoder()\n    if enc is None:\n        print(\"!! no ogg encoder found; leaving WAV files in place\")\n    else:\n        print(\"encoder: %s\" % enc)\n\n    manifest = build_manifest()\n    names = sorted(manifest)\n    if only:\n        names = [n for n in names if n in only]\n\n    total = 0\n    for i, name in enumerate(names, 1):\n        builder, loop = manifest[name]\n        try:\n            data = builder()\n        except Exception as e:  # keep going; report at the end\n            print(\"  [%3d/%3d] %-22s FAILED: %s\" % (i, len(names), name, e))\n            continue\n        wav = os.path.join(RAW_DIR, name + \".wav\")\n        save_wav(wav, data)\n        dur = len(np.asarray(data)) / float(SR)\n        out = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, name + \".ogg\")\n        if enc is not None:\n            r = subprocess.run([\"ffmpeg\", \"-y\", \"-hide_banner\", \"-loglevel\", \"error\",\n                                \"-i\", wav] + enc_args + [\"-ar\", \"44100\", \"-ac\", \"2\", out],\n                               capture_output=True, text=True)\n            if r.returncode != 0:\n                print(\"  [%3d/%3d] %-22s ffmpeg error: %s\" % (i, len(names), name, r.stderr.strip()[:120]))\n                shutil.copy(wav, os.path.join(OUT_DIR, name + \".wav\"))\n                out = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, name + \".wav\")\n        else:\n            shutil.copy(wav, os.path.join(OUT_DIR, name + \".wav\"))\n            out = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, name + \".wav\")\n        sz = os.path.getsize(out)\n        total += sz\n        print(\"  [%3d/%3d] %-22s %5.1fs  loop=%-5s %6.0f KB\"\n              % (i, len(names), name, dur, str(loop), sz / 1024.0))\n\n    if os.path.isdir(RAW_DIR):\n        shutil.rmtree(RAW_DIR, ignore_errors=True)\n    print(\"\\n%d files, %.1f MB total -> %s\" % (len(names), total / 1048576.0, OUT_DIR))\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

> _Reasoning_
> No libvorbis, but `vorbis` (experimental, needs -strict -2) and `libopus` are available. Godot 4 supports:
> - AudioStreamOggVorbis (.ogg)
> - AudioStreamMP3 (.mp3)
> - AudioStreamWAV (.wav)
> 
> Godot does NOT support .opus files as a stream format natively (no AudioStreamOpus). So options: `vorbis` (experimental encoder, works with -strict -2) or MP3 (libmp3lame) or WAV.
> 
> Godot supports .ogg (Vorbis) and .mp3. Let's use the built-in experimental `vorbis` encoder with `-strict -2`. Let me test it. If quality/gapless issues arise, fall back to MP3 (libmp3lame has a gapless problem — MP3 loops may have a gap, bad for music loops). Vorbis is preferred for seamless looping.
> 
> Let me test the experimental vorbis encoder.
> 
> Fix find_encoder: probe by actually encoding a tiny sample rather than `-h`. Let me rewrite find_encoder to do a real test encode.

> ### AudioDirector
> Autoload Node. Creates buses: Master(existing), Music, SFX, Ambience, UI, Voice.
> Loads all streams lazily from `res://assets/audio/<name>.ogg` via `load()`. Since Godot imports them, path is res://assets/audio/name.ogg (the importer keeps the .ogg extension in the res path? Actually Godot 4 keeps the original file extension for imported resources; `load("res://assets/audio/rifle_fire_1.ogg")` works and returns AudioStreamOggVorbis). Yes.
> 
> API:
> - `play(name, opts)` — 2D/global one-shot with pitch variation, bus routing.
> - `play_at(name, pos3d)` — spatialized one-shot with an AudioStreamPlayer3D pooled at a position.
> - `music(track)` — crossfade to loop.
> - `ambience(track, fade)`.
> - `stop_music(fade)`.
> - Pool of AudioStreamPlayer (non-positional) and AudioStreamPlayer3D.
> 
> Pitch variation for footsteps/gunshots for realism.
> 
> Implementation notes: 
> - Keep a dictionary `_streams` cache.
> - Pool: create N=24 AudioStreamPlayer2D? For non-positional, AudioStreamPlayer. For positional, AudioStreamPlayer3D with `unit_size`, `max_distance`, `attenuation_model`.
> - `play_at(name, pos, volume_db, pitch_jitter)` → find free player, set `global_position = pos`, play.
> - 3D players need to be in the scene tree under some node; add them to the AudioDirector itself (a Node). AudioStreamPlayer3D must be in a 3D context — being a child of a plain Node at the root works? AudioStreamPlayer3D requires being inside a Viewport/World3D. Autoload is a child of root (the Window/Viewport) → it gets the default World3D. Should work since the root viewport's world_3d is the main one. Yes, that works in Godot 4 (autoloads are children of root viewport). Good.
> 
> But careful: when switching scenes, will the 3D players' positions be interpreted in the current world? Yes, root viewport has one World3D used by the main scene.
> 
> - Master volume: `AudioServer.set_bus_volume_db(idx, linear_to_db(v))`.
> - Loop: set `stream.loop = true` for looping streams when creating/loading.

{
  "path": "scripts/core/Settings.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the settings autoload with graphics/audio/display options and persistence.",
  "content": "extends Node\n## Player-facing options: graphics, audio, display, controls.\n##\n## Persisted to `user://settings.cfg` as JSON.  Anything that affects the\n## renderer is pushed out through the `changed` signal so live scenes can\n## re-apply quality to their own WorldEnvironment.\n\nsignal changed\nsignal display_changed\n\nconst PATH := \"user://settings.cfg\"\n\nconst DEFAULTS := {\n\t# audio\n\t\"master_volume\": 0.85,\n\t\"music_volume\": 0.62,\n\t\"sfx_volume\": 0.95,\n\t\"ambience_volume\": 0.55,\n\t\"voice_volume\": 0.9,\n\t# controls\n\t\"mouse_sensitivity\": 0.0021,\n\t\"invert_y\": false,\n\t\"gamepad_sensitivity\": 2.4,\n\t\"fov\": 78.0,\n\t\"camera_shake\": 1.0,\n\t# graphics\n\t\"preset\": 2,\n\t\"shadows\": true,\n\t\"ssao\": true,\n\t\"ssr\": true,\n\t\"ssil\": true,\n\t\"glow\": true,\n\t\"volumetric_fog\": true,\n\t\"render_scale\": 1.0,\n\t\"msaa\": 1,\n\t\"film_grain\": true,\n\t\"motion_blur\": true,\n\t# display\n\t\"fullscreen\": false,\n\t\"max_fps\": 0,\n\t\"vsync\": true,\n\t\"subtitles\": true,\n\t\"show_fps\": false,\n}\n\nconst PRESETS := [\n\t# Low\n\t{\"shadows\": false, \"ssao\": false, \"ssr\": false, \"ssil\": false, \"glow\": true,\n\t \"volumetric_fog\": false, \"render_scale\": 0.75, \"msaa\": 0, \"film_grain\": false, \"motion_blur\": false},\n\t# Medium\n\t{\"shadows\": true, \"ssao\": false, \"ssr\": false, \"ssil\": false, \"glow\": true,\n\t \"volumetric_fog\": false, \"render_scale\": 1.0, \"msaa\": 0, \"film_grain\": true, \"motion_blur\": false},\n\t# High\n\t{\"shadows\": true, \"ssao\": true, \"ssr\": true, \"ssil\": false, \"glow\": true,\n\t \"volumetric_fog\": true, \"render_scale\": 1.0, \"msaa\": 1, \"film_grain\": true, \"motion_blur\": true},\n\t# Ultra\n\t{\"shadows\": true, \"ssao\": true, \"ssr\": true, \"ssil\": true, \"glow\": true,\n\t \"volumetric_fog\": true, \"render_scale\": 1.0, \"msaa\": 2, \"film_grain\": true, \"motion_blur\": false},\n]\n\nconst PRESET_NAMES := [\"LOW\", \"MEDIUM\", \"HIGH\", \"ULTRA\"]\n\nvar data: Dictionary = {}\n\n\nfunc _ready() -> void:\n\tdata = DEFAULTS.duplicate(true)\n\t_load()\n\tapply_audio()\n\tapply_display()\n\tapply_viewport()\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- accessors\n\nfunc getv(key: String):\n\treturn data.get(key, DEFAULTS.get(key))\n\n\nfunc setv(key: String, value) -> void:\n\tif data.get(key) == value:\n\t\treturn\n\tdata[key] = value\n\tchanged.emit()\n\n\nfunc set_preset(index: int) -> void:\n\tindex = clampi(index, 0, PRESETS.size() - 1)\n\tdata[\"preset\"] = index\n\tfor k in PRESETS[index]:\n\t\tdata[k] = PRESETS[index][k]\n\tchanged.emit()\n\n\nfunc preset_name() -> String:\n\treturn PRESET_NAMES[clampi(int(getv(\"preset\")), 0, PRESETS.size() - 1)]\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ saving\n\nfunc save_settings() -> void:\n\tvar f := FileAccess.open(PATH, FileAccess.WRITE)\n\tif f == null:\n\t\tpush_warning(\"could not write settings\")\n\t\treturn\n\tf.store_string(JSON.stringify(data, \"\\t\"))\n\n\nfunc _load() -> void:\n\tif not FileAccess.file_exists(PATH):\n\t\treturn\n\tvar f := FileAccess.open(PATH, FileAccess.READ)\n\tif f == null:\n\t\treturn\n\tvar parsed = JSON.parse_string(f.get_as_text())\n\tif typeof(parsed) != TYPE_DICTIONARY:\n\t\treturn\n\tfor k in parsed:\n\t\tif DEFAULTS.has(k):\n\t\t\tdata[k] = parsed[k]\n\n\nfunc reset_to_defaults() -> void:\n\tdata = DEFAULTS.duplicate(true)\n\tapply_audio()\n\tapply_display()\n\tapply_viewport()\n\tchanged.emit()\n\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------- applying\n\nfunc apply_audio() -> void:\n\tfor bus_name in [\"Master\", \"Music\", \"SFX\", \"Ambience\", \"UI\", \"Voice\"]:\n\t\tvar idx := AudioServer.get_bus_index(bus_name)\n\t\tif idx < 0:\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\tvar v := 1.0\n\t\tmatch bus_name:\n\t\t\t\"Master\": v = float(getv(\"master_volume\"))\n\t\t\t\"Music\": v = float(getv(\"music_volume\"))\n\t\t\t\"SFX\": v = float(getv(\"sfx_volume\"))\n\t\t\t\"Ambience\": v = float(getv(\"ambience_volume\"))\n\t\t\t\"UI\": v = float(getv(\"sfx_volume\"))\n\t\t\t\"Voice\": v = float(getv(\"voice_volume\"))\n\t\tAudioServer.set_bus_mute(idx, v <= 0.001)\n\t\tAudioServer.set_bus_volume_db(idx, linear_to_db(maxf(v, 0.0001)))\n\n\nfunc apply_display() -> void:\n\tvar mode := DisplayServer.WINDOW_MODE_FULLSCREEN if bool(getv(\"fullscreen\")) \\\n\t\t\telse DisplayServer.WINDOW_MODE_WINDOWED\n\tDisplayServer.window_set_mode(mode)\n\tEngine.max_fps = int(getv(\"max_fps\"))\n\tDisplayServer.window_set_vsync_mode(\n\t\tDisplayServer.VSYNC_ENABLED if bool(getv(\"vsync\")) else DisplayServer.VSYNC_DISABLED)\n\tdisplay_changed.emit()\n\n\nfunc apply_viewport() -> void:\n\tvar vp := get_viewport()\n\tif vp == null:\n\t\treturn\n\tvp.scaling_3d_scale = float(getv(\"render_scale\"))\n\tvar msaa := int(getv(\"msaa\"))\n\tvp.msaa_3d = [Viewport.MSAA_DISABLED, Viewport.MSAA_2X, Viewport.MSAA_4X, Viewport.MSAA_8X][clampi(msaa, 0, 3)]\n\tvp.screen_space_aa = Viewport.SCREEN_SPACE_AA_FXAA if msaa == 0 else Viewport.SCREEN_SPACE_AA_DISABLED\n\tvp.use_debanding = true\n\n\n## Push the quality flags into a scene's Environment.  Called by each scene\n## when it builds its WorldEnvironment and again whenever settings change.\nfunc apply_quality(env: Environment) -> void:\n\tif env == null:\n\t\treturn\n\tenv.ssao_enabled = bool(getv(\"ssao\"))\n\tenv.ssao_radius = 1.4\n\tenv.ssao_intensity = 1.8\n\tenv.ssao_power = 1.6\n\tenv.ssao_detail = 0.7\n\tenv.ssil_enabled = bool(getv(\"ssil\"))\n\tenv.ssr_enabled = bool(getv(\"ssr\"))\n\tenv.ssr_max_steps = 48\n\tenv.ssr_fade_in = 0.2\n\tenv.ssr_fade_out = 1.6\n\tenv.glow_enabled = bool(getv(\"glow\"))\n\tenv.volumetric_fog_enabled = bool(getv(\"volumetric_fog\"))\n\tenv.sdfgi_enabled = false\n"
}

```json
{
  "path": "scripts/core/InputSetup.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the input map builder.",
  "content": "class_name InputSetup\n## Builds the whole InputMap in code.\n##\n## Keeping the bindings here rather than in project.godot means the defaults\n## are version-controlled next to the gameplay code that consumes them, and\n## we can bind physical keycodes (so WASD works on AZERTY) plus full gamepad\n## support in one pass.\n\nconst DEADZONE := 0.22\n\n\nstatic func install() -> void:\n\t# --- movement ---------------------------------------------------------\n\tkey(\"move_forward\", [KEY_W], [JOY_BUTTON_DPAD_UP])\n\tkey(\"move_back\", [KEY_S], [JOY_BUTTON_DPAD_DOWN])\n\tkey(\"move_left\", [KEY_A], [JOY_BUTTON_DPAD_LEFT])\n\tkey(\"move_right\", [KEY_D], [JOY_BUTTON_DPAD_RIGHT])\n\taxis(\"move_forward\", JOY_AXIS_LEFT_Y, -1.0)\n\taxis(\"move_back\", JOY_AXIS_LEFT_Y, 1.0)\n\taxis(\"move_left\", JOY_AXIS_LEFT_X, -1.0)\n\taxis(\"move_right\", JOY_AXIS_LEFT_X, 1.0)\n\n\tkey(\"jump\", [KEY_SPACE], [JOY_BUTTON_A])\n\tkey(\"sprint\", [KEY_SHIFT], [JOY_BUTTON_LEFT_STICK])\n\tkey(\"crouch\", [KEY_CTRL, KEY_C], [JOY_BUTTON_B])\n\n\t# --- camera -----------------------------------------------------------\n\taxis(\"look_left\", JOY_AXIS_RIGHT_X, -1.0)\n\taxis(\"look_right\", JOY_AXIS_RIGHT_X, 1.0)\n\taxis(\"look_up\", JOY_AXIS_RIGHT_Y, -1.0)\n\taxis(\"look_down\", JOY_AXIS_RIGHT_Y, 1.0)\n\tkey(\"camera_reset\", [KEY_MIDDLE], [])\n\n\t# --- combat -----------------------------------------------------------\n\tmouse(\"fire\", [MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT])\n\taxis(\"fire\", JOY_AXIS_TRIGGER_RIGHT, 1.0)\n\tmouse(\"aim\", [MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT])\n\taxis(\"aim\", JOY_AXIS_TRIGGER_LEFT, 1.0)\n\tkey(\"reload\", [KEY_R], [JOY_BUTTON_X])\n\tkey(\"melee\", [KEY_V], [JOY_BUTTON_RIGHT_STICK])\n\tkey(\"flashlight\", [KEY_F], [JOY_BUTTON_DPAD_RIGHT])\n\n\t# --- interaction ------------------------------------------------------\n\tkey(\"interact\", [KEY_E], [JOY_BUTTON_Y])\n\tkey(\"radial\", [KEY_TAB, KEY_Q], [])\n\tkey(\"hide\", [KEY_E], [])\n\n\t# --- system -----------------------------------------------------------\n\tvar esc := InputEventKey.new()\n\tesc.physical_keycode = KEY_ESCAPE\n\tesc.keycode = KEY_ESCAPE\n\tadd(\"pause\", [esc, button(JOY_BUTTON_START)])\n\tkey(\"hud_toggle\", [KEY_H], [])\n\tkey(\"fps_toggle\", [KEY_F3], [])\n\tkey(\"screenshot\", [KEY_F2], [])\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- internals\n\nstatic func add(action: String, events: Array) -> void:\n\tif not InputMap.has_action(action):\n\t\tInputMap.add_action(action, DEADZONE)\n\telse:\n\t\tInputMap.action_erase_events(action)\n\tfor e in events:\n\t\tInputMap.action_add_event(action, e)\n\n\nstatic func key(action: String, keys: Array, pads: Array) -> void:\n\tvar events: Array = []\n\tfor k in keys:\n\t\tvar e := InputEventKey.new()\n\t\te.physical_keycode = k\n\t\tevents.append(e)\n\tfor b in pads:\n\t\tevents.append(button(b))\n\tif events.is_empty():\n\t\treturn\n\tif InputMap.has_action(action):\n\t\tfor e in events:\n\t\t\tInputMap.action_add_event(action, e)\n\telse:\n\t\tadd(action, events)\n\n\nstatic func mouse(action: String, buttons: Array) -> void:\n\tvar events: Array = []\n\tfor b in buttons:\n\t\tvar e := InputEventMouseButton.new()\n\t\te.button_index = b\n\t\tevents.append(e)\n\tadd(action, events)\n\n\nstatic func axis(action: String, joy_axis: int, value: float) -> void:\n\tvar e := InputEventJoypadMotion.new()\n\te.axis = joy_axis\n\te.axis_value = value\n\tif not InputMap.has_action(action):\n\t\tInputMap.add_action(action, DEADZONE)\n\tInputMap.action_add_event(action, e)\n\n\nstatic func button(b: int) -> InputEventJoypadButton:\n\tvar e := InputEventJoypadButton.new()\n\te.button_index = b\n\treturn e\n\n\n## Human-readable key hint for the HUD, resolved at runtime.\nstatic func hint(action: String) -> String:\n\tvar names := {\n\t\t\"interact\": \"E\", \"reload\": \"R\", \"jump\": \"SPACE\", \"sprint\": \"SHIFT\",\n\t\t\"crouch\": \"CTRL\", \"aim\": \"RMB\", \"fire\": \"LMB\", \"radial\": \"TAB\",\n\t\t\"flashlight\": \"F\", \"melee\": \"V\", \"pause\": \"ESC\", \"hide\": \"E\",\n\t}\n\treturn names.get(action, action.to_upper())\n"
}

> _Reasoning_
> Wait, there's a bug in my InputSetup: `key()` function checks `if InputMap.has_action(action)` and appends — but for the first call for an action, it calls `add(action, events)` which erases events. That's fine. But subsequent `key()` calls for the same action (e.g., "crouch" is only called once). OK.
> 
> However, there's an issue: `key("move_forward", [KEY_W], [...])` then `axis("move_forward", ...)` — `axis` appends to existing. Good, since `key` already added the action. But ordering: `key` is called first, so the action exists before `axis` appends. Good.
> 
> But `add()` erases events if action exists — `add` is used by `mouse()`. If I call `mouse("fire",...)` then `axis("fire", ...)`, axis appends. Good. But if some other call used `add` for a different action, no conflict.
> 
> Wait — `key("hide", [KEY_E], [])` — duplicate of interact. Harmless but pointless; remove later maybe. Fine.
> 
> Also `InputEventKey` with `physical_keycode = KEY_MIDDLE` in the `key()` function for "camera_reset" — that's wrong: KEY_MIDDLE isn't a keycode; it's a mouse button. Let me fix: camera_reset should be a mouse middle button. Let me change to `mouse("camera_reset", [MOUSE_BUTTON_MIDDLE])`.
> 
> Also `key("sprint", [KEY_SHIFT]...)` — physical_keycode KEY_SHIFT works.
> 
> Let me fix camera_reset.
> 
> Also potential issue: `KEYS`: KEY_CTRL exists in Godot 4 as KEY_CTRL. Yes.
> 
> Let me fix the camera_reset line.
> 
> Now also: `axis()` uses `JOY_AXIS_LEFT_X` etc. Constants: JOY_AXIS_LEFT_X = 0, JOY_AXIS_LEFT_Y = 1, JOY_AXIS_TRIGGER_LEFT = 4, JOY_AXIS_TRIGGER_RIGHT = 5, JOY_AXIS_RIGHT_X = 2, JOY_AXIS_RIGHT_Y = 3. Good.
> 
> Let me fix that one line, then write AudioDirector and Transition and UI helper and GameManager.

{
  "path": "scripts/ui/UI.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the shared UI style helper library.",
  "content": "class_name UI\n## Shared look-and-feel helpers so every screen is styled identically.\n##\n## The whole game uses one typographic system: the engine's default sans,\n## tracked out with a FontVariation, plus a small palette.  Building the theme\n## in code keeps it in one place instead of scattered across .tscn files.\n\nconst TEXT := Color(\"dfe7ee\")\nconst DIM := Color(\"8d9daa\")\nconst FAINT := Color(\"58666f\")\nconst AMBER := Color(\"ffb45a\")\nconst COLD := Color(\"7fd4ff\")\nconst BLOOD := Color(\"ff5b4a\")\nconst GREEN := Color(\"8fd68a\")\nconst INK := Color(\"0a0d11\")\n\nconst SIZE_H1 := 64\nconst SIZE_H2 := 30\nconst SIZE_BODY := 17\nconst SIZE_SMALL := 14\n\n\nstatic func font(spacing := 0.0, embolden := 0.0) -> FontVariation:\n\tvar fv := FontVariation.new()\n\tfv.base_font = ThemeDB.fallback_font\n\tfv.spacing_glyph = int(spacing)\n\tfv.variation_embolden = embolden\n\treturn fv\n\n\nstatic func label(text: String, size := SIZE_BODY, color := TEXT, spacing := 0.0,\n\t\tembolden := 0.0) -> Label:\n\tvar l := Label.new()\n\tl.text = text\n\tl.add_theme_font_override(\"font\", font(spacing, embolden))\n\tl.add_theme_font_size_override(\"font_size\", size)\n\tl.add_theme_color_override(\"font_color\", color)\n\tl.add_theme_color_override(\"font_outline_color\", Color(0, 0, 0, 0.85))\n\tl.add_theme_constant_override(\"outline_size\", 6 if size > 24 else 4)\n\tl.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE\n\treturn l\n\n\nstatic func panel(bg := Color(0.03, 0.04, 0.05, 0.72), radius := 3, border := 0.0,\n\t\tborder_color := Color(1, 1, 1, 0.08)) -> StyleBoxFlat:\n\tvar sb := StyleBoxFlat.new()\n\tsb.bg_color = bg\n\tsb.set_corner_radius_all(radius)\n\tif border > 0.0:\n\t\tsb.set_border_width_all(int(border))\n\t\tsb.border_color = border_color\n\tsb.set_content_margin_all(12)\n\treturn sb\n\n\nstatic func hrule(width := 260, color := Color(1, 1, 1, 0.22)) -> ColorRect:\n\tvar r := ColorRect.new()\n\tr.color = color\n\tr.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(width, 1)\n\tr.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE\n\treturn r\n\n\nstatic func button(text: String, size := 20, accent := AMBER) -> Button:\n\tvar b := Button.new()\n\tb.text = text\n\tb.flat = false\n\tb.focus_mode = Control.FOCUS_ALL\n\tb.add_theme_font_override(\"font\", font(2.0))\n\tb.add_theme_font_size_override(\"font_size\", size)\n\tb.add_theme_color_override(\"font_color\", DIM)\n\tb.add_theme_color_override(\"font_hover_color\", accent)\n\tb.add_theme_color_override(\"font_focus_color\", accent)\n\tb.add_theme_color_override(\"font_pressed_color\", accent)\n\tb.add_theme_color_override(\"font_disabled_color\", FAINT)\n\tb.alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT\n\n\tvar normal := StyleBoxFlat.new()\n\tnormal.bg_color = Color(0, 0, 0, 0)\n\tnormal.content_margin_left = 18\n\tnormal.content_margin_right = 18\n\tnormal.content_margin_top = 8\n\tnormal.content_margin_bottom = 8\n\tvar hover := normal.duplicate() as StyleBoxFlat\n\thover.bg_color = Color(accent.r, accent.g, accent.b, 0.10)\n\thover.border_color = Color(accent.r, accent.g, accent.b, 0.5)\n\thover.set_border_width_all(1)\n\thover.border_width_left = 3\n\tvar pressed := hover.duplicate() as StyleBoxFlat\n\tpressed.bg_color = Color(accent.r, accent.g, accent.b, 0.22)\n\tb.add_theme_stylebox_override(\"normal\", normal)\n\tb.add_theme_stylebox_override(\"hover\", hover)\n\tb.add_theme_stylebox_override(\"focus\", hover)\n\tb.add_theme_stylebox_override(\"pressed\", pressed)\n\tb.add_theme_stylebox_override(\"disabled\", normal)\n\treturn b\n\n\nstatic func slider(minv: float, maxv: float, step: float, value: float) -> HSlider:\n\tvar s := HSlider.new()\n\ts.min_value = minv\n\ts.max_value = maxv\n\ts.step = step\n\ts.value = value\n\ts.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(260, 22)\n\ts.focus_mode = Control.FOCUS_ALL\n\tvar bg := StyleBoxFlat.new()\n\tbg.bg_color = Color(1, 1, 1, 0.10)\n\tbg.set_corner_radius_all(2)\n\tvar grab := StyleBoxFlat.new()\n\tgrab.bg_color = AMBER\n\tgrab.set_corner_radius_all(1)\n\tgrab.content_margin_top = 4\n\tgrab.content_margin_bottom = 4\n\ts.add_theme_stylebox_override(\"slider\", bg)\n\ts.add_theme_stylebox_override(\"grabber_area\", bg)\n\ts.add_theme_stylebox_override(\"grabber_area_highlight\", bg)\n\ts.add_theme_stylebox_override(\"grabber\", grab)\n\treturn s\n\n\nstatic func check(text: String, pressed: bool) -> CheckButton:\n\tvar c := CheckButton.new()\n\tc.text = text\n\tc.button_pressed = pressed\n\tc.add_theme_font_override(\"font\", font(1.0))\n\tc.add_theme_font_size_override(\"font_size\", SIZE_BODY)\n\tc.add_theme_color_override(\"font_color\", DIM)\n\tc.add_theme_color_override(\"font_hover_color\", TEXT)\n\tc.add_theme_color_override(\"font_pressed_color\", AMBER)\n\treturn c\n\n\nstatic func hsep(h := 10) -> Control:\n\tvar c := Control.new()\n\tc.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, h)\n\tc.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE\n\treturn c\n\n\nstatic func title(text: String, size := SIZE_H1, color := TEXT) -> Label:\n\treturn label(text.to_upper(), size, color, size * 0.16, 0.35)\n\n\nstatic func section(text: String) -> VBoxContainer:\n\tvar v := VBoxContainer.new()\n\tv.add_theme_constant_override(\"separation\", 8)\n\tv.add_child(label(text.to_upper(), SIZE_SMALL, AMBER, 3.0, 0.3))\n\tv.add_child(hrule(200, Color(AMBER.r, AMBER.g, AMBER.b, 0.35)))\n\treturn v\n\n\nstatic func format_time(seconds: float) -> String:\n\tvar s := int(maxf(seconds, 0.0))\n\treturn \"%02d:%02d\" % [s / 60, s % 60]\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "path": "scripts/core/Transition.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the transition/fade/title-card autoload.",
  "content": "extends CanvasLayer\n## Global screen furniture: fades, letterboxing and chapter title cards.\n##\n## Every method that animates is awaitable, so scenes read like a screenplay:\n##     await Transition.fade_out(0.8)\n##     Transition.show_title(\"CHAPTER II\", \"THE TAVERN AT KILOMETER NINE\")\n##     await Transition.fade_in(1.4)\n\nconst BAR := 0.11  # letterbox height as a fraction of the screen\n\nvar _fade: ColorRect\nvar _flash: ColorRect\nvar _bar_top: ColorRect\nvar _bar_bottom: ColorRect\nvar _title_box: VBoxContainer\nvar _title_header: Label\nvar _title_rule: ColorRect\nvar _title_sub: Label\n\nvar _bars_on := false\n\n\nfunc _ready() -> void:\n\tlayer = 100\n\tprocess_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS\n\n\t_fade = ColorRect.new()\n\t_fade.color = Color(0, 0, 0, 1)\n\t_fade.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)\n\t_fade.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE\n\tadd_child(_fade)\n\n\t_flash = ColorRect.new()\n\t_flash.color = Color(0, 0, 0, 0)\n\t_flash.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)\n\t_flash.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE\n\tadd_child(_flash)\n\n\t_bar_top = _make_bar(true)\n\t_bar_bottom = _make_bar(false)\n\n\t_title_box = VBoxContainer.new()\n\t_title_box.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_CENTER)\n\t_title_box.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_CENTER\n\t_title_box.add_theme_constant_override(\"separation\", 6)\n\t_title_box.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE\n\t_title_box.modulate = Color(1, 1, 1, 0)\n\tadd_child(_title_box)\n\n\t_title_header = UI.title(\"\", 30, UI.DIM)\n\t_title_header.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER\n\t_title_rule = UI.hrule(180, Color(UI.AMBER.r, UI.AMBER.g, UI.AMBER.b, 0.7))\n\t_title_rule.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_CENTER\n\t_title_sub = UI.title(\"\", 54)\n\t_title_sub.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER\n\t_title_box.add_child(_title_header)\n\t_title_box.add_child(_title_rule)\n\t_title_box.add_child(_title_sub)\n\n\nfunc _make_bar(top: bool) -> ColorRect:\n\tvar c := ColorRect.new()\n\tc.color = Color(0, 0, 0, 1)\n\tc.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE\n\tc.anchor_left = 0.0\n\tc.anchor_right = 1.0\n\tif top:\n\t\tc.anchor_top = 0.0\n\t\tc.anchor_bottom = 0.0\n\t\tc.offset_bottom = 0.0\n\telse:\n\t\tc.anchor_top = 1.0\n\t\tc.anchor_bottom = 1.0\n\t\tc.offset_top = 0.0\n\tadd_child(c)\n\treturn c\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ fades\n\nfunc to_black(duration := 0.8) -> void:\n\tvar t := create_tween()\n\tt.set_ease(Tween.EASE_IN_OUT).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_SINE)\n\tt.tween_property(_fade, \"color:a\", 1.0, duration)\n\tawait t.finished\n\n\nfunc from_black(duration := 0.8) -> void:\n\tvar t := create_tween()\n\tt.set_ease(Tween.EASE_IN_OUT).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_SINE)\n\tt.tween_property(_fade, \"color:a\", 0.0, duration)\n\tawait t.finished\n\n\nfunc set_black(on: bool) -> void:\n\t_fade.color.a = 1.0 if on else 0.0\n\n\nfunc flash(color := Color(1, 0.4, 0.3), strength := 0.5, duration := 0.45) -> void:\n\t_flash.color = Color(color.r, color.g, color.b, strength)\n\tvar t := create_tween()\n\tt.set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_EXPO)\n\tt.tween_property(_flash, \"color:a\", 0.0, duration)\n\tawait t.finished\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------- letterbox\n\nfunc letterbox(on: bool, duration := 0.9) -> void:\n\tif on == _bars_on:\n\t\treturn\n\t_bars_on = on\n\tvar vp := get_viewport().get_visible_rect().size\n\tvar target := vp.y * BAR if on else 0.0\n\tvar t := create_tween().set_parallel(true)\n\tt.set_ease(Tween.EASE_IN_OUT).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_CUBIC)\n\tt.tween_property(_bar_top, \"offset_bottom\", target, duration)\n\tt.tween_property(_bar_bottom, \"offset_top\", -target, duration)\n\tawait t.finished\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------ title cards\n\nfunc show_title(header: String, subtitle: String, hold := 2.8, appear := 1.4) -> void:\n\t_title_header.text = header.to_upper()\n\t_title_sub.text = subtitle.to_upper()\n\tvar t_in := create_tween()\n\tt_in.set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_SINE)\n\tt_in.tween_property(_title_box, \"modulate:a\", 1.0, appear)\n\tawait t_in.finished\n\tawait get_tree().create_timer(hold).timeout\n\tvar t_out := create_tween()\n\tt_out.set_ease(Tween.EASE_IN).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_SINE)\n\tt_out.tween_property(_title_box, \"modulate:a\", 0.0, appear * 0.8)\n\tawait t_out.finished\n\n\n## Non-blocking corner caption used for location callouts.\nfunc caption(text: String, hold := 3.0) -> void:\n\t_title_header.text = \"\"\n\t_title_rule.modulate.a = 0.0\n\t_title_sub.text = text.to_upper()\n\t_title_sub.add_theme_font_size_override(\"font_size\", 26)\n\tvar t_in := create_tween()\n\tt_in.tween_property(_title_box, \"modulate:a\", 1.0, 0.5)\n\tawait t_in.finished\n\tawait get_tree().create_timer(hold).timeout\n\tvar t_out := create_tween()\n\tt_out.tween_property(_title_box, \"modulate:a\", 0.0, 0.7)\n\tawait t_out.finished\n\t_title_rule.modulate.a = 1.0\n\t_title_sub.add_theme_font_size_override(\"font_size\", 54)\n"
}

\".wav\", \".mp3\"]:\n\t\tvar path := DIR + name + ext\n\t\tif ResourceLoader.exists(path):\n\t\t\ts = load(path)\n\t\t\tbreak\n\tif s == null:\n\t\tpush_warning(\"AudioDirector: missing stream '%s'\" 0.0\n\telif s is AudioStreamWAV:\n\t\ts.loop_mode = AudioStreamWAV.LOOP_FORWARD if loops else AudioStreamWAV.LOOP_DISABLED\n\t_cache[name] = s\n\treturn s\n\n\n# _ambience.has(name):\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\tvar s := stream(String(name))\n\t\tif s == null:\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\tvar p := AudioStreamPlayer.new()\n\t\tp.bus = \"Ambience\"\n\t\tp.stream = s\n\t\tp.volume_db = SILENT_DB\n\t\tadd_child(p)\n\t\tp.play()\n\t\tvar t := create_tween()\n\t\tt.tween_property(p, \"volume_db\", 0.0, fade)\n\t\t_ambience[name] = p\n\n\nfunc _release_ambience(name: String, fade: float) -> void:\n\tvar p: AudioStreamPlayer = _ambience.get(name)\n\t_ambience.erase(name)\n\tif p == null:\n\t\treturn\n\tvar t := create_tween()\n\tt.tween_property(p, \"volume_db\", SILENT_DB, fade)\n\tt.tween_callback(p.stop)\n\tt.tween_callback(p.queue_free)\n\n\nfunc stop_ambience(fade := 2.0) -> void:\n\tfor name in _ambience.keys():\n\t\t_release_ambience(name, fade)\n\n\nfunc stop_all() -> void:\n\tmusic(\"\", 0.6)\n\tstop_ambience(0.6)\n\tfor p in _flat:\n\t\tp.stop()\n\tfor p in _spatial:\n\t\tp.stop()\n"
}
```
