```json
{
  "path": "scripts/core/GameManager.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the game manager autoload with chapter flow, objectives, stats and saving.",
  "content": "extends Node\n## Run state: which chapter we are in, what the player is being asked to do,\n## progress statistics and persistence.\n\nsignal objectives_changed\nsignal objective_completed(id: String)\nsignal toast(text: String)\nsignal chapter_completed(index: int)\n\nenum State { BOOT, MENU, PLAYING, PAUSED, CINEMATIC, DEAD, COMPLETE }\n\nconst SAVE_PATH := \"user://monolith_save.json\"\nconst SAVE_VERSION := 1\nconst CHAPTER_COUNT := 5\n\nconst CHAPTERS := [\n\t{\n\t\t\"title\": \"CHAPTER I\",\n\t\t\"name\": \"THE DROWNED DISTRICT\",\n\t\t\"music\": \"mus_explore\",\n\t\t\"ambience\": [\"amb_rain\", \"amb_wind\", \"amb_water\"],\n\t\t\"brief\": \"Forty years of rain over a city that never drained.\",\n\t},\n\t{\n\t\t\"title\": \"CHAPTER II\",\n\t\t\"name\": \"THE TAVERN AT KILOMETER NINE\",\n\t\t\"music\": \"mus_menu\",\n\t\t\"ambience\": [\"amb_interior\", \"amb_wind\"],\n\t\t\"brief\": \"The only warm room left on this side of the water.\",\n\t},\n\t{\n\t\t\"title\": \"CHAPTER III\",\n\t\t\"name\": \"THE FLOOD MARKET\",\n\t\t\"music\": \"mus_combat\",\n\t\t\"ambience\": [\"amb_rain\", \"amb_water\"],\n\t\t\"brief\": \"They hold the market. They are not going to share it.\",\n\t},\n\t{\n\t\t\"title\": \"CHAPTER IV\",\n\t\t\"name\": \"THE DARK\",\n\t\t\"music\": \"mus_stealth\",\n\t\t\"ambience\": [\"amb_interior\", \"amb_embers\"],\n\t\t\"brief\": \"No rifle. No light. Somewhere above, the archive.\",\n\t},\n\t{\n\t\t\"title\": \"CHAPTER V\",\n\t\t\"name\": \"THE MEADOW\",\n\t\t\"music\": \"mus_meadow\",\n\t\t\"ambience\": [\"amb_meadow\"],\n\t\t\"brief\": \"Off the concrete, into the open.\",\n\t},\n]\n\nconst DEFAULT_STATS := {\n\t\"shots_fired\": 0,\n\t\"shots_hit\": 0,\n\t\"kills\": 0,\n\t\"deaths\": 0,\n\t\"damage_taken\": 0.0,\n\t\"playtime\": 0.0,\n\t\"started\": \"\",\n}\n\nvar state: State = State.BOOT\nvar chapter := 0\nvar objectives: Array = []          # [{id, text, done}]\nvar stats: Dictionary = {}\nvar difficulty := 1\n\nvar _first_run := true\n\n\nfunc _ready() -> void:\n\tprocess_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS\n\tInputSetup.install()\n\tstats = DEFAULT_STATS.duplicate(true)\n\n\nfunc _process(delta: float) -> void:\n\tif state == State.PLAYING:\n\t\tstats[\"playtime\"] = float(stats.get(\"playtime\", 0.0)) + delta\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- new / load\n\nfunc start_new_game() -> void:\n\tstats = DEFAULT_STATS.duplicate(true)\n\tstats[\"started\"] = Time.get_datetime_string_from_system()\n\tchapter = 0\n\tobjectives = []\n\t_first_run = false\n\tstate = State.PLAYING\n\n\nfunc has_save() -> bool:\n\treturn FileAccess.file_exists(SAVE_PATH)\n\n\nfunc save_game() -> bool:\n\tvar payload := {\n\t\t\"version\": SAVE_VERSION,\n\t\t\"chapter\": chapter,\n\t\t\"objectives\": objectives,\n\t\t\"stats\": stats,\n\t\t\"saved_at\": Time.get_datetime_string_from_system(),\n\t}\n\tvar f := FileAccess.open(SAVE_PATH, FileAccess.WRITE)\n\tif f == null:\n\t\tpush_warning(\"GameManager: could not write save\")\n\t\treturn false\n\tf.store_string(JSON.stringify(payload, \"\\t\"))\n\ttoast_message(\"PROGRESS SAVED\")\n\treturn true\n\n\nfunc load_game() -> bool:\n\tif not has_save():\n\t\treturn false\n\tvar f := FileAccess.open(SAVE_PATH, FileAccess.READ)\n\tif f == null:\n\t\treturn false\n\tvar parsed = JSON.parse_string(f.get_as_text())\n\tif typeof(parsed) != TYPE_DICTIONARY:\n\t\treturn false\n\tif int(parsed.get(\"version\", 0)) != SAVE_VERSION:\n\t\treturn false\n\tchapter = clampi(int(parsed.get(\"chapter\", 0)), 0, CHAPTER_COUNT - 1)\n\tobjectives = parsed.get(\"objectives\", [])\n\tvar loaded: Dictionary = parsed.get(\"stats\", {})\n\tstats = DEFAULT_STATS.duplicate(true)\n\tfor k in loaded:\n\t\tstats[k] = loaded[k]\n\t_first_run = false\n\tstate = State.PLAYING\n\treturn true\n\n\nfunc delete_save() -> void:\n\tif has_save():\n\t\tDirAccess.remove_absolute(ProjectSettings.globalize_path(SAVE_PATH))\n\n\nfunc is_first_run() -> bool:\n\treturn _first_run\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- objectives\n\nfunc set_objectives(list: Array) -> void:\n\tobjectives = []\n\tfor entry in list:\n\t\tobjectives.append({\n\t\t\t\"id\": String(entry.get(\"id\", \"\")),\n\t\t\t\"text\": String(entry.get(\"text\", \"\")),\n\t\t\t\"done\": bool(entry.get(\"done\", false)),\n\t\t})\n\tobjectives_changed.emit()\n\n\nfunc push_objective(id: String, text: String) -> void:\n\tif find_objective(id) != null:\n\t\treturn\n\tobjectives.append({\"id\": id, \"text\": text, \"done\": false})\n\tobjectives_changed.emit()\n\ttoast_message(text)\n\n\nfunc find_objective(id: String) -> Variant:\n\tfor o in objectives:\n\t\tif o.get(\"id\") == id:\n\t\t\treturn o\n\treturn null\n\n\nfunc is_done(id: String) -> bool:\n\tvar o = find_objective(id)\n\treturn o != null and bool(o.get(\"done\", false))\n\n\nfunc complete_objective(id: String) -> void:\n\tvar o = find_objective(id)\n\tif o == null or bool(o.get(\"done\", false)):\n\t\treturn\n\to[\"done\"] = true\n\tobjectives_changed.emit()\n\tobjective_completed.emit(id)\n\tAudioDirector.play(\"objective\", -4.0, 1.0, 0.0, \"UI\")\n\n\n## Next unfinished objective, or an empty string when the list is clear.\nfunc active_objective() -> String:\n\tfor o in objectives:\n\t\tif not bool(o.get(\"done\", false)):\n\t\t\treturn String(o.get(\"text\", \"\"))\n\treturn \"\"\n\n\nfunc objective_progress() -> Vector2:\n\tvar done := 0\n\tfor o in objectives:\n\t\tif bool(o.get(\"done\", false)):\n\t\t\tdone += 1\n\treturn Vector2(done, objectives.size())\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- state\n\nfunc set_state(s: State) -> void:\n\tstate = s\n\n\nfunc set_paused(paused: bool) -> void:\n\tget_tree().paused = paused\n\tstate = State.PAUSED if paused else State.PLAYING\n\tInput.mouse_mode = Input.MOUSE_MODE_VISIBLE if paused else Input.MOUSE_MODE_CAPTURED\n\n\nfunc chapter_info(index: int) -> Dictionary:\n\treturn CHAPTERS[clampi(index, 0, CHAPTERS.size() - 1)]\n\n\nfunc toast_message(text: String) -> void:\n\ttoast.emit(text)\n\n\nfunc add_stat(key: String, amount: float) -> void:\n\tstats[key] = float(stats.get(key, 0.0)) + amount\n\n\nfunc accuracy() -> float:\n\tvar shots := float(stats.get(\"shots_fired\", 0.0))\n\tif shots <= 0.0:\n\t\treturn 0.0\n\treturn clampf(float(stats.get(\"shots_hit\", 0.0)) / shots, 0.0, 1.0)\n"
}

> Scale + rotation: need `Transform3D` with basis = rotation_matrix * scale. If I set `mi.scale = size` and `mi.rotation_degrees = rot`, the resulting transform is R * S? In Godot, scale and rotation are combined as basis = R * S (scale applied first in local space). For a box, applying scale then rotation is what we want. Setting `mi.scale` and `mi.rotation_degrees` separately works and Godot composes basis = rotation * scale. Good, that's correct.

> Collision: add a `CollisionShape3D` child of the MeshInstance3D? Then it inherits scale. But the static body needs to be somewhere. Let me structure:

Since CollisionShape3D can't be scaled without issues (actually it can, but Godot recommends against), I'll set box_shape.size = size directly.
> 
> So:

> finds/creates a child named "__collision".

Use unit CylinderMesh (top_radius=1, bottom_radius=1, height=1) scaled by (r, h, r). Non-uniform scale on Y works fine. Cache one mesh.
> - `static func sphere(parent, radius, pos, mat)` — unit SphereMesh scaled.

Let me instead write the world foundation first, then MenuScene (which is a good early test since it exercises rendering + UI), then run a smoke test, then Player/Enemy/GameScene/Chapters.

> 
> Let me write.

> For the water shader I need a separate .gdshader file. Let me write shaders/water.gdshader and shaders/post_process.gdshader.
> 
> Water shader plan:
> - spatial shader, unshaded? No — use standard lighting with a custom normal for ripples, so SSR works. Let me write:
> ```glsl
> shader_type spatial;
> render_mode blend_mix, depth_draw_opaque, cull_back, diffuse_burley, specular_schlick_ggx;
> 
> uniform vec3 shallow_color : source_color = vec3(0.05,0.09,0.11);
> uniform vec3 deep_color : source_color = vec3(0.01,0.03,0.045);
> uniform float wave_scale = 0.35;
> uniform float wave_speed = 0.6;
> uniform float normal_strength = 0.6;
> uniform sampler2D noise_tex : hint_default_white, filter_linear_mipmap, repeat_enable;
> ...
> void vertex() { ... small vertical displacement }
> void fragment() {
> 	vec2 uv1 = UV * ... ;
> 	// sample noise at 2 scrolling offsets -> normal
> 	NORMAL_MAP = ...
> 	ALBEDO = mix(deep_color, shallow_color, ...);
> 	ROUGHNESS = 0.06;
> 	METALLIC = 0.35;
> }
> ```
> Normal map from a noise texture: I'll generate derivative normals from the noise samples in the shader (3 samples). Simpler: sample the noise texture (which is grayscale) and use `texture(noise_tex, uv).r` gradient to perturb the normal.
> 
> Let me write it properly:
> ```glsl
> float h(vec2 uv) { return texture(noise_tex, uv).r; }
> ...
> vec2 uv = UV * wave_scale + vec2(TIME*wave_speed*0.02, TIME*wave_speed*0.013);
> float e = 0.004;
> float h0 = h(uv);
> float hx = h(uv + vec2(e,0));
> float hy = h(uv + vec2(0,e));
> vec3 n = normalize(vec3((h0-hx)/e * normal_strength * 0.02, 1.0, (h0-hy)/e * normal_strength * 0.02));
> NORMAL_MAP = n * vec3(0.5) + 0.5 ;
> ```
> Hmm, NORMAL_MAP expects tangent-space normal in [0,1] map form? In Godot 4, `NORMAL_MAP` is a vec3 in tangent space with range [0,1]? Actually in Godot 4 the fragment built-ins: `NORMAL_MAP` (vec3, tangent space, default vec3(0.5,0.5,1.0) → hmm docs say "Normal mapping, in tangent space" and you assign like `NORMAL_MAP = vec3(0.0,0.0,1.0)`? Let me recall: In Godot 4 spatial shaders, `NORMAL_MAP` default is `vec3(0.5, 0.5, 1.0)`. Hmm, I'm not 100% sure. 
> 
> Let me check the Godot 4 docs from memory: In the fragment shader, the available write built-ins include `NORMAL_MAP` — "vec3 NORMAL_MAP = vec3(0.5, 0.5, 1.0)". I'm fairly confident it's in [0,1] encoded space (like a normal texture sample). Actually the Godot docs' spatial shader reference lists:
> 
> ```
> vec3 NORMAL_MAP = vec3(0.5, 0.5, 1.0)
> ```
> Hmm. There's also `NORMAL` (world space). To be safe and avoid guessing wrong, I can just write to `NORMAL` directly in world space... but NORMAL in fragment is view/world space and writable? Docs: `out vec3 NORMAL` — "Normal that comes from the vertex function, in view space"? In Godot 4 `NORMAL` in fragment is in view space.
> 
> Safest: avoid custom normals entirely and instead use two scrolling normal-ish textures via `NORMAL_MAP`... still the same uncertainty.
> 
> Alternative that's fully safe: use `NORMAL_MAP` by assigning a normal MAP texture sampled from a NoiseTexture2D generated with `as_normal_map = true`, scrolled via a second UV. That's the standard path and definitely correct: 
> ```glsl
> uniform sampler2D ripple_normal : hint_normal;
> NORMAL_MAP = texture(ripple_normal, uv).rgb;
> NORMAL_MAP_DEPTH = normal_strength;
> ```
> That's clean and correct. I'll generate a NoiseTexture2D with `as_normal_map = true` from GDScript and assign it as a shader param. 
> 
> And I can also blend two scrolling samples:
> ```glsl
> vec3 n1 = texture(ripple_normal, uv*1.0 + vec2(TIME*0.008, 0.0)).rgb;
> vec3 n2 = texture(ripple_normal, uv*1.7 - vec2(0.0, TIME*0.011)).rgb;
> NORMAL_MAP = normalize(vec3((n1.xy+n2.xy-1.0)*normal_strength, 1.0))*0.5+0.5;
> ```
> Hmm mixing. Let me keep it simple: sample once per layer and average the .rgb then remap. I'll do:
> ```glsl
> vec3 n = (n1 + n2) * 0.5;
> NORMAL_MAP = mix(vec3(0.5,0.5,1.0), n, normal_strength);
> ```
> That's safe.
> 
> For the water color: use `ALBEDO`, `ROUGHNESS`, `METALLIC`, `SPECULAR`, and `FRESNEL`? Godot 4 has `FRESNEL` for rim. Let me add depth-based color using `DEPTH_TEXTURE` — needs `depth_texture` available in fragment (it is, in forward+). Use:
> ```glsl
> float scene_depth = textureLod(DEPTH_TEXTURE, SCREEN_UV, 0.0).r;
> float depth = linearize_depth? 
> ```
> Depth linearization requires INV_PROJECTION_MATRIX. Godot 4 provides `INV_PROJECTION_MATRIX` in fragment. Standard snippet:
> ```glsl
> float depth_lin(float d) { return -PROJECTION_MATRIX[3][2] / (d + PROJECTION_MATRIX[2][2]); }
> ```
> Hmm getting complicated. Let me skip depth-based color and just use a fresnel-driven mix between deep and shallow using `FRESNEL` (which Godot 4 provides in fragment as `float FRESNEL`). Yes, Godot 4 spatial fragment has `float FRESNEL`. I'll use it.
> 
> Also add `ALPHA` transparency? Keep opaque for SSR.
> 
> Also I want `RIM`? Not needed.
> 
> OK let me write the water shader simply and safely.
> 
> Also a second shader: `post_process.gdshader` for vignette/grain/chromatic aberration. Full-screen ColorRect with a shader reading SCREEN_TEXTURE (in canvas_item shaders, use `hint_screen_texture`). Implementation:
> ```glsl
> shader_type canvas_item;
> render_mode unshaded;
> uniform sampler2D screen_tex : hint_screen_texture, filter_linear_mipmap;
> uniform float vignette_strength = 0.45;
> uniform float grain_amount = 0.035;
> uniform float aberration = 0.0012;
> uniform float damage = 0.0;
> uniform float low_health = 0.0;
> uniform float desaturate = 0.0;
> uniform float time_seed = 0.0;
> ```
> Then sample with offset per channel.
> 
> In Godot 4, `hint_screen_texture` on a canvas_item shader works when the ColorRect is in a CanvasLayer over the 3D scene. Yes.
> 
> Note: `TIME` is available.
> 
> Careful: For screen texture sampling in canvas_item shader, use `texture(screen_tex, SCREEN_UV + offset)`. SCREEN_UV is available in canvas_item fragment? Yes, Godot 4 canvas_item has `SCREEN_UV`. Good.

{
  "path": "scripts/world/Build.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the primitive geometry builder toolkit.",
  "content": "class_name Build\n## Kit-bash toolkit for building levels out of unit primitives.\n##\n## All meshes are shared 1x1x1 resources and are sized with the node\n## transform, so the entire city costs a handful of GPU buffers.  Collision\n## shapes are funnelled into one StaticBody3D per parent, which keeps the\n## physics broadphase tidy.\n\nstatic var _meshes: Dictionary = {}\nstatic var _shapes: Dictionary = {}\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- meshes\n\nstatic func _mesh(kind: String) -> Mesh:\n\tif _meshes.has(kind):\n\t\treturn _meshes[kind]\n\tvar m: Mesh\n\tmatch kind:\n\t\t\"box\":\n\t\t\tvar b := BoxMesh.new()\n\t\t\tb.size = Vector3.ONE\n\t\t\tm = b\n\t\t\"sphere\":\n\t\t\tvar s := SphereMesh.new()\n\t\t\ts.radius = 0.5\n\t\t\ts.height = 1.0\n\t\t\ts.radial_segments = 20\n\t\t\ts.rings = 10\n\t\t\tm = s\n\t\t\"cyl\":\n\t\t\tvar c := CylinderMesh.new()\n\t\t\tc.top_radius = 0.5\n\t\t\tc.bottom_radius = 0.5\n\t\t\tc.height = 1.0\n\t\t\tc.radial_segments = 18\n\t\t\tc.rings = 1\n\t\t\tm = c\n\t\t\"cone\":\n\t\t\tvar k := CylinderMesh.new()\n\t\t\tk.top_radius = 0.0\n\t\t\tk.bottom_radius = 0.5\n\t\t\tk.height = 1.0\n\t\t\tk.radial_segments = 14\n\t\t\tm = k\n\t\t\"capsule\":\n\t\t\tvar p := CapsuleMesh.new()\n\t\t\tp.radius = 0.5\n\t\t\tp.height = 2.0\n\t\t\tp.radial_segments = 14\n\t\t\tp.rings = 6\n\t\t\tm = p\n\t\t\"plane\":\n\t\t\tvar pl := PlaneMesh.new()\n\t\t\tpl.size = Vector2.ONE\n\t\t\tpl.subdivide_width = 4\n\t\t\tpl.subdivide_depth = 4\n\t\t\tm = pl\n\t\t\"quad\":\n\t\t\tvar q := QuadMesh.new()\n\t\t\tq.size = Vector2.ONE\n\t\t\tm = q\n\t\t\"prism\":\n\t\t\tvar pr := PrismMesh.new()\n\t\t\tpr.size = Vector3.ONE\n\t\t\tm = pr\n\t\t_:\n\t\t\tm = BoxMesh.new()\n\t_meshes[kind] = m\n\treturn m\n\n\nstatic func _body_shape(size: Vector3) -> Shape3D:\n\tvar key := \"%.3f_%.3f_%.3f\" % [size.x, size.y, size.z]\n\tif _shapes.has(key):\n\t\treturn _shapes[key]\n\tvar s := BoxShape3D.new()\n\ts.size = size\n\t_shapes[key] = s\n\treturn s\n\n\nstatic func _sphere_shape(r: float) -> Shape3D:\n\tvar key := \"s%.3f\" % r\n\tif _shapes.has(key):\n\t\treturn _shapes[key]\n\tvar s := SphereShape3D.new()\n\ts.radius = r\n\t_shapes[key] = s\n\treturn s\n\n\nstatic func xform(pos: Vector3, rot_deg := Vector3.ZERO, scl := Vector3.ONE) -> Transform3D:\n\tvar r := Basis.from_euler(Vector3(deg_to_rad(rot_deg.x), deg_to_rad(rot_deg.y), deg_to_rad(rot_deg.z)))\n\treturn Transform3D(r * Basis.from_scale(scl), pos)\n\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------- physics\n\nstatic func collision_body(parent: Node) -> StaticBody3D:\n\tfor c in parent.get_children():\n\t\tif c is StaticBody3D and String(c.name) == \"__collision\":\n\t\t\treturn c\n\tvar b := StaticBody3D.new()\n\tb.name = \"__collision\"\n\tb.collision_layer = 1\n\tb.collision_mask = 0\n\tparent.add_child(b)\n\treturn b\n\n\nstatic func _add_shape(parent: Node3D, pos: Vector3, rot: Vector3, shape: Shape3D) -> void:\n\tvar body := collision_body(parent)\n\tvar cs := CollisionShape3D.new()\n\tcs.shape = shape\n\tcs.transform = xform(pos, rot)\n\tbody.add_child(cs)\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------- primitives\n\nstatic func box(parent: Node3D, size: Vector3, pos: Vector3, mat: Material,\n\t\trot := Vector3.ZERO, collide := true) -> MeshInstance3D:\n\tvar mi := MeshInstance3D.new()\n\tmi.mesh = _mesh(\"box\")\n\tif mat != null:\n\t\tmi.material_override = mat\n\tmi.transform = xform(pos, rot, size)\n\tparent.add_child(mi)\n\tif collide:\n\t\t_add_shape(parent, pos, rot, _body_shape(size))\n\treturn mi\n\n\nstatic func cylinder(parent: Node3D, radius: float, height: float, pos: Vector3,\n\t\tmat: Material, rot := Vector3.ZERO, collide := false) -> MeshInstance3D:\n\tvar mi := MeshInstance3D.new()\n\tmi.mesh = _mesh(\"cyl\")\n\tif mat != null:\n\t\tmi.material_override = mat\n\tmi.transform = xform(pos, rot, Vector3(radius * 2.0, height, radius * 2.0))\n\tparent.add_child(mi)\n\tif collide:\n\t\t_add_shape(parent, pos, rot, _body_shape(Vector3(radius * 2.0, height, radius * 2.0)))\n\treturn mi\n\n\nstatic func cone(parent: Node3D, radius: float, height: float, pos: Vector3,\n\t\tmat: Material, rot := Vector3.ZERO) -> MeshInstance3D:\n\tvar mi := MeshInstance3D.new()\n\tmi.mesh = _mesh(\"cone\")\n\tif mat != null:\n\t\tmi.material_override = mat\n\tmi.transform = xform(pos, rot, Vector3(radius * 2.0, height, radius * 2.0))\n\tparent.add_child(mi)\n\treturn mi\n\n\nstatic func sphere(parent: Node3D, radius: float, pos: Vector3, mat: Material,\n\t\tsquash := Vector3.ONE, collide := false) -> MeshInstance3D:\n\tvar mi := MeshInstance3D.new()\n\tmi.mesh = _mesh(\"sphere\")\n\tif mat != null:\n\t\tmi.material_override = mat\n\tmi.transform = xform(pos, Vector3.ZERO, Vector3(radius * 2, radius * 2, radius * 2) * squash)\n\tparent.add_child(mi)\n\tif collide:\n\t\t_add_shape(parent, pos, Vector3.ZERO, _sphere_shape(radius))\n\treturn mi\n\n\nstatic func capsule(parent: Node3D, radius: float, height: float, pos: Vector3,\n\t\tmat: Material, rot := Vector3.ZERO) -> MeshInstance3D:\n\tvar mi := MeshInstance3D.new()\n\tmi.mesh = _mesh(\"capsule\")\n\tif mat != null:\n\t\tmi.material_override = mat\n\tmi.transform = xform(pos, rot, Vector3(radius * 2.0, height * 0.5, radius * 2.0))\n\tparent.add_child(mi)\n\treturn mi\n\n\nstatic func floor_plane(parent: Node3D, size: Vector2, pos: Vector3, mat: Material) -> MeshInstance3D:\n\tvar mi := MeshInstance3D.new()\n\tmi.mesh = _mesh(\"plane\")\n\tif mat != null:\n\t\tmi.material_override = mat\n\tmi.transform = xform(pos, Vector3.ZERO, Vector3(size.x, 1.0, size.y))\n\tparent.add_child(mi)\n\treturn mi\n\n\nstatic func quad(parent: Node3D, size: Vector2, pos: Vector3, mat: Material,\n\t\trot := Vector3.ZERO) -> MeshInstance3D:\n\tvar mi := MeshInstance3D.new()\n\tmi.mesh = _mesh(\"quad\")\n\tif mat != null:\n\t\tmi.material_override = mat\n\tmi.transform = xform(pos, rot, Vector3(size.x, size.y, 1.0))\n\tparent.add_child(mi)\n\treturn mi\n\n\nstatic func ramp(parent: Node3D, width: float, length: float, height: float, pos: Vector3,\n\t\tmat: Material, yaw := 0.0) -> MeshInstance3D:\n\t## A box tilted about its local X axis so it reads as a ramp.\n\tvar pitch := atan2(height, length)\n\tvar len := sqrt(length * length + height * height)\n\treturn box(parent, Vector3(width, 0.4, len),\n\t\t\tpos + Vector3(0, height * 0.5, 0), mat, Vector3(-rad_to_deg(pitch), yaw, 0), true)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ detail\n\nstatic func multimesh(parent: Node3D, kind: String, mat: Material, xforms: Array,\n\t\tname_hint := \"Scatter\") -> MultiMeshInstance3D:\n\tvar mm := MultiMesh.new()\n\tmm.transform_format = MultiMesh.TRANSFORM_3D\n\tmm.mesh = _mesh(kind)\n\tmm.instance_count = xforms.size()\n\tfor i in xforms.size():\n\t\tmm.set_instance_transform(i, xforms[i])\n\tvar mi := MultiMeshInstance3D.new()\n\tmi.name = name_hint\n\tmi.multimesh = mm\n\tif mat != null:\n\t\tmi.material_override = mat\n\tparent.add_child(mi)\n\treturn mi\n\n\n## Scatter `count` randomly transformed instances inside a box region.\nstatic func scatter(parent: Node3D, kind: String, mat: Material, count: int,\n\t\tcenter: Vector3, extents: Vector3, base_scale: Vector3, seed_v := 1,\n\t\tyaw_random := true, jitter := 0.35) -> MultiMeshInstance3D:\n\tvar r := RandomNumberGenerator.new()\n\tr.seed = seed_v\n\tvar xf: Array = []\n\tfor i in count:\n\t\tvar p := center + Vector3(\n\t\t\t\tr.randf_range(-extents.x, extents.x),\n\t\t\t\tr.randf_range(-extents.y, extents.y),\n\t\t\t\tr.randf_range(-extents.z, extents.z))\n\t\tvar s := base_scale * (1.0 - jitter * 0.5 + r.randf() * jitter)\n\t\tvar yaw := r.randf_range(0.0, TAU) if yaw_random else 0.0\n\t\txf.append(xform(p, Vector3(0, rad_to_deg(yaw), 0), s))\n\treturn multimesh(parent, kind, mat, xf, \"Scatter_%s\" % kind)\n\n\nstatic func rubble_field(parent: Node3D, center: Vector3, extents: Vector3, count := 60,\n\t\tseed_v := 5) -> void:\n\tscatter(parent, \"box\", MatLib.concrete_dark(), count, center + Vector3(0, 0.15, 0),\n\t\t\textents, Vector3(0.55, 0.35, 0.55), seed_v, true, 0.9)\n\tscatter(parent, \"box\", MatLib.brick(), int(count * 0.4), center,\n\t\t\textents, Vector3(0.3, 0.2, 0.3), seed_v + 1, true, 1.0)\n\n\nstatic func grass_field(parent: Node3D, center: Vector3, extents: Vector3, count := 900,\n\t\tseed_v := 9, height := 0.5) -> void:\n\tvar m := MatLib.flat(Color(\"6f9d47\"), \"grass_blade\")\n\tm.albedo_color = Color(\"6f9d47\")\n\tm.cull_mode = BaseMaterial3D.CULL_DISABLED\n\tscatter(parent, \"quad\", m, count, center + Vector3(0, height * 0.5, 0), extents,\n\t\t\tVector3(0.12, height, 1.0), seed_v, true, 0.5)\n\n\n# -------------------------------------------------------------- decorations\n\nstatic func sign(parent: Node3D, text: String, pos: Vector3, rot: Vector3, color: Color,\n\t\tsize := 48, no_depth := false) -> Label3D:\n\tvar l := Label3D.new()\n\tl.text = text\n\tl.font_size = size\n\tl.modulate = color\n\tl.outline_size = 0\n\tl.pixel_size = 0.006\n\tl.shaded = false\n\tl.double_sided = true\n\tl.no_depth_test = no_depth\n\tl.alpha_cut = Label3D.ALPHA_CUT_DISCARD\n\tl.rotation_degrees = rot\n\tl.position = pos\n\tl.render_priority = 1\n\tparent.add_child(l)\n\treturn l\n\n\nstatic func billboard(parent: Node3D, size: Vector2, pos: Vector3, color: Color,\n\t\talpha := 0.6, add := true) -> MeshInstance3D:\n\tvar m := StandardMaterial3D.new()\n\tm.albedo_color = Color(color.r, color.g, color.b, alpha)\n\tm.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED\n\tm.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA\n\tm.billboard_mode = BaseMaterial3D.BILLBOARD_ENABLED\n\tm.blend_mode = BaseMaterial3D.BLEND_MODE_ADD if add else BaseMaterial3D.BLEND_MODE_MIX\n\tm.cull_mode = BaseMaterial3D.CULL_DISABLED\n\tm.disable_receive_shadows = true\n\tvar mi := MeshInstance3D.new()\n\tmi.mesh = _mesh(\"quad\")\n\tmi.material_override = m\n\tmi.transform = xform(pos, Vector3.ZERO, Vector3(size.x, size.y, 1.0))\n\tmi.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF\n\tparent.add_child(mi)\n\treturn mi\n\n\nstatic func shaft(parent: Node3D, size: Vector2, pos: Vector3, rot: Vector3, color: Color,\n\t\talpha := 0.14) -> MeshInstance3D:\n\t## Unshaded additive quad used to fake a volumetric light shaft.\n\tvar m := StandardMaterial3D.new()\n\tm.albedo_color = Color(color.r, color.g, color.b, alpha)\n\tm.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED\n\tm.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA\n\tm.blend_mode = BaseMaterial3D.BLEND_MODE_ADD\n\tm.cull_mode = BaseMaterial3D.CULL_DISABLED\n\tm.disable_receive_shadows = true\n\tm.billboard_mode = BaseMaterial3D.BILLBOARD_ENABLED\n\tm.billboard_keep_scale = true\n\tvar mi := MeshInstance3D.new()\n\tmi.mesh = _mesh(\"quad\")\n\tmi.material_override = m\n\tmi.transform = xform(pos, rot, Vector3(size.x, size.y, 1.0))\n\tmi.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF\n\tparent.add_child(mi)\n\treturn mi\n\n\nstatic func omni(parent: Node3D, pos: Vector3, color: Color, energy: float, range_m: float,\n\t\tshadows := false, volumetric := 1.0) -> OmniLight3D:\n\tvar l := OmniLight3D.new()\n\tl.position = pos\n\tl.light_color = color\n\tl.light_energy = energy\n\tl.omni_range = range_m\n\tl.omni_attenuation = 1.4\n\tl.shadow_enabled = shadows\n\tl.shadow_bias = 0.06\n\tl.light_volumetric_fog_energy = volumetric\n\tl.light_specular = 0.6\n\tparent.add_child(l)\n\treturn l\n\n\nstatic func spot(parent: Node3D, pos: Vector3, rot: Vector3, color: Color, energy: float,\n\t\trange_m: float, angle := 32.0, shadows := true, volumetric := 2.0) -> SpotLight3D:\n\tvar l := SpotLight3D.new()\n\tl.position = Color, energy: float,\n\t\tangular := 3.0) -> DirectionalLight3D:\n\tvar l := DirectionalLight3D.new()\n\tl.rotation_degrees = rot\n\tl.light_color = color\n\tl.light_energy = >     col *= mix(1.0, v, vignette);
>     ...

> ```
> Hmm careful: `smoothstep(0.85, 0.18, x)` with edge0 > edge1 — GLSL smoothstep requires edge0 < edge1 (undefined otherwise). Use `1.0 - smoothstep(0.18, 0.85, ...)`. Let me write properly:
> 
> ```glsl
> float vig = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.25, 0.95, r2 * (1.0 + vignette));
> col *= mix(1.0, vig, 0.85);
> ```
> 
> Then desaturate: `col = mix(col, vec3(dot(col, vec3(0.299,0.587,0.114))), desaturate)`.
> 
> Then damage overlay: red tint edges
> ```glsl
> if (damage > 0.001) {
>     float edge = smoothstep(0.05, 0.9, r2 * 1.6);
>     col = mix(col, damage_color.rgb, damage * (0.35 + edge * 0.65));
> }
> ```
> 
> low_health: pulsing red vignette
> ```glsl
> if (low_health > 0.001) {

>     float edge = smoothstep(0.1, 0.9, r2 * 1.4);
>     col = mix(col, vec3(0.35, 0.02, 0.02), low_health * pulse * edge * 0.8);
> }
> ```
> 
> Output `COLOR = vec4(col, 1.0)`.
> 
> In canvas_item shaders, output is `COLOR`. And SCREEN_UV is available. `TIME` available. Good.
> 
> Note: `hint_screen_texture` requires the ColorRect to be drawn after the 3D content — a CanvasLayer above the 3D scene works.

{
  "path": "shaders/water.gdshader",
  "instructions": "Create the animated water surface shader.",
  "content": "shader_type spatial;\nrender_mode blend_mix, depth_draw_opaque, cull_back, diffuse_burley, specular_schlick_ggx;\n\n// Flood water.  Two counter-scrolling layers of a procedural normal map give\n// the surface motion; a Fresnel term mixes deep/shallow/horizon colour so the\n// water reads as reflective at grazing angles without costing a reflection\n// probe.  Works with the screen-space reflections enabled in the Environment.\n\nuniform sampler2D ripple_normal : hint_normal, filter_linear_mipmap, repeat_enable;\n\nuniform vec4 deep_color : source_color = vec4(0.010, 0.028, 0.038, 1.0);\nuniform vec4 shallow_color : source_color = vec4(0.055, 0.105, 0.125, 1.0);\nuniform vec4 horizon_color : source_color = vec4(0.150, 0.205, 0.240, 1.0);\n\nuniform float normal_strength : hint_range(0.0, 2.0) = 0.9;\nuniform float scroll_speed : hint_range(0.0, 0.2) = 0.022;\nuniform float uv_scale : hint_range(0.5, 30.0) = 7.0;\nuniform float wave_height : hint_range(0.0, 0.5) = 0.045;\nuniform float wave_scale : hint_range(0.0, 0.5) = 0.07;\nuniform float roughness_min : hint_range(0.0, 0.4) = 0.05;\nuniform float metallic_amount : hint_range(0.0, 1.0) = 0.35;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n\tfloat w = sin(VERTEX.x * wave_scale + TIME * 0.75)\n\t\t\t* cos(VERTEX.z * wave_scale * 1.27 - TIME * 0.52);\n\tVERTEX.y += w * wave_height;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n\tvec2 uv = UV * uv_scale;\n\tvec3 n1 = texture(ripple_normal, uv + vec2(TIME * scroll_speed, TIME * scroll_speed * 0.55)).rgb;\n\tvec3 n2 = texture(ripple_normal, uv * 1.70 - vec2(TIME * scroll_speed * 0.65, TIME * scroll_speed)).rgb;\n\tNORMAL_MAP = mix(vec3(0.5, 0.5, 1.0), (n1 + n2) * 0.5, normal_strength);\n\tNORMAL_MAP_DEPTH = 1.0;\n\n\tfloat facing = clamp(FRESNEL, 0.0, 1.0);\n\tvec3 water = mix(deep_color.rgb, shallow_color.rgb, 0.30 + 0.70 * (1.0 - facing));\n\twater = mix(water, horizon_color.rgb, facing * 0.60);\n\n\tALBEDO = water;\n\tROUGHNESS = mix(roughness_min, roughness_min + 0.10, facing);\n\tMETALLIC = metallic_amount;\n\tSPECULAR = 0.85;\n}\n"
}

```json
{
  "path": "shaders/post_process.gdshader",
  "instructions": "Create the full-screen post-processing shader.",
  "content": "shader_type canvas_item;\nrender_mode unshaded;\n\n// Final grade for the 3D image: chromatic aberration, film grain, vignette,\n// plus the two feedback effects that carry combat information -- a red damage\n// wash and a low-health pulse.  Driven from PostFX.gd.\n\nuniform sampler2D screen_tex : hint_screen_texture, filter_linear_mipmap;\n\nuniform float vignette_amount : hint_range(0.0, 2.0) = 0.55;\nuniform float grain_amount : hint_range(0.0, 0.25) = 0.028;\nuniform float aberration : hint_range(0.0, 0.01) = 0.0011;\nuniform float damage : hint_range(0.0, 1.0) = 0.0;\nuniform float low_health : hint_range(0.0, 1.0) = 0.0;\nuniform float desaturate : hint_range(0.0, 1.0) = 0.0;\nuniform float contrast_boost : hint_range(0.5, 2.0) = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 damage_color : source_color = vec4(0.55, 0.05, 0.03, 1.0);\nuniform float grain_enabled = 1.0;\n\nfloat hash12(vec2 p) {\n\tvec3 p3 = fract(vec3(p.xyx) * 0.1031);\n\tp3 += dot(p3, p3.yzx + 33.33);\n\treturn fract((p3.x + p3.y) * p3.z);\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n\tvec2 uv = SCREEN_UV;\n\tvec2 dir = uv - vec2(0.5);\n\tfloat r2 = dot(dir, dir);\n\n\t// --- chromatic aberration, stronger under stress -------------------\n\tfloat ab = aberration * (1.0 + damage * 3.0 + low_health * 2.5);\n\tvec3 col;\n\tcol.r = texture(screen_tex, uv + dir * ab).r;\n\tcol.g = texture(screen_tex, uv).g;\n\tcol.b = texture(screen_tex, uv - dir * ab).b;\n\n\t// --- contrast / saturation -----------------------------------------\n\tcol = (col - 0.5) * contrast_boost + 0.5;\n\tif (desaturate > 0.001) {\n\t\tfloat lum = dot(col, vec3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));\n\t\tcol = mix(col, vec3(lum), desaturate);\n\t}\n\n\t// --- film grain ----------------------------------------------------\n\tif (grain_enabled > 0.5 && grain_amount > 0.0001) {\n\t\tfloat g = hash12(uv * vec2(1920.0, 1080.0) + vec2(TIME * 37.0, TIME * 17.0)) - 0.5;\n\t\tcol += g * grain_amount;\n\t}\n\n\t// --- vignette ------------------------------------------------------\n\tfloat vig = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.18, 0.95, r2 * (1.0 + vignette_amount));\n\tcol *= mix(1.0, vig, clamp(vignette_amount, 0.0, 1.0));\n\n\t// --- combat feedback ----------------------------------------------\n\tif (damage > 0.001) {\n\t\tfloat edge = smoothstep(0.02, 0.75, r2 * 1.7);\n\t\tcol = mix(col, damage_color.rgb, clamp(damage, 0.0, 1.0) * (0.30 + edge * 0.70));\n\t}\n\tif (low_health > 0.001) {\n\t\tfloat pulse = 0.50 + 0.50 * sin(TIME * 3.6);\n\t\tfloat edge = smoothstep(0.05, 0.80, r2 * 1.5);\n\t\tcol = mix(col, vec3(0.34, 0.015, 0.02), low_health * pulse * edge * 0.85);\n\t}\n\n\tCOLOR = vec4(max(col, vec3(0.0)), 1.0);\n}\n"
}

Orientation: Decal projects along -Y by default, so rotate so -Y aligns with the surface normal: `look_at_from_position(pos + n*0.1, pos, ...)`. A Decal's projection axis is local -Y. To align -Y with normal n: build a basis where the Y axis = -n.

> Let me construct manually:
> ```gdscript
> static func _decal_basis(n: Vector3) -> Basis:
> 	var y := -n.normalized()
> 	var x := y.cross(Vector3.UP)
> 	if x.length() < 0.01: x = y.cross(Vector3.RIGHT)
> 	x = x.normalized()
> 	var z := x.cross(y).normalized()
> 	return Basis(x, y, z)
> ```

1.1\n\tpm.color_ramp = _ramp(Color(1, 0.85, 0.4, 0.0), Color(1, 0.6, 0.2, 0.9), Color(0.5, 0.1, 0.02, 0.0))\n\tp.process_material = pm\n\tparent.add_child(p)\n\treturn p\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers\n\nstatic func _ramp(a: Color, b: Color, c: Color) -> GradientTexture1D:\n\tvar g := Gradient.new()\n\tg.set_offset(0, 0.0)\n\tg.set_color(0, a)\n\tg.set_offset(1, 0.5)\n\tg.set_color(1, b)\n\tg.add_point(1.0, c)\n\tvar t := GradientTexture1D.new()\n\tt.gradient = g\n\tt.width = 64\n\treturn t\n\n\n## A radial gradient, used as the bullet-hole decal albedo.\nstatic func hole_texture() -> GradientTexture2D:\n\tvar g := Gradient.new()\n\tg.set_offset(0, 0.0)\n\tg.set_color(0, Color(0.02, 0.02, 0.02, 0.95))\n\tg.set_offset(1, 0.35)\n\tg.set_color(1, Color(0.05, 0.05, 0.05, 0.75))\n\tg.add_point(1.0, Color(0.1, 0.1, 0.1, 0.0))\n\tvar t := GradientTexture2D.new()\n\tt.gradient = g\n\tt.fill = GradientTexture2D.FILL_RADIAL\n\tt.fill_from = Vector2(0.5, 0.5)\n\tt.fill_to = Vector2(1.0, 0.5)\n\tt.width = 64\n\tt.height = 64\n\treturn t\n\n\n## Basis that points a Decal's projection axis (-Y) along `n`.\nstatic func decal_basis(n: Vector3) -> Basis:\n\tvar y := -n.normalized()\n\tvar x := y.cross(Vector3.UP)\n\tif x.length_squared() < 0.0001:\n\t\tx = y.cross(Vector3.RIGHT)\n\tx = x.normalized()\n\tvar z := x.cross(y).normalized()\n\treturn Basis(x, y, z)\n\n\nstatic func align_z(dir: Vector3, up := Vector3.UP) -> Basis:\n\tvar d := dir.normalized()\n\tif absf(d.dot(up)) > 0.999:\n\t\tup = Vector3.RIGHT\n\treturn Basis.looking_at(d, up)\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "path": "scripts/fx/TracerPool.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the pooled bullet tracer renderer.",
  "content": "class_name TracerPool\nextends Node3D\n## Recycled bullet tracers.\n##\n## A tracer is a thin box stretched from the muzzle to the impact point and\n## shown for a few frames.  Nothing is allocated per shot: the pool is built\n## once and each slot carries its own expiry stamp.\n\nconst LIFE := 0.06\nconst SLOTS := 40\n\nvar _slots: Array[MeshInstance3D] = []\nvar _expiry := PackedFloat32Array()\nvar _next := 0\n\n\nfunc _ready() -> void:\n\ttop_level = true\n\tfor i in SLOTS:\n\t\tvar mi := MeshInstance3D.new()\n\t\tvar bm := BoxMesh.new()\n\t\tbm.size = Vector3.ONE\n\t\tmi.mesh = bm\n\t\tvar m := StandardMaterial3D.new()\n\t\tm.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED\n\t\tm.albedo_color = Color(1.0, 0.82, 0.45, 0.95)\n\t\tm.blend_mode = BaseMaterial3D.BLEND_MODE_ADD\n\t\tm.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA\n\t\tm.disable_receive_shadows = true\n\t\tmi.material_override = m\n\t\tmi.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF\n\t\tmi.visible = false\n\t\tadd_child(mi)\n\t\t_slots.append(mi)\n\t\t_expiry.append(0.0)\n\tset_process(true)\n\n\nfunc _process(_delta: float) -> void:\n\tvar now := Time.get_ticks_msec() / 1000.0\n\tfor i in _slots.size():\n\t\tif _slots[i].visible and now >= _expiry[i]:\n\t\t\t_slots[i].visible = false\n\n\n## Draw a tracer from `from` to `to`.  `width` is the visual thickness in metres.\nfunc fire(from: Vector3, to: Vector3, width := 0.022, color := Color(1.0, 0.82, 0.45)) -> void:\n\tvar d := to - from\n\tvar dist := d.length()\n\tif dist < 0.05:\n\t\treturn\n\tvar mi := _slots[_next]\n\t_next = (_next + 1) % SLOTS\n\tmi.visible = true\n\tvar mat := mi.material_override as StandardMaterial3D\n\tif mat != null:\n\t\tmat.albedo_color = Color(color.r, color.g, color.b, 0.95)\n\tvar t := Transform3D(FX.align_z(d), from + d * 0.5)\n\tmi.transform = t.scaled_local(Vector3(width, width, dist))\n\t_expiry[_next == 0] = Time.get_ticks_msec() / 1000.0 + LIFE\n\t_expiry[_next == 0] = Time.get_ticks_msec() / 1000.0 + LIFE\n\tvar idx := (_next - 1 + SLOTS) % SLOTS\n\t_expiry[idx] = Time.get_ticks_msec() / 1000.0 + LIFE\n\n\nfunc clear_all() -> void:\n\tfor mi in _slots:\n\t\tmi.visible = false\n"
}
```

> _Reasoning_
> The TracerPool file has redundant junk lines:
> ```gdscript
> 	_expiry[_next == 0] = ...
> 	_expiry[_next == 0] = ...
> 	var idx := (_next - 1 + SLOTS) % SLOTS
> 	_expiry[idx] = ...
> ```
> The first two are wrong/bogus. Let me fix to just the idx version.
> 
> Note: `_expiry` is a PackedFloat32Array; assigning by index works. Good.
> 
> Let me fix that.
> 
> Then ImpactPool.gd.
> 
> Actually `_expiry[idx]` where idx = (_next - 1 + SLOTS) % SLOTS. Since `_next` was already advanced, the slot we just used is `_next - 1 mod SLOTS`. Correct.
> 
> Let me fix.
> 
> Also `_slots[i].visible` — MeshInstance3D has `visible`. Good.
> 
> Also `FX.align_z(d)` builds `Basis.looking_at(d, up)`. Then `t.scaled_local(Vector3(width,width,dist))` — scaled_local applies scale in local space, i.e., basis * scale. For a unit box, that gives a box of those dimensions rotated by the basis. Good — local Z is length. But note `Basis.looking_at` gives a basis where -Z points toward the target. The box is symmetric along Z so it doesn't matter.
> 
> Now ImpactPool.

{

  "instructions": "Create the pooled impact effects: decals and spark bursts.",
  "content": "class_name ImpactPool\nextends Node3D\n## Bullet impacts: a projected decal plus a short spark burst.\n##\n## Decals are recycled in a ring buffer (oldest hole is re-used), spark\n## emitters are pooled one-shot GPUParticles3D nodes that get restarted in\n## place -- so a firefight costs no allocations at all.\n\nconst DECAL_SLOTS := 48\nconst SPARK_SLOTS := 14\nconst DECAL_LIFE := 24.0\n\nvar _decals: Array[Decal] = []\nvar _decal_expiry := PackedFloat32Array()\nvar _decal_next := 0\n\nvar _sparks: Array[GPUParticles3D] = []\nvar _spark_next := 0\n\nvar _splashes: Array[GPUParticles3D] = []\nvar _splash_next := 0\n\nvar _hole_tex: Texture2D\n\n\nfunc _ready() -> void:\n\ttop_level = true\n\t_hole_tex = FX.hole_texture()\n\n\tfor i in DECAL_SLOTS:\n\t\tvar d := Decal.new()\n\t\td.texture_albedo = _hole_tex\n\t\td.size = Vector3(0.28, 0.6, 0.28)\n\t\td.modulate = Color(0.08, 0.07, 0.07, 1.0)\n\t\td.albedo_mix = 0.85\n\t\td.upper_fade = 0.35\n\t\td.lower_fade = 0.35\n\t\td.distance_fade_enabled = true\n\t\td.distance_fade_begin = 22.0\n\t\td.distance_fade_length = 8.0\n\t\tadd_child(d)\n\t\t_decals.append(d)\n\t\t_decal_expiry.append(0.0)\n\n\tfor i in SPARK_SLOTS:\n\t\tvar p := _make_burst(\"Spark\", Color(1.0, 0.72, 0.32, 0.95), 12, 0.032, 0.32,\n\t\t\t\tVector3(0, -9.8, 0), 4.0, 11.0)\n\t\tadd_child(p)\n\t\t_sparks.append(p)\n\n\tfor i in 3:\n\t\tvar p := _make_burst(\"Splash\", Color(0.78, 0.88, 0.94, 0.85), 22, 0.05, 0.75,\n\t\t\t\tVector3(0, -11.0, 0), 3.0, 8.0)\n\t\tadd_child(p)\n\t\t_splashes.append(p)\n\n\tset_process(true)\n\n\nfunc _make_burst(name_hint: String, color: Color, amount: int, size: float, life: float,\n\t\tgravity: Vector3, vmin: float, vmax: float) -> GPUParticles3D:\n\tvar p := GPUParticles3D.new()\n\tp.name = name_hint\n\tp.emitting = false\n\tp.one_shot = true\n\tp.amount = amount\n\tp.lifetime = life\n\tp.explosiveness = 1.0\n\tp.fixed_fps = 0\n\tvar q := QuadMesh.new()\n\tq.size = Vector2(size, size)\n\tp.draw_pass_1 = q\n\tp.material_override = FX.sprite_mat(\"burst_\" + name_hint, color, true, true)\n\tp.visibility_aabb = AABB(Vector3(-3, -3, -3), Vector3(6, 6, 6))\n\tvar pm := ParticleProcessMaterial.new()\n\tpm.emission_shape = ParticleProcessMaterial.EMISSION_SHAPE_SPHERE\n\tpm.emission_sphere_radius = 0.03\n\tpm.direction = Vector3(0, 1, 0)\n\tpm.spread = 60.0\n\tpm.initial_velocity_min = vmin\n\tpm.initial_velocity_max = vmax\n\tpm.gravity = gravity\n\tpm.damping_min = 1.5\n\tpm.damping_max = 4.0\n\tpm.scale_min = 0.5\n\tpm.scale_max = 1.6\n\tpm.color_ramp = _ramp(Color(color.r, color.g, color.b, 0.0),\n\t\t\tColor(color.r, color.g, color.b, color.a),\n\t\t\tColor(color.r * 0.4, color.g * 0.3, color.b * 0.2, 0.0))\n\tp.process_material = pm\n\treturn p\n\n\nfunc _ramp(a: Color, b: Color, c: Color) -> GradientTexture1D:\n\tvar g := Gradient.new()\n\tg.set_offset(0, 0.0)\n\tg.set_color(0, a)\n\tg.set_offset(1, 0.5)\n\tg.set_color(1, b)\n\tg.add_point(1.0, c)\n\tvar t := GradientTexture1D.new()\n\tt.gradient = g\n\treturn t\n\n\nfunc _process(_delta: float) -> void:\n\tvar now := Time.get_ticks_msec() / 1000.0\n\tfor i in _decals.size():\n\t\tif _decals[i].visible and now >= _decal_expiry[i]:\n\t\t\t_decals[i].visible = false\n\n\n## surface: \"concrete\" | \"metal\" | \"flesh\" | \"water\" | \"dirt\"\nfunc impact(pos: Vector3, normal: Vector3, surface := \"concrete\") -> void:\n\tvar n := normal.normalized()\n\tif surface == \"water\":\n\t\tvar sp := _splashes[_splash_next]\n\t\t_splash_next = (_splash_next + 1) % _splashes.size()\n\t\tsp.global_position = pos\n\t\tsp.restart()\n\t\treturn\n\n\tvar d := _decals[_decal_next]\n\t_decal_next = (_decal_next + 1) % _decals.size()\n\td.visible = true\n\td.global_transform = Transform3D(FX.decal_basis(n), pos + n * 0.03)\n\tvar scale := 1.0 if surface == \"concrete\" else 0.7\n\td.size = Vector3(0.28, 0.6, 0.28) * scale\n\td.modulate = Color(0.06, 0.05, 0.05) if surface != \"metal\" else Color(0.35, 0.33, 0.3)\n\t_decal_expiry[_decal_next == 0 ? _decals.size() - 1 : _decal_next - 1] = \\\n\t\t\tTime.get_ticks_msec() / 1000.0 + DECAL_LIFE\n\n\tif surface == \"flesh\":\n\t\treturn\n\tvar p := _sparks[_spark_next]\n\t_spark_next = (_spark_next + 1) % _sparks.size()\n\tp.global_position = pos + n * 0.02\n\tvar pm := p.process_material as ParticleProcessMaterial\n\tif pm != null:\n\t\tpm.direction = n\n\tp.restart()\n"
}

> There's a bug: `_decal_expiry[_decal_next == 0 ? _decals.size() - 1 : _decal_next - 1]` — GDScript ternary syntax is `a if cond else b`. Let me fix that line properly.

> 
> Wait, setting `top_level = true` on ImpactPool means its own transform is ignored but children still use global transforms normally. Actually `top_level` on the ImpactPool node means ImpactPool's transform is not affected by its parent — children still inherit from ImpactPool. Fine, and it keeps things simple.
>

```json
{
  "path": "scripts/fx/PostFX.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the full-screen post-processing overlay node.",
  "content": "class_name PostFX\nextends ColorRect\n## The final image grade.  Lives on a CanvasLayer above the 3D world.\n##\n## Owns the damage wash and the low-health pulse, both of which decay on their\n## own so gameplay code only has to push events in.\n\nvar damage := 0.0\nvar low_health := 0.0\nvar desaturate := 0.0\nvar vignette := 0.55\nvar contrast := 1.0\n\nconst DAMAGE_DECAY := 2.2\n\nvar _mat: ShaderMaterial\n\n\nfunc _ready() -> void:\n\tset_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)\n\tmouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE\n\tcolor = Color(1, 1, 1, 1)\n\t_mat = ShaderMaterial.new()\n\t_mat.shader = load(\"res://shaders/post_process.gdshader\")\n\tmaterial = _mat\n\tSettings.changed.connect(_push)\n\t_push()\n\n\nfunc _push() -> void:\n\tif _mat == null:\n\t\treturn\n\t_mat.set_shader_parameter(\"grain_enabled\", 1.0 if bool(Settings.getv(\"film_grain\")) else 0.0)\n\t_mat.set_shader_parameter(\"grain_amount\", 0.028)\n\t_mat.set_shader_parameter(\"vignette_amount\", vignette)\n\t_mat.set_shader_parameter(\"contrast_boost\", contrast)\n\n\nfunc _process(delta: float) -> void:\n\tif damage > 0.0:\n\t\tdamage = maxf(0.0, damage - DAMAGE_DECAY * delta)\n\tif _mat == null:\n\t\treturn\n\t_mat.set_shader_parameter(\"damage\", damage)\n\t_mat.set_shader_parameter(\"low_health\", low_health)\n\t_mat.set_shader_parameter(\"desaturate\", desaturate)\n\n\nfunc hit(amount := 1.0) -> void:\n\tdamage = clampf(damage + amount, 0.0, 1.0)\n\n\n## Flash the screen a solid colour (explosions, teleports, deaths).\nfunc flash(color: Color, strength := 0.7, duration := 0.5) -> void:\n\tvar layer := get_parent() as CanvasLayer\n\tif layer == null:\n\t\treturn\n\tvar r := ColorRect.new()\n\tr.color = Color(color.r, color.g, color.b, strength)\n\tr.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)\n\tr.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE\n\tlayer.add_child(r)\n\tvar t := create_tween()\n\tt.tween_property(r, \"color:a\", 0.0, duration)\n\tt.tween_callback(r.queue_free)\n"
}
```

> Position computed in _process:
> ```gdscript
> _t += delta * 0.045
> var a := _t
> var pos := Vector3(sin(a) * 26.0, 6.0 + sin(_t * 0.7) * 0.8, cos(a) * 26.0)
> _cam.global_position = pos
> _cam.look_at(Vector3(0, 7.5, 0) + Vector3(_mouse.x * 1.6, _mouse.y * 0.9, 0), Vector3.UP)
> ```
> Mouse parallax via `_unhandled_input` MouseMotion when visible.

> Also the camera must not be affected by the world's rain visibility AABB... The rain emitter follows the camera: set rain.global_position = cam.global_position + Vector3(0, 10, 0) each frame.

> 
> func open_menu(fade := true) -> void:
> 	await _swap(MENU_SCENE)
> 	var m := _current
> 	m.start_requested.connect(_on_start)
> 	m.quit_requested.connect(_on_quit)
> 	# if we came from a run, fade in
> 	await Transition.from_black(1.2)
> 
> func _on_start(new_game: bool) -> void:
> 	GameManager.set_state(GameManager.State.CINEMATIC)
> 	await Transition.to_black(1.0)
> 	if new_game:
> 		GameManager.start_new_game()
> 	elif not GameManager.load_game():
> 		GameManager.start_new_game()
> 	await _swap(GAME_SCENE, false)
> 	_current.to_menu_requested.connect(...)
> 	_current.run_finished.connect(...)
> 	_current.begin(GameManager.chapter)
> 	await Transition.from_black(1.4)
> ```
> 
> _swap with `fade` bool to avoid double-fading.
> 
> ```gdscript
> func _swap(path: String) -> void:
> 	if _current != null:
> 		remove_child(_current)
> 		_current.queue_free()
> 		_current = null
> 	var script = load(path)
> 	var n = script.new()
> 	add_child(n)
> 	_current = n
> 	await get_tree().process_frame
> ```
> 
> Note `load(path).new()` returns a Node.
> 
> Quit: `get_tree().quit()`.
> 
> Also handle `NOTIFICATION_WM_CLOSE_REQUEST` → save settings.
> 
> Let me write.
> 
> Also for run_finished → show credits then back to menu. I'll handle credits inside GameScene; Main just returns to menu.
> 
> Let me write MenuScene now.
> 
> One important consideration: MenuScene and GameScene both need to set `Input.mouse_mode`. MenuScene sets VISIBLE.
> 
> Let me write.
> 
> For the skyline I'll write a helper `_skyline()`.
> 
> Materials for buildings: concrete_dark, plaster, brick, metal_rust. Emissive window strips: thin emissive boxes on building faces.

{
  "path": "scripts/Main.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the boot script that routes between the menu and the campaign.",
  "content": "extends Node\n## Boot and top-level routing.\n##\n## Owns exactly one scene at a time: either the main menu or a run of the\n## campaign.  Swapping always happens behind a fade so there is never a frame\n## where two environments are competing for the same viewport.\n\nconst MENU_SCRIPT := \"res://scripts/menu/MenuScene.gd\"\nconst GAME_SCRIPT := \"res://scripts/game/GameScene.gd\"\n\nvar _current: Node = null\n\n\nfunc _ready() -> void:\n\trandomize()\n\tTransition.set_black(true)\n\tSettings.apply_viewport()\n\tSettings.apply_audio()\n\tawait get_tree().process_frame\n\tif GameManager.is_first_run() and not FileAccess.file_exists(Settings.PATH):\n\t\tSettings.save_settings()\n\tawait open_menu()\n\tawait Transition.from_black(1.6)\n\n\nfunc open_menu() -> void:\n\tawait _swap(MENU_SCRIPT)\n\tvar menu := _current as Node3D\n\tif menu == null:\n\t\treturn\n\tmenu.start_requested.connect(_on_start_requested)\n\tmenu.quit_requested.connect(_on_quit)\n\tGameManager.set_state(GameManager.State.MENU)\n\n\nfunc _on_start_requested(new_game: bool) -> void:\n\tGameManager.set_state(GameManager.State.CINEMATIC)\n\tawait Transition.to_black(1.1)\n\tif new_game:\n\t\tGameManager.start_new_game()\n\telif not GameManager.load_game():\n\t\tGameManager.start_new_game()\n\n\tawait _swap(GAME_SCRIPT)\n\tvar game := _current\n\tif game == null:\n\t\treturn\n\tgame.to_menu_requested.connect(_on_to_menu)\n\tgame.run_finished.connect(_on_run_finished)\n\tgame.begin(GameManager.chapter)\n\tawait Transition.from_black(1.6)\n\n\nfunc _on_to_menu() -> void:\n\tAudioDirector.stop_all()\n\tGameManager.set_state(GameManager.State.MENU)\n\tawait open_menu()\n\tawait Transition.from_black(1.2)\n\n\nfunc _on_run_finished() -> void:\n\tAudioDirector.stop_all()\n\tGameManager.delete_save()\n\tGameManager.set_state(GameManager.State.MENU)\n\tawait open_menu()\n\tawait Transition.from_black(1.8)\n\n\nfunc _on_quit() -> void:\n\tSettings.save_settings()\n\tAudioDirector.stop_all()\n\tawait Transition.to_black(0.7)\n\tget_tree().quit()\n\n\nfunc _swap(script_path: String) -> void:\n\tif _current != null:\n\t\tremove_child(_current)\n\t\t_current.queue_free()\n\t\t_current = null\n\t\tAudioDirector.music(\"\", 0.5)\n\t\tAudioDirector.stop_ambience(0.5)\n\t\tawait get_tree().process_frame\n\t_current = load(script_path).new()\n\tadd_child(_current)\n\tawait get_tree().process_frame\n\n\nfunc _notification(what: int) -> void:\n\tif what == NOTIFICATION_WM_CLOSE_REQUEST:\n\t\tSettings.save_settings()\n\t\tGameManager.save_game()\n"
}

> _Reasoning_
> Good — only one error: `FRESNEL` is not a built-in in Godot 4 spatial fragment shaders (my memory was wrong). Let me replace with a computed fresnel using available built-ins.
> 
> In Godot 4 spatial fragment shader, what's available?
> - `VIEW` — vec3, "Vector from fragment position to camera (in view space)"? In Godot 4 docs, `VIEW` is defined in fragment as "Vector from the fragment to the camera, in view space." Hmm.
> - `NORMAL` in fragment — Godot 4 docs say NORMAL is in *world* space? Let me think about Godot 4's shader built-ins for fragment:
> 
> From the Godot 4 spatial shader reference (fragment):
> ```
> in vec3 VIEW  // Normalized vector from fragment position to camera (in view space)
> inout vec3 NORMAL // Normal vector, in world space... 
> ```
> Hmm, I recall Godot 4 changed NORMAL to be in world space in the fragment stage and view space in the vertex stage. Actually the docs note: "NORMAL: in the fragment function, this is in world space" — I'm not certain.
> 
> Safer approach: compute fresnel in the vertex shader where things are more standard? Or use a built-in that definitely exists: `RIM` (float, output) — `RIM` is a write-only built-in in the fragment shader ("Rim lighting"... ) Actually `RIM` in Godot 4 fragment is an output: "Emissive rim light, based on the light's angle". Hmm, that's for light() function.
> 
> Simplest and definitely safe: derive the fresnel from the view direction using `VIEW` and `NORMAL` in whatever space, but instead of relying on their space, compute it geometrically as `abs(dot(normalize(VIEW), ...))` — still needs matching spaces.
> 
> Alternative approach that avoids the issue entirely: compute fresnel in the VERTEX shader as a varying. In the vertex shader, `NORMAL` is in model (local) space and `VIEW`... In Godot 4 vertex: `in vec3 VERTEX` (model space), `in vec3 NORMAL` (model space). We can compute view-space position via `MODELVIEW_MATRIX`. 
> 
> So in vertex:
> ```glsl
> varying float fres;
> void vertex() {
>     vec4 view_pos = MODELVIEW_MATRIX * vec4(VERTEX, 1.0);
>     vec3 n_view = normalize(NORMAL_MATRIX * NORMAL);  // NORMAL_MATRIX available in vertex? 
>     fres = 1.0 - clamp(dot(n_view, normalize(-view_pos.xyz)), 0.0, 1.0);
> }
> ```
> `NORMAL_MATRIX` is available in the vertex shader in Godot 4 (it's listed as a vertex built-in: `in mat3 NORMAL_MATRIX`). Hmm, is it? Godot 4 vertex built-ins include MODEL_MATRIX, MODELVIEW_MATRIX, VIEW_MATRIX, PROJECTION_MATRIX, MODELVIEW_PROJECTION_MATRIX, NORMAL_MATRIX, INSTANCE_ID, etc. Yes, `NORMAL_MATRIX` exists in vertex (added in 4.0). 
> 
> That's clean and uses only standard, well-documented built-ins. Let me do that: compute `fres` in the vertex function and use it in fragment via a `varying`.
> 
> Actually even simpler and 100% guaranteed: use `UV`-based approximation? No, fresnel genuinely improves the water.
> 
> Let me use the vertex approach. Risk: NORMAL_MATRIX availability. Alternative without NORMAL_MATRIX: use world-space normal via MODEL_MATRIX (but normals need the inverse-transpose for non-uniform scale — our water plane has scale (420,1,420) which is non-uniform! So NORMAL_MATRIX handles that correctly; MODEL_MATRIX would give wrong normals. But for a plane, the normal is +Y in local space, and scaling Y doesn't rotate it... For an axis-aligned scale, the normal direction stays the same for a face-aligned normal (it just gets scaled in magnitude). So `normalize((MODEL_MATRIX * vec4(NORMAL, 0.0)).xyz)` gives +Y correctly here. And camera position in world = `INV_VIEW_MATRIX[3].xyz`.
> 
> Let me do:
> ```glsl
> varying float fres;
> void vertex() {
>     vec4 world = MODEL_MATRIX * vec4(VERTEX, 1.0);
>     vec3 n = normalize((MODEL_MATRIX * vec4(NORMAL, 0.0)).xyz);
>     vec3 v = normalize(CAMERA_POSITION_WORLD - world.xyz);
>     fres = pow(1.0 - clamp(dot(n, v), 0.0, 1.0), 4.0);
>     ...displacement...
> }
> ```
> `CAMERA_POSITION_WORLD` is available in the vertex shader in Godot 4. Yes (`in vec3 CAMERA_POSITION_WORLD` — actually it's `CAMERA_POSITION_WORLD` listed under global/vertex built-ins). I'm fairly confident. Hmm. In Godot 4 the vertex built-in is `CAMERA_POSITION_WORLD`. Yes, that exists (along with CAMERA_DIRECTION_WORLD, CAMERA_POSITION_WORLD).
>

The plane is a unit plane scaled by 420, so local X ranges -0.5..0.5. So `sin(VERTEX.x * 0.07)` barely varies. I should use UV instead for the displacement: `sin(UV.x * 60.0 + TIME)`.

```json
{
  "path": "shaders/water.gdshader",
  "instructions": "Fix the water shader to compute its own Fresnel term and displace on UV.",
  "content": "shader_type spatial;\nrender_mode blend_mix, depth_draw_opaque, cull_back, diffuse_burley, specular_schlick_ggx;\n\n// Flood water.  Two counter-scrolling layers of a procedural normal map give\n// the surface motion; a Fresnel term (computed in the vertex stage, since\n// Godot has no FRESNEL built-in) mixes deep/shallow/horizon colour so the\n// water reads as reflective at grazing angles.  Pairs with the screen-space\n// reflections enabled in the Environment.\n\nuniform sampler2D ripple_normal : hint_normal, filter_linear_mipmap, repeat_enable;\n\nuniform vec4 deep_color : source_color = vec4(0.010, 0.028, 0.038, 1.0);\nuniform vec4 shallow_color : source_color = vec4(0.055, 0.105, 0.125, 1.0);\nuniform vec4 horizon_color : source_color = vec4(0.150, 0.205, 0.240, 1.0);\n\nuniform float normal_strength : hint_range(0.0, 2.0) = 0.9;\nuniform float scroll_speed : hint_range(0.0, 0.2) = 0.022;\nuniform float uv_scale : hint_range(0.5, 60.0) = 7.0;\nuniform float wave_height : hint_range(0.0, 0.6) = 0.05;\nuniform float wave_frequency : hint_range(1.0, 200.0) = 44.0;\nuniform float roughness_min : hint_range(0.0, 0.4) = 0.05;\nuniform float metallic_amount : hint_range(0.0, 1.0) = 0.35;\nuniform float emission_amount : hint_range(0.0, 1.0) = 0.0;\n\nvarying float fres;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n\t// Ripple displacement, driven by UV so it is resolution-independent.\n\tfloat w = sin(UV.x * wave_frequency + TIME * 0.75)\n\t\t\t* cos(UV.y * wave_frequency * 1.27 - TIME * 0.52);\n\tVERTEX.y += w * wave_height;\n\n\t// Fresnel from world-space geometry.\n\tvec4 world = MODEL_MATRIX * vec4(VERTEX, 1.0);\n\tvec3 n = normalize((MODEL_MATRIX * vec4(NORMAL, 0.0)).xyz);\n\tvec3 v = normalize(CAMERA_POSITION_WORLD - world.xyz);\n\tfres = pow(1.0 - clamp(dot(n, v), 0.0, 1.0), 4.0);\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n\tvec2 uv = UV * uv_scale;\n\tvec3 n1 = texture(ripple_normal, uv + vec2(TIME * scroll_speed, TIME * scroll_speed * 0.55)).rgb;\n\tvec3 n2 = texture(ripple_normal, uv * 1.70 - vec2(TIME * scroll_speed * 0.65, TIME * scroll_speed)).rgb;\n\tNORMAL_MAP = mix(vec3(0.5, 0.5, 1.0), (n1 + n2) * 0.5, normal_strength);\n\tNORMAL_MAP_DEPTH = 1.0;\n\n\tvec3 water = mix(deep_color.rgb, shallow_color.rgb, 0.30 + 0.70 * (1.0 - fres));\n\twater = mix(water, horizon_color.rgb, fres * 0.60);\n\n\tALBEDO = water;\n\tROUGHNESS = mix(roughness_min, roughness_min + 0.10, fres);\n\tMETALLIC = metallic_amount;\n\tSPECULAR = 0.85;\n\tEMISSION = water * emission_amount;\n}\n"
}

_joint(shoulder, Vector3(0, -UPPER_ARM, 0), \"Elbow%s\" % (\"L\" if side < 0 else \"R\"))\n\t_mesh(elbow, \"capsule\", Vector3(0.088, FOREARM, 0.088) * bulk,\n\t\t\tVector3(0, -FOREARM * 0.5, 0), secondary)\n\t_mesh(elbow, \"sphere\", Vector3(0.10, 0.10, 0.12), Vector3(0, -FOREARM, 0), skin)\n\tif side < 0:\n\t\telbow_l = elbow\n\telse:\n\t\telbow_r = elbow\n\n\nfunc _leg(hip: Node3D, secondary: Material, accent: Material) -> void:\n\t_mesh(hip, \"capsule\", Vector3(0.135, THIGH, 0.135) * bulk, Vector3(0, -THIGH * 0.5, 0), secondary)\n\tvar knee := _joint(hip, Vector3(0, -THIGH, 0), \"Knee%s\" % hip.name.substr(3))\n\t_mesh(knee, \"capsule\", Vector3(0.115, SHIN, 0.115) * bulk, Vector3(0, -SHIN * 0.5, 0), secondary)\n\t_mesh(knee, \"box\", Vector3(0.14, 0.09, 0.26), Vector3(0, -SHIN - 0.02, -0.05), accent)\n\tif hip.name == \"HipL\":\n\t\tknee_l = knee\n\telse:\n\t\tknee_r = knee\n\n\nfunc _mesh(parent: Node3D, kind: String, size: Vector3, offset: Vector3,\n\t\tmat: Material) -> MeshInstance3D:\n\tvar mi := MeshInstance3D.new()\n\tmatch kind:\n\t\t\"capsule\":\n\t\t\tmi.mesh = unit_capsule()\n\t\t\"sphere\":\n\t\t\tmi.mesh = unit_sphere()\n\t\t_:\n\t\t\tmi.mesh = unit_box()\n\tmi.transform = Transform3D(Basis.from_scale(size), offset)\n\tif mat != null:\n\t\tmi.material_override = mat\n\tmi.material_overlay = _overlay\n\tmi.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_ON\n\tparent.add_child(mi)\n\t_meshes.append(mi)\n\treturn mi\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ posing\n\nfunc update(delta: float, p: Dictionary) -> void:\n\tif _dead:\n\t\t_dead_t = minf(_dead_t + delta * 2.4, 1.0)\n\t\t_pose_dead()\n\t\treturn\n\n\tvar speed := float(p.get(\"speed\", 0.0))\n\tvar max_speed := maxf(float(p.get(\"max_speed\", 4.0)), 0.5)\n\tvar crouch := clampf(float(p.get(\"crouch\", 0.0)), 0.0, 1.0)\n\tvar aiming := bool(p.get(\"aiming\", false))\n\tvar aim_pitch := float(p.get(\"aim_pitch\", 0.0))\n\tvar grounded := bool(p.get(\"grounded\", true))\n\tvar strafe := float(p.get(\"strafe\", 0.0))\n\tvar forward_amount := float(p.get(\"forward\", 1.0))\n\n\tvar ratio := clampf(speed / max_speed, 0.0, 1.6)\n\tvar moving := speed > 0.25\n\tif moving:\n\t\t_phase += delta * (2.4 + speed * 1.55)\n\telse:\n\t\t_phase = lerpf(_phase, nearest_idle_phase(), minf(1.0, delta * 6.0))\n\t_breathe += delta\n\n\tvar swing := ratio * 0.80\n\tvar air := 0.0 if grounded else 1.0\n\n\t# --- hips / spine ---------------------------------------------------\n\tvar bob := absf(sin(_phase)) * -0.028 * ratio\n\thip_root.position.y = HIP_H - crouch * 0.34 + bob - air * 0.04\n\tvar lean := ratio * 0.10 * forward_amount + crouch * 0.16\n\tspine.rotation.x = lerpf(spine.rotation.x, -lean, minf(1.0, delta * 8.0))\n\tspine.rotation.y = lerpf(spine.rotation.y, -strafe * 0.12, minf(1.0, delta * 8.0))\n\tspine.rotation.z = sin(_phase) * 0.035 * ratio\n\tspine.rotation.x += sin(_breathe * 1.5) * 0.012 * (1.0 - ratio)\n\n\thip_root.rotation.z = sin(_phase) * 0.03 * ratio\n\thip_root.rotation.y = lerpf(hip_root.rotation.y, strafe * 0.10, minf(1.0, delta * 8.0))\n\n\t# --- legs ------------------------------------------------------------\n\tvar knee_base := crouch * 1.05\n\tvar thigh_l := sin(_phase) * swing\n\tvar thigh_r := -sin(_phase) * swing\n\thip_l.rotation.x = thigh_l - crouch * 0.55 - air * 0.35\n\thip_r.rotation.x = thigh_r - crouch * 0.55 + air * 0.10\n\tknee_l.rotation.x = -(maxf(0.0, -sin(_phase + 0.5)) * swing * 1.35 + knee_base)\n\tknee_r.rotation.x = -(maxf(0.0, sin(_phase + 0.5)) * swing * 1.35 + knee_base)\n\n\t# --- arms ------------------------------------------------------------\n\tvar arm_base := -0.12 - crouch * 0.15\n\tvar swing_arm := swing * 0.55\n\tif aiming:\n\t\tvar pitch := clampf(aim_pitch, -1.1, 1.1)\n\t\tshoulder_r.rotation.x = lerp_angle(shoulder_r.rotation.x, -pitch * 0.85 - 0.35, delta * 16.0)\n\t\tshoulder_r.rotation.z = lerp_angle(shoulder_r.rotation.z, -0.28, delta * 16.0)\n\t\tshoulder_r.rotation.y = lerp_angle(shoulder_r.rotation.y, -0.22, delta * 16.0)\n\t\telbow_r.rotation.x = lerpf(elbow_r.rotation.x, -0.55, delta * 16.0)\n\t\tshoulder_l.rotation.x = lerp_angle(shoulder_l.rotation.x, -pitch * 0.85 - 0.30, delta * 14.0)\n\t\tshoulder_l.rotation.y = lerp_angle(shoulder_l.rotation.y, 0.55, delta * 14.0)\n\t\tshoulder_l.rotation.z = lerp_angle(shoulder_l.rotation.z, 0.42, delta * 14.0)\n\t\telbow_l.rotation.x = lerpf(elbow_l.rotation.x, -1.15, delta * 14.0)\n\t\tneck.rotation.x = lerpf(neck.rotation.x, -pitch * 0.25, delta * 10.0)\n\telse:\n\t\tshoulder_r.rotation.x = lerp_angle(shoulder_r.rotation.x,\n\t\t\t\tsin(_phase) * swing_arm + arm_base, delta * 10.0)\n\t\tshoulder_l.rotation.x = lerp_angle(shoulder_l.rotation.x,\n\t\t\t\t-sin(_phase) * swing_arm + arm_base, delta * 10.0)\n\t\tshoulder_r.rotation.z = lerpf(shoulder_r.rotation.z, -0.10, delta * 10.0)\n\t\tshoulder_l.rotation.z = lerpf(shoulder_l.rotation.z, 0.10, delta * 10.0)\n\t\tshoulder_r.rotation.y = lerpf(shoulder_r.rotation.y, 0.0, delta * 10.0)\n\t\tshoulder_l.rotation.y = lerpf(shoulder_l.rotation.y, 0.0, delta * 10.0)\n\t\telbow_r.rotation.x = lerpf(elbow_r.rotation.x, -0.55 - crouch * 0.25, delta * 10.0)\n\t\telbow_l.rotation.x = lerpf(elbow_l.rotation.x, -0.45 - crouch * 0.25, delta * 10.0)\n\t\tneck.rotation.x = lerpf(neck.rotation.x, -0.04 * ratio, delta * 6.0)\n\t\tneck.rotation.y = lerpf(neck.rotation.y, sin(_breathe * 0.7) * 0.09, delta * 4.0)\n\n\nfunc nearest_idle_phase() -> float:\n\t## Settle the legs to a neutral stance rather than freezing mid-stride.\n\treturn round(_phase / PI) * PI\n\n\nfunc _pose_dead() -> void:\n\tvar t := _dead_t\n\tvar ease := 1.0 - pow(1.0 - t, 3.0)\n\trotation.x = -PI * 0.5 * ease\n\tposition.y = -0.06 * ease\n\thip_root.position.y = HIP_H - 0.10 * ease\n\tspine.rotation.x = -0.25 * ease\n\tspine.rotation.z = 0.18 * ease\n\tneck.rotation.x = 0.45 * ease\n\tshoulder_l.rotation.x = 0.55 * ease\n\tshoulder_r.rotation.x = 0.35 * ease\n\tshoulder_l.rotation.z = 0.7 * ease\n\tshoulder_r.rotation.z = -1.0 * ease\n\telbow_l.rotation.x = -0.4 * ease\n\telbow_r.rotation.x = -0.9 * ease\n\thip_l.rotation.x = -0.45 * ease\n\thip_r.rotation.x = 0.25 * ease\n\tknee_l.rotation.x = -0.85 * ease\n\tknee_r.rotation.x = -0.35 * ease\n\trotation.y = lerpf(rotation.y, _dead_yaw, ease)\n\n\n## Collapse the rig.  `dir_yaw` gives the fall direction variety.\nfunc die(dir_yaw := 0.0) -> void:\n\t_dead = true\n\t_dead_t = 0.0\n\t_dead_yaw = dir_yaw\n\n\nfunc is_dead() -> bool:\n\treturn _dead\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ effects\n\nfunc flash(strength := 0.8) -> void:\n\t_flash = maxf(_flash, strength)\n\n\nfunc tick_flash(delta: float) -> void:\n\tif _flash <= 0.0:\n\t\treturn\n\t_flash = maxf(0.0, _flash - delta * 4.2)\n\tif _overlay != null:\n\t\t_overlay.albedo_color = Color(1.0, 0.93, 0.86, _flash * 0.85)\n\n\nfunc set_visible_body(v: bool) -> void:\n\tvisible = v\n\n\nfunc aim_transform() -> Transform3D:\n\t## World transform the held weapon should follow.\n\treturn muzzle.global_transform if muzzle != null else global_transform\n"
}

{
  "path": "scripts/player/CameraRig.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the third-person orbit camera with collision, shake and recoil.",
  "content": "class_name CameraRig\nextends Camera3D\n## Third-person camera.\n##\n## Positions itself from a target each frame: yaw/pitch from look input, an arm\n## that swings out behind the target and is pulled in by geometry (three rays,\n## so it does not clip corners), a shoulder offset that grows when aiming, and\n## trauma-based shake.  Recoil is a separate spring so firing does not fight\n## the player's own aim.\n\nvar target: Node3D\nvar head_offset := 1.52\nvar yaw := 0.0\nvar pitch := -0.10\n\nvar arm := 3.5\nvar arm_aim := 2.15\nvar shoulder := 0.40\nvar shoulder_aim := 0.78\n\nvar fov_base := 78.0\nvar fov_aim := 54.0\nvar fov_sprint := 85.0\nvar fov_scale_speed := 7.0\n\nvar trauma := 0.0\nvar recoil_pitch := 0.0\nvar recoil_yaw := 0.0\n\nvar pitch_min := -1.15\nvar pitch_max := 0.85\n\nvar _arm_current := 3.5\nvar _fov := 78.0\nvar _shake_t := 0.0\nvar _noise := Vector3.ZERO\nvar _collide := true\n\n\nfunc setup(target_node: Node3D, fov: float) -> void:\n\ttarget = target_node\n\tfov_base = fov\n\t_fov = fov\n\tfov = fov\n\tnear = 0.08\n\tfar = 600.0\n\ttop_level = true\n\t_arm_current = arm\n\n\nfunc look(relative: Vector2) -> void:\n\tyaw -= relative.x\n\tpitch = clampf(pitch - relative.y, pitch_min, pitch_max)\n\n\nfunc add_trauma(amount: float) -> void:\n\ttrauma = clampf(trauma + amount, 0.0, 1.0)\n\n\nfunc add_recoil(pitch_amount: float, yaw_amount: float) -> void:\n\trecoil_pitch += pitch_amount\n\trecoil_yaw += yaw_amount\n\n\nfunc update(delta: float, aiming: bool, sprinting: bool, occupied := false) -> void:\n\t# --- recover spring --------------------------------------------------\n\trecoil_pitch = lerpf(recoil_pitch, 0.0, minf(1.0, delta * 7.0))\n\trecoil_yaw = lerpf(recoil_yaw, 0.0, minf(1.0, delta * 7.0))\n\ttrauma = maxf(0.0, trauma - delta * 1.35)\n\n\tvar desired_arm := arm_aim if aiming else arm\n\tif occupied:\n\t\tdesired_arm = 0.02\n\tvar target_arm := desired_arm\n\tvar desired_shoulder := shoulder_aim if aiming else shoulder\n\tvar shake_amount := trauma * trauma * float(Settings.getv(\"camera_shake\"))\n\n\t_shake_t += delta * 22.0\n\t_noise = Vector3(\n\t\t\t_wobble(_shake_t, 0.0),\n\t\t\t_wobble(_shake_t, 13.7),\n\t\t\t_wobble(_shake_t, 29.1))\n\n\tvar basis := Basis.from_euler(Vector3(pitch + recoil_pitch, yaw + recoil_yaw, 0.0))\n\tvar origin := Vector3.ZERO\n\tif target != null:\n\t\torigin = target.global_position + Vector3(0, head_offset, 0)\n\tvar pivot := origin + basis.x * desired_shoulder\n\tvar back := basis.z\n\n\t# --- pull the arm in when geometry is in the way ---------------------\n\tif _collide and not occupied:\n\t\tvar worst := target_arm\n\t\tfor side in [-0.42, 0.0, 0.42]:\n\t\t\tvar from := pivot + basis.x * side\n\t\t\tvar to := from + back * target_arm\n\t\t\tvar q := PhysicsRayQueryParameters3D.create(from, to)\n\t\t\tq.collision_mask = 1\n\t\t\tif target is CollisionObject3D:\n\t\t\t\tq.exclude = [(target as CollisionObject3D).get_rid()]\n\t\t\tvar hit := get_world_3d().direct_space_state.intersect_ray(q)\n\t\t\tif hit.is_empty():\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\tvar dist: float = from.distance_to(hit.position) - 0.22\n\t\t\tworst = minf(worst, maxf(dist, 0.35))\n\t\ttarget_arm = worst\n\n\tvar rate := 26.0 if target_arm < _arm_current else 5.0\n\t_arm_current = lerpf(_arm_current, target_arm, minf(1.0, delta * rate))\n\n\tvar final_pos := pivot + back * _arm_current\n\tfinal_pos += basis.x * _noise.x * shake_amount * 0.22\n\tfinal_pos += basis.y * _noise.y * shake_amount * 0.22\n\tfinal_pos += back * _noise.z * shake_amount * 0.14\n\n\tvar shaken := Basis.from_euler(Vector3(\n\t\t\tpitch + recoil_pitch + _noise.y * shake_amount * 0.045,\n\t\t\tyaw + recoil_yaw + _noise.x * shake_amount * 0.045,\n\t\t\t_noise.z * shake_amount * 0.05))\n\tglobal_transform = Transform3D(shaken, final_pos)\n\n\t# --- fov -------------------------------------------------------------\n\tvar want_fov := fov_aim if aiming else (fov_sprint if sprinting else fov_base)\n\t_fov = lerpf(_fov, want_fov, minf(1.0, delta * fov_scale_speed))\n\tfov = _fov\n\n\nfunc _wobble(t: float, seed_v: float) -> float:\n\t## Cheap smooth noise in [-1, 1].\n\treturn sin(t * 1.7 + seed_v) * 0.6 + sin(t * 3.9 + seed_v * 2.3) * 0.4\n\n\nfunc forward() -> Vector3:\n\treturn -global_transform.basis.z\n\n\nfunc aim_point(distance := 200.0) -> Vector3:\n\treturn global_position + forward() * distance\n\n\nfunc set_collision(enabled: bool) -> void:\n\t_collide = enabled\n"
}

> For hiding in a locker: 
> ```gdscript
> func enter_hidden(locker) -> void:
> 	_hidden = locker
> 	velocity = Vector3.ZERO
> 	global_position = locker.eye_position.global_position
> 	rig.set_visible_body(false)
> 	cam_rig.set_collision(false)
> 	hidden_changed.emit(true)
> ```
> and camera yaw set to face outward. In `_physics_process`, if `_hidden`, skip movement and set `cam_rig.update(delta, false, false, true)` with `occupied = true` → arm 0.02 → first-person. And the camera position = player position + head offset, which is inside the locker. 
> 
> Exit: `_hidden = null; rig visible; cam_rig collision on; hidden_changed.emit(false)`.
> 
> Enemies check `player.is_hidden()`.

[get_rid()]\n\tvar hit := get_world_3d().direct_space_state.intersect_ray(q)\n\tvar hit_something := not hit.is_empty()\n\tif hit_something:\n\t\tend = hit.position\n\t\t_resolve_hit(hit, dir)\n\n\tvar muzzle_pos := rig.muzzle.global_position\n\tif game != null and game.tracers != null:\n\t\tgame.tracers.fire(muzzle_pos, end, 0.024)\n\n\t# --- presentation ----------------------------------------------------\n\tAudioDirector.play_variant(\"rifle_fire\", 3, -3.0, 0.05)\n\tAudioDirector.play(\"shell_drop\", -14.0, 1.0, 0.15)\n\tnoised.emit(global_position, 55.0)\n\n\t_muzzle_light.light_energy = 9.0\n\t_muzzle_quad_mat.albedo_color.a = 0.95\n\t_muzzle_quad.rotation.z = randf_range(0.0, TAU)\n\n\tcam_rig.add_recoil(deg_to_rad(randf_range(0.55, 0.95)), deg_to_rad(randf_range(-0.34, 0.34)))\n\tcam_rig.add_trauma(0.12)\n\t_spread_heat = minf(_spread_heat + 0.42, 3.0)\n\tif _spread_heat > 2.2:\n\t\tGameManager.add_stat(\"shots_hit\", 0)  # keep the field defined\n\n\nfunc _current_spread() -> float:\n\tvar base := deg_to_rad(0.75)\n\tif aiming:\n\t\tbase = deg_to_rad(0.22)\n\telif crouching:\n\t\tbase = deg_to_rad(0.45)\n\tvar moving := clampf(Vector2(velocity.x, velocity.z).length() / SPRINT_SPEED, 0.0, 1.0)\n\tbase += deg_to_rad(2.1) * moving\n\tif not is_on_floor():\n\t\tbase += deg_to_rad(2.6)\n\tbase += deg_to_rad(1.5) * (_spread_heat / 3.0)\n\tif health < MAX_HEALTH * 0.28:\n\t\tbase += deg_to_rad(0.9)\n\treturn base\n\n\nfunc _resolve_hit(hit: Dictionary, dir: Vector3) -> void:\n\tvar collider = hit.get(\"collider\")\n\tvar point: Vector3 = hit.get(\"position\", Vector3.ZERO)\n\tvar normal: Vector3 = hit.get(\"normal\", Vector3.UP)\n\n\tif collider is Enemy:\n\t\tvar enemy := collider as Enemy\n\t\tvar is_head := false\n\t\tif enemy.head_area != null:\n\t\t\tis_head = (hit.get(\"collider\") == enemy.head_area)\n\t\tvar dmg := HEAD_DAMAGE if is_head else BODY_DAMAGE\n\t\tenemy.take_damage(dmg, point, dir)\n\t\tGameManager.add_stat(\"shots_hit\", 1)\n\t\tAudioDirector.play(\"hit_crit\" if is_head else \"hit_marker\", -6.0, 1.0, 0.05, \"UI\")\n\t\tif game != null and game.impacts != null:\n\t\t\tgame.impacts.impact(point, normal, \"flesh\")\n\t\treturn\n\n\tif collider is Node and (collider as Node).has_method(\"take_damage\"):\n\t\t(collider as Node).call(\"take_damage\", BODY_DAMAGE * 0.5, point, dir)\n\n\tvar surface := \"concrete\"\n\tif collider is Node3D and (collider as Node3D).is_in_group(\"metal\"):\n\t\tsurface = \"metal\"\n\telif point.y < 0.2 and game != null and game.surface_at(point) == \"water\":\n\t\tsurface = \"water\"\n\tif game != null and game.impacts != null:\n\t\tgame.impacts.impact(point, normal, surface)\n\tAudioDirector.play_variant_at(\"impact_\" + surface, 3 if surface == \"concrete\" else 2,\n\t\t\tpoint, -3.0, 55.0)\n\n\nfunc _melee() -> void:\n\t_melee_cd = 0.75\n\tvar origin := cam_rig.global_position\n\tvar dir := cam_rig.forward()\n\tvar q := PhysicsRayQueryParameters3D.create(origin, origin + dir * MELEE_RANGE)\n\tq.collision_mask = 1 | 4 | 8\n\tq.exclude = [get_rid()]\n\tvar hit := get_world_3d().direct_space_state.intersect_ray(q)\n\tAudioDirector.play(\"whoosh\", -6.0, 1.0, 0.1)\n\tnoised.emit(global_position, 16.0)\n\tif hit.is_empty():\n\t\treturn\n\tvar collider = hit.get(\"collider\")\n\tif collider is Enemy:\n\t\t(collider as Enemy).take_damage(MELEE_DAMAGE, hit.position, dir)\n\t\tcam_rig.add_trauma(0.3)\n\telif collider is Node and (collider as Node).has_method(\"take_damage\"):\n\t\t(collider as Node).call(\"take_damage\", MELEE_DAMAGE * 0.4, hit.position, dir)\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------- interaction\n\nfunc _update_interaction() -> void:\n\tif _hidden_in != null:\n\t\t_focus = _hidden_in\n\t\tfocus_changed.emit(_focus, \"Leave\")\n\t\tif Input.is_action_just_pressed(\"interact\"):\n\t\t\t_exit_hidden()\n\t\treturn\n\n\tvar origin := cam_rig.global_position\n\tvar dir := cam_rig.forward()\n\tvar q := PhysicsRayQueryParameters3D.create(origin, origin + dir * 3.6)\n\tq.collision_mask = 8\n\tq.exclude = [get_rid()]\n\tvar hit := get_world_3d().direct_space_state.intersect_ray(q)\n\tvar found: Node = null\n\tif not hit.is_empty() and hit.get(\"collider\") is Node:\n\t\tvar c := hit.get(\"collider\") as Node\n\t\tif c.has_method(\"interact\"):\n\t\t\tfound = c\n\n\tif found != _focus:\n\t\t_focus = found\n\t\tvar label := \"\"\n\t\tif found != null and found.has_method(\"prompt\"):\n\t\t\tlabel = String(found.call(\"prompt\"))\n\t\tfocus_changed.emit(found, label)\n\n\tif _focus != null and Input.is_action_just_pressed(\"interact\"):\n\t\t_focus.call(\"interact\", self)\n\n\nfunc enter_hidden(holder: Node) -> void:\n\t_hidden_in = holder\n\tvelocity = Vector3.ZERO\n\tif holder is Node3D:\n\t\tglobal_position = (holder as Node3D).global_position\n\trig.set_visible_body(false)\n\tcam_rig.set_collision(false)\n\tif holder.has_method(\"facing_yaw\"):\n\t\tcam_rig.yaw = float(holder.call(\"facing_yaw\"))\n\t\t_body_yaw = cam_rig.yaw\n\thidden_changed.emit(true)\n\tAudioDirector.play(\"locker_close\", -6.0, 1.0, 0.03)\n\n\nfunc _exit_hidden() -> void:\n\t_hidden_in = null\n\trig.set_visible_body(true)\n\tcam_rig.set_collision(true)\n\tglobal_position += Vector3(0, 0.1, 0)\n\thidden_changed.emit(false)\n\tAudioDirector.play(\"locker_open\", -6.0, 1.0, 0.03)\n\n\nfunc is_hidden() -> bool:\n\treturn _hidden_in != null\n\n\nfunc noise_radius() -> float:\n\tif _hidden_in != null:\n\t\treturn 0.0\n\treturn 10.0 + Vector2(velocity.x, velocity.z).length() * 3.6\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ damage\n\nfunc apply_damage(amount: float, from_position: Vector3, silent := false) -> void:\n\tif not alive:\n\t\treturn\n\thealth -= amount\n\tGameManager.add_stat(\"damage_taken\", amount)\n\t_regen_delay = 0.0\n\tvar dir := (from_position - global_position).normalized()\n\tdamaged.emit(dir, amount)\n\tif game != null and game.post != null:\n\t\tgame.post.hit(clampf(amount / 28.0, 0.18, 1.0))\n\tcam_rig.add_trauma(clampf(amount / 70.0, 0.12, 0.6))\n\tif not silent:\n\t\tAudioDirector.play(\"player_hurt\", -7.0, 1.0, 0.06)\n\thealth_changed.emit(health, MAX_HEALTH)\n\tif health <= 0.0:\n\t\t_die()\n\n\nfunc heal(amount: float) -> void:\n\thealth = minf(MAX_HEALTH, health + amount)\n\thealth_changed.emit(health, MAX_HEALTH)\n\tAudioDirector.play(\"heal\", -6.0, 1.0, 0.0, \"UI\")\n\n\nfunc give_ammo(amount: int) -> void:\n\treserve += amount\n\tammo_changed.emit(mag, reserve)\n\n\nfunc _die() -> void:\n\talive = false\n\t_dead_t = 0.0\n\thealth = 0.0\n\trig.die(randf_range(-0.4, 0.4))\n\tAudioDirector.play(\"player_death\", -2.0, 1.0, 0.05)\n\tGameManager.add_stat(\"deaths\", 1)\n\tdamaged.emit(Vector3.ZERO, 0.0)\n\tdied.emit()\n\n\nfunc revive(at: Vector3, yaw: float) -> void:\n\talive = true\n\t_dead_t = 0.0\n\thealth = MAX_HEALTH * 0.7\n\tvelocity = Vector3.ZERO\n\tglobal_position = at\n\t_body_yaw = yaw\n\tcam_rig.yaw = yaw\n\tcam_rig.pitch = -0.08\n\tcam_rig.head_offset = 1.52\n\tcam_rig.trauma = 0.0\n\trig.rotation = Vector3.ZERO\n\trig.position = Vector3.ZERO\n\trig.visible = true\n\t_reloading = false\n\tmag = MAG_SIZE\n\treserve = maxi(reserve, 60)\n\thealth_changed.emit(health, MAX_HEALTH)\n\tammo_changed.emit(mag, reserve)\n"
}
