```
file: scripts/player/Player.gd
message: Created file successfully.
```

> _Reasoning_
> A couple of issues to fix in Player.gd:
> 1. `_resolve_hit`: `is_head` detection via `hit.get("collider") == enemy.head_area` — the ray query returns the *collider* which for an Area3D is the Area3D itself. So checking `collider == enemy.head_area` is right. But I wrote `if enemy.head_area != null: is_head = (hit.get("collider") == enemy.head_area)`. OK that works. But the ray may hit the Area3D before the body; areas and bodies are sorted by distance so the closest wins — fine.
> 
> 2. `GameManager.add_stat("shots_hit", 0)` in the spread heat block — pointless leftover. Let me remove.
> 
> 3. `_update_movement`: the facing logic `if aiming or _forward_amt > 0.6 and dir.length_squared() > 0.01:` — operator precedence: `and` binds tighter than `or`, so this is `aiming or (_forward_amt > 0.6 and dir...)`. That's actually the intent (always face camera when aiming). OK, but let me make it explicit with parens for clarity.
> 
> 4. In `_update_movement`, `basis.inverse() * velocity` — Basis.inverse() then multiply by Vector3 works: `basis.inverse() * velocity`. Fine.
> 
> 5. `rig.rotation.y = _body_yaw` — but the rig is a child of Player, and Player's own rotation stays 0. So the rig rotates locally. Good. But `rig.rotation.y` accumulates; and HumanoidRig has `rotation.x` used for death pose. OK.
> 
> 6. `get_node("CollisionShape3D")` — the CollisionShape3D was added with `add_child(cs)` and no name, so Godot auto-names it "CollisionShape3D". That works but is fragile. Let me store a reference `_shape` instead.
> 
> 7. `_hidden_in` check in `_update_interaction`: when hidden, it emits focus_changed every frame with "Leave" — that's fine but spams. Let me guard so it only emits on change.
> 
> 8. `apply_damage` is used for fall damage with `silent=true`.
> 
> 9. `_die()` calls `damaged.emit(Vector3.ZERO, 0.0)` — hmm, that's to force HUD refresh; fine but let me remove and rely on `died`.
> 
> 10. `Enemy` class referenced before it exists — GDScript resolves global class names, so it'll be fine once Enemy.gd exists with `class_name Enemy`. But if Enemy.gd doesn't exist, Player.gd fails to parse. I must create Enemy.gd. Circular reference between Player and Enemy (both reference each other) — GDScript 4 handles cyclic class references between files? Cyclic dependencies can be problematic. Player references `Enemy` type; Enemy references `Player`. GDScript 4 does support this in many cases but can produce "cyclic reference" errors.
> 
> To be safe, I'll avoid static typing to the other class: in Player, use `var collider = hit.get("collider")` (untyped) and check `if collider is Enemy` — that still needs the Enemy identifier at parse time (a cyclic reference). Hmm.
> 
> Godot 4 GDScript generally does handle cyclic references between scripts now (it resolves lazily). But it has historically thrown "Could not resolve class" errors in cycles. Let me minimize risk: make Player NOT reference `Enemy` at all. Instead, use duck typing: `if collider != null and collider.has_method("take_damage")` and pass damage + hit info. For headshots, Enemies can expose `is_head_hit(area)`.
> 
> So in Player:
> ```gdscript
> if collider is Node and collider.has_method("receive_hit"):
> 	collider.call("receive_hit", point, dir, hit.get("collider") == collider, false)
> ```
> Hmm, let me design a clean interface: enemies implement
> ```gdscript
> func receive_bullet(point: Vector3, dir: Vector3, shape_index: int) -> bool
> ```
> Hmm, `shape_index` from the ray query result: `hit.get("shape")` gives the shape index within the collider. For a CharacterBody3D with 2 shapes, index 0 = body, 1 = head. That avoids needing a head Area3D entirely! Cleaner: the enemy is on layer 4; the ray query returns `shape` = index. I can map index → hitbox kind via a dictionary on the enemy.
> 
> Let me do that: Enemy has `hitboxes = {0: "body", 1: "head"}` and implements `func hit(pos, normal, dir, shape_index) -> void`.
> 
> Player:
> ```gdscript
> if collider is Node and collider.has_method("bullet_hit"):
> 	collider.call("bullet_hit", point, normal, dir, int(hit.get("shape", 0)))
> 	GameManager.add_stat("shots_hit", 1)
> ```
> And who plays the hitmarker sound / decides headshot? The enemy returns nothing; I can have `bullet_hit` return a Dictionary or just have the Player compute damage. Let me have the enemy expose `func damage_for(shape_index) -> float` and `func is_head_shape(i) -> bool`. Simpler: Player passes a base damage and the enemy scales it.
> 
> Design:
> ```gdscript
> # Enemy side
> func bullet_hit(point: Vector3, normal: Vector3, dir: Vector3, shape_index: int) -> void:
> 	var head := shape_index == HEAD_SHAPE
> 	take_damage(HEAD_DAMAGE if head else BODY_DAMAGE, point, dir)
> 	# emits hit_feedback signal
> ```
> But then Player doesn't know if it was a headshot for the UI sound. Let the enemy emit a signal the GameScene connects to, or the Player can query `enemy.last_hit_was_head`. Let me have `bullet_hit` return a bool (was head).
> 
> ```gdscript
> var was_head: bool = collider.call("bullet_hit", point, normal, dir)
> ```
> `call()` returns Variant → assign to bool with explicit cast. Fine.
> 
> This removes the type dependency entirely. Player uses `has_method("bullet_hit")`. 
> 
> Also `Player._resolve_hit` calls `take_damage` on generic nodes — remove that.
> 
> Let me also handle: melee on Enemy via `bullet_hit`.
> 
> Let me now patch Player.gd accordingly, and also fix the other issues.
> 
> Let me make the edits:
> - Replace `_resolve_hit` with duck-typed version.
> - `_melee` duck-typed.
> - Remove the `GameManager.add_stat("shots_hit", 0)` leftover.
> - Store `_capsule` shape ref instead of get_node.
> - Guard focus emit when hidden.
> 
> Let me apply with str_replace (multiple replacements).
> 
> Also `Enemy` won't be referenced. Good.
> 
> Let me do the edits.

> _Reasoning_
> Now Enemy.gd. Let me design it with duck-typed interface:
> 
> ```gdscript
> class_name Enemy extends CharacterBody3D
> 
> signal died(enemy)
> signal alerted(enemy, position)
> signal shot_fired(enemy, from, to)
> 
> const HEAD_SHAPE := 1
> 
> var kind := "trooper"
> var game: Node = null
> var rig: HumanoidRig
> var player: Node3D = null
> 
> var health := 100.0
> var max_health := 100.0
> var alive := true
> var alert_level := 0.0   # 0 calm, 1 suspicious, 2 combat
> var state := State.PATROL
> var patrol_points: Array[Vector3] = []
> var _patrol_i := 0
> var last_known := Vector3.ZERO

For a CollisionObject3D with shapes added as CollisionShape3D children, the shape index corresponds to the order of the CollisionShape3D children. So index 0 = first child, 1 = second. Good.

> 
> Actually a one-sided collision can cause the player to be pushed/trapped. Let me set enemy mask = 1 | 4 (world + other enemies) and NOT include the player. Player mask = 1 (world) only — simpler and avoids weirdness; enemies are damage dealers, not obstacles. But then the player can walk through enemies, which feels cheap. Compromise: player mask includes enemies (bit 4). Enemies mask = 1 | 4. So enemies avoid each other and walls; the player is blocked by enemies. That's the common setup. Good.

> Enemy AI:
> - States: IDLE, PATROL, ALERT, COMBAT, SEARCH, DEAD
> - Vision: 
>   - distance < view_range (28 m) 
>   - angle between forward and to-player < fov (60° half-angle → 120° cone)
>   - LOS raycast clear (mask 1; exclude self; also if it hits the player → visible)
>   - modifiers: player crouching → range × 0.6; player sprinting → range × 1.2; if the player is hidden → never visible. Also "player in darkness" — chapters can set `_ambient_light` — skip.
> - Hearing: the game forwards `noised(pos, radius)` events to nearby enemies.
> - On spotting: `alert_level = 1` → after `reaction_time` → COMBAT, shout, notify allies within 30 m.
> - COMBAT: move toward the player until within preferred range (12 m), then strafe/hold; fire in bursts with accuracy falloff; take cover? Keep simple: advance, strafe, fire.
> - SEARCH: go to last_known, look around for 8 s, then return to patrol.
> - Taking damage: flinch, become alerted to the shooter's position (already handled by the player's noise), brief stagger.
> 
> Movement: steering with obstacle avoidance:
> ```gdscript
> var desired_dir = (target - pos).normalized()
> # avoid walls: 3 whiskers
> for angle in [-30, 0, 30]:
> 	var d = desired_dir.rotated(UP, deg_to_rad(angle))
> 	var hit = raycast(pos + Vector3(0,1,0), pos + Vector3(0,1,0) + d*1.8)
> 	if hit: weight avoidance
> ```
> Then `velocity.x/z = move_toward(...)`.
> 
> Simple approach that works: 
> ```gdscript
> var avoid := Vector3.ZERO
> for a in [-40, -20, 0, 20, 40]:
> 	var d := dir.rotated(Vector3.UP, deg_to_rad(a))
> 	if _probe(d, 1.6):  # returns hit distance
> 		avoid += Vector3.UP.cross(d) * ... 
> ```
> Let me implement a simple "whisker" avoidance: for the 5 directions, compute the free distance; choose the direction with the best free distance weighted by alignment with the desired direction. That's a mini context-steering behavior — robust and easy.
> 
> ```gdscript
> func _steer(desired: Vector3) -> Vector3:
> 	var best := desired
> 	var best_score := -INF
> 	for a in range(-60, 61, 15):
> 		var d := desired.rotated(Vector3.UP, deg_to_rad(float(a)))
> 		var free := _free_distance(d, 2.2)
> 		var score := free + d.dot(desired) * 1.4
> 		if score > best_score:
> 			best_score = score
> 			best = d
> 	return best
> ```
> `_free_distance(d, max)`: raycast from self+ (0,0.9,0) to +d*max, mask 1; if hit → distance else max.
> 
> Good.

45.0\n\n\nfunc _perceive(delta: float) -> void:\n\t# decay awareness when nothing is confirmed\n\tvar p := player()\n\tvar sees := _can_see_player()\n\tif sees:\n\t\talert = 1.0\n\t\tlast_known = p.global_position\n\t\t_seen_t = 0.0\n\telse:\n\t\t_seen_t += delta\n\t\tif _seen_t > 3.5:\n\t\t\talert = maxf(0.0, alert - delta * 0.28)\n\n\tmatch state:\n\t\tState.IDLE, State.PATROL:\n\t\t\tif sees:\n\t\t\t\t_set_state(State.ALERT)\n\t\t\telif alert > 0.35 and _seen_t > 0.6:\n\t\t\t\t_set_state(State.SEARCH)\n\t\tState.ALERT:\n\t\t\t_reaction -= delta\n\t\t\tif sees and _reaction <= 0.0:\n\t\t\t\t_set_state(State.COMBAT)\n\t\t\telif not sees and _seen_t > 4.0:\n\t\t\t\t_set_state(State.SEARCH)\n\t\tState.COMBAT:\n\t\t\tif not sees and _seen_t > 5.0:\n\t\t\t\t_set_state(State.SEARCH)\n\t\tState.SEARCH:\n\t\t\t_search_t += delta\n\t\t\tif sees:\n\t\t\t\t_set_state(State.COMBAT)\n\t\t\telif _search_t > 11.0:\n\t\t\t\talert = 0.0\n\t\t\t\t_set_state(State.PATROL if patrol_points.size() > 0 else State.IDLE)\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------- states\n\nfunc _do_idle(_delta: float) -> float:\n\t_brake()\n\treturn 0.0\n\n\nfunc _do_patrol(delta: float) -> float:\n\tif patrol_points.size() == 0:\n\t\treturn 0.0\n\tif _wait > 0.0:\n\t\t_wait -= delta\n\t\t_brake()\n\t\treturn 0.0\n\tvar target := patrol_points[_patrol_i]\n\tif global_position.distance_to(target) < 1.4:\n\t\t_patrol_i = (_patrol_i + 1) % patrol_points.size()\n\t\t_wait = randf_range(1.4, 3.6)\n\t\t_yaw = randf_range(0.0, TAU)\n\t\treturn 0.0\n\treturn _move_towards(target, 2.4, delta)\n\n\nfunc _do_alert(delta: float) -> float:\n\t# turn towards the disturbance, weapon up, no advance yet\n\tvar to := last_known - global_position\n\t_yaw = atan2(-to.x, -to.z)\n\t_brake()\n\treturn 0.0\n\n\nfunc _do_search(delta: float) -> float:\n\tvar dist := global_position.distance_to(last_known)\n\tif dist > 2.0:\n\t\treturn _move_towards(last_known, 4.0, delta)\n\t# sweep the area\n\t_yaw = _yaw + sin(_search_t * 1.1) * delta * 1.4\n\tif _search_t > 3.0 and randf() < delta * 0.4:\n\t\tlast_known = _spawn + Vector3(randf_range(-8, 8), 0, randf_range(-8, 8))\n\t_search_t += delta\n\t_brake()\n\treturn 0.0\n\n\nfunc _do_combat(delta: float) -> float:\n\tvar p := player()\n\tif p == null:\n\t\treturn 0.0\n\tvar to := p.global_position - global_position\n\tvar dist := to.length()\n\tvar sees := _can_see_player()\n\n\t# hold a firing lane\n\tif dist > preferred_max:\n\t\treturn _move_towards(p.global_position, 4.6, delta)\n\tif dist < preferred_min:\n\t\treturn _move_towards(global_position * 2.0 - p.global_position, 3.2, delta)\n\n\t# strafe and keep the lane\n\tif randf() < delta * 0.5:\n\t\t_strafe_sign *= -1.0\n\tvar sideways := Vector3(to.z, 0, -to.x).normalized() * _strafe_sign\n\tvar lat := _steer(sideways)\n\t_set_horizontal(lat, 2.6, delta)\n\t_last_attacker_pos = p.global_position\n\n\tif sees and _reaction <= 0.0:\n\t\t_shoot(delta, dist)\n\treturn velocity.length()\n\n\nfunc _shoot(delta: float, dist: float) -> void:\n\t_fire_cd -= delta\n\t_pause_cd -= delta\n\tif _pause_cd > 0.0:\n\t\treturn\n\tif _burst_left <= 0:\n\t\t_burst_left = randi_range(burst_min, burst_max)\n\tif _fire_cd > 0.0:\n\t\treturn\n\n\t_fire_cd = shot_interval\n\t_burst_left -= 1\n\tif _burst_left <= 0:\n\t\t_pause_cd = burst_pause * randf_range(0.7, 1.5)\n\n\tvar p := player()\n\tif p == null:\n\t\treturn\n\n\tvar eye := rig.muzzle.global_position\n\tvar aim_point := p.global_position + Vector3(0, randf_range(0.7, 1.5), 0)\n\t# Accuracy falls off with range and improves while the target is tracked.\n\tvar falloff := clampf(1.0 - (dist - 6.0) / 34.0, 0.25, 1.0)\n\tvar spread := deg_to_rad(lerpf(5.5, 0.7, accuracy * falloff))\n\tvar dir := (aim_point - eye).normalized()\n\tdir = dir.rotated(Vector3.UP, randf_range(-spread, spread))\n\tdir = dir.rotated(rig.global_transform.basis.x, randf_range(-spread, spread)).normalized()\n\n\tvar q := PhysicsRayQueryParameters3D.create(eye, eye + dir * 120.0)\n\tq.collision_mask = 1 | 2 | 8\n\tq.exclude = [get_rid()]\n\tvar hit := get_world_3d().direct_space_state.intersect_ray(q)\n\tvar end_pos := eye + dir * 120.0\n\tif not hit.is_empty():\n\t\tend_pos = hit.position\n\t\tif hit.get(\"collider\") == p:\n\t\t\tvar dmg := randf_range(damage_min, damage_max) * falloff\n\t\t\tif p.has_method(\"apply_damage\"):\n\t\t\t\tp.call(\"apply_damage\", dmg, global_position)\n\n\tif game != null and game.tracers != null:\n\t\tgame.tracers.fire(eye, end_pos, 0.018, Color(1.0, 0.45, 0.32))\n\tif game != null and game.impacts != null and not hit.is_empty() and hit.get(\"collider\") != p:\n\t\tgame.impacts.impact(hit.position, hit.get(\"normal\", Vector3.UP), \"concrete\")\n\n\tAudioDirector.play_at(\"rifle_fire_%d\" % randi_range(1, 3), global_position, -6.0, 0.9, 0.06, 90.0)\n\t_muzzle_light.light_energy = 7.0\n\tif game != null and game.has_method(\"alert_enemies\"):\n\t\tgame.alert_enemies(global_position, p.global_position, 34.0)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- steering\n\nfunc _move_towards(target: Vector3, speed: float, delta: float) -> float:\n\tvar to := target - global_position\n\tto.y = 0.0\n\tif to.length() < 0.05:\n\t\t_brake()\n\t\treturn 0.0\n\tvar dir := _steer(to.normalized())\n\t_set_horizontal(dir, speed, delta)\n\treturn velocity.length()\n\n\nfunc _set_horizontal(dir: Vector3, speed: float, delta: float) -> void:\n\tvar slow := 0.55 if _stagger > 0.0 else 1.0\n\tvar desired := dir * speed * slow\n\tvar rate := 12.0\n\tvar k := 1.0 - exp(-rate * delta)\n\tvelocity.x = lerpf(velocity.x, desired.x, k)\n\tvelocity.z = lerpf(velocity.z, desired.z, k)\n\n\nfunc _brake() -> void:\n\tvelocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 18.0 * get_physics_process_delta_time())\n\tvelocity.z = move_toward(velocity.z, 0.0, 18.0 * get_physics_process_delta_time())\n\n\nfunc _steer(desired: Vector3) -> Vector3:\n\tvar best := desired\n\tvar best_score := -1e9\n\tfor a in range(-70, 71, 14):\n\t\tvar d := desired.rotated(Vector3.UP, deg_to_rad(float(a)))\n\t\tvar free := _free_distance(d, 2.4)\n\t\tvar score := free + d.dot(desired) * 1.5\n\t\tif score > best_score:\n\t\t\tbest_score = score\n\t\t\tbest = d\n\treturn best.normalized()\n\n\nfunc _free_distance(dir: Vector3, max_dist: float) -> float:\n\tvar from := global_position + Vector3(0, 0.9, 0)\n\tvar q := PhysicsRayQueryParameters3D.create(from, from + dir * max_dist)\n\tq.collision_mask = 1\n\tq.exclude = [get_rid()]\n\tvar hit := get_world_3d().direct_space_state.intersect_ray(q)\n\tif hit.is_empty():\n\t\treturn max_dist\n\treturn from.distance_to(hit.position)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- death\n\nfunc _die(dir: Vector3) -> void:\n\talive = false\n\tstate = State.DEAD\n\t_dead_t = 0.0\n\tcollision_layer = 0\n\tcollision_mask = 0\n\trig.die(randf_range(-0.5, 0.5))\n\tAudioDirector.play_at(\"enemy_death\", global_position, -4.0, 1.0, 0.08)\n\tAudioDirector.play_at(\"flesh_fall\", global_position, -8.0, 1.0, 0.1)\n\tGameManager.add_stat(\"kills\", 1)\n\tdied.emit(self)\n\tif game != null and game.has_method(\"on_enemy_died\"):\n\t\tgame.on_enemy_died(self)\n\tset_physics_process(true)\n\n\nfunc _update_health_bar() -> void:\n\tif _bar_fill == null:\n\t\treturn\n\tvar ratio := clampf(health / MAX_HEALTH, 0.0, 1.0)\n\t_bar_fill.scale.x = maxf(ratio, 0.001)\n\t_bar_fill.position.x = -(0.78 * (1.0 - ratio)) * 0.5\n\t_bar_vis = maxf(_bar_vis, 0.3)\n\n\nfunc disarm() -> void:\n\t## Used by scripted moments (surrender, cutscene).\n\tset_physics_process(false)\n\tvelocity = Vector3.ZERO\n"
}

0)\n\t_subtitle_root.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_CENTER\n\t_subtitle_root.visible = false\n\tadd_child(_subtitle_root)\n\t_subtitle_speaker = UI.label(\"\", UI.SIZE_SMALL, UI.AMBER, 4.0, 0.3)\n\t_subtitle_speaker.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER\n\t_subtitle_root.add_child(_subtitle_speaker)\n\t_subtitle_text = UI.label(\"\", 20, HUD_FONT_COLOR, 0.4)\n\t_subtitle_text.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER\n\t_subtitle_text.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART\n\t_subtitle_text.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(760, 0)\n\t_subtitle_root.add_child(_subtitle_text)\n\n\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------- toasts\n\t_toast_box = VBoxContainer.new()\n\t_toast_box.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_CENTER_TOP)\n\t_toast_box.position = Vector2(-250, 96)\n\t_toast_box.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(500, 0)\n\t_toast_box.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_CENTER\n\tadd_child(_toast_box)\n\n\t# ----------------------------------------------------------------- alert\n\t_alert_label = UI.label(\"\", UI.SIZE_SMALL, UI.BLOOD, 5.0, 0.35)\n\t_alert_label.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_CENTER_TOP)\n\t_alert_label.position = Vector2(-100, 58)\n\t_alert_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER\n\t_alert_label.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(200, 0)\n\tadd_child(_alert_label)\n\n\t_fps_label = UI.label(\"\", UI.SIZE_SMALL, UI.FAINT)\n\t_fps_label.position = Vector2(0, 0)\n\t_fps_label.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_TOP_RIGHT)\n\t_fps_label.position = Vector2(-190, 40)\n\t_fps_label.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(170, 0)\n\t_fps_label.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT\n\tadd_child(_fps_label)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- binding\n\nfunc bind_player(p: Player) -> void:\n\tplayer = p\n\tp.health_changed.connect(_on_health)\n\tp.ammo_changed.connect(_on_ammo)\n\tp.stamina_changed.connect(_on_stamina)\n\tp.focus_changed.connect(_on_focus)\n\tp.damaged.connect(_on_damaged)\n\tp.hidden_changed.connect(_on_hidden)\n\tp.died.connect(_on_died)\n\t_on_health(p.health, Player.MAX_HEALTH)\n\t_on_ammo(p.mag, p.reserve)\n\trefresh_objectives()\n\n\nfunc refresh_objectives() -> void:\n\tvar info := GameManager.chapter_info(GameManager.chapter)\n\t_objective_title.text = String(info.get(\"title\", \"\")) + \"  //  \" + String(info.get(\"name\", \"\"))\n\tvar active := GameManager.active_objective()\n\tif active.is_empty():\n\t\tvar prog := GameManager.objective_progress()\n\t\t_objective_body.text = \"— all objectives complete —\" if prog.y > 0 else \"\"\n\telse:\n\t\t_objective_body.text = \"▸ \" + active\n\n\nfunc _on_objective_completed(_id: String) -> void:\n\t_hit_flash_objective()\n\trefresh_objectives()\n\n\nfunc _hit_flash_objective() -> void:\n\tif _objective_body == null:\n\t\treturn\n\tvar t := create_tween()\n\t_objective_body.modulate = UI.GREEN\n\tt.tween_property(_objective_body, \"modulate\", Color(1, 1, 1), 1.0)\n\n\n# -------------------------------------------------------------------- events\n\nfunc _on_health(health: float, max_health: float) -> void:\n\tvar ratio := clampf(health / max_health, 0.0, 1.0)\n\t_health_fill.size.x = 328.0 * ratio\n\t_health_glow.size.x = 328.0 * ratio\n\tvar col := Color(\"cfe4ef\")\n\tif ratio < 0.35:\n\t\tcol = UI.BLOOD\n\telif ratio < 0.65:\n\t\tcol = UI.AMBER\n\t_health_fill.color = col\n\t_health_glow.color = Color(col.r, col.g, col.b, 0.55)\n\t_health_label.text = \"%d\" % roundi(health)\n\t_health_label.add_theme_color_override(\"font_color\", col)\n\t_low_health = 0.0 if ratio > 0.35 else (0.35 - ratio) / 0.35\n\tif player != null and player.game != null and player.game.post != null:\n\t\tplayer.game.post.low_health = _low_health\n\n\nfunc _on_ammo(mag: int, reserve: int) -> void:\n\t_ammo_label.text = \"%d\" % mag\n\tif player != null and player._reloading:\n\t\t_ammo_label.text = \"--\"\n\t_reserve_label.text = \"/ %d\" % reserve\n\tvar low := mag <= 5\n\t_ammo_label.add_theme_color_override(\"font_color\", UI.AMBER if low else HUD_FONT_COLOR)\n\n\nfunc _on_stamina(value: float) -> void:\n\t_stamina_fill.size.x = 200.0 * clampf(value, 0.0, 1.0)\n\t_stamina_fill.color = UI.AMBER if value > 0.25 else UI.BLOOD\n\n\nfunc _on_focus(target: Node, label: String) -> void:\n\tvar showing := target != null\n\t_prompt_root.visible = showing\n\tif showing:\n\t\t_prompt_text.text = label.to_upper()\n\t\t_prompt_root.position.x = -110.0 - float(_prompt_text.text.length()) * 4.0\n\n\nfunc _on_damaged(from_direction: Vector3, amount: float) -> void:\n\tif player == null or player.cam_rig == null:\n\t\treturn\n\tif amount <= 0.0:\n\t\treturn\n\tvar local := player.cam_rig.global_transform.basis.inverse() * from_direction\n\t_damage_arcs.append({\"dir\": atan2(local.x, -local.z), \"t\": 1.0, \"amount\": amount})\n\t_hit_t = maxf(_hit_t, 0.0)\n\n\nfunc _on_hidden(hidden: bool) -> void:\n\t_hidden = hidden\n\t_status_label.text = \"CONCEALED — [E] to leave\" if hidden else \"\"\n\t_status_label.add_theme_color_override(\"font_color\", UI.COLD)\n\n\nfunc _on_died() -> void:\n\t_status_label.text = \"\"\n\n\nfunc hitmarker(head := false) -> void:\n\t_hit_t = 0.34\n\t_hit_head = head\n\n\nfunc set_spread(radians: float) -> void:\n\t_spread = radians\n\n\nfunc set_alert(level: float) -> void:\n\t_alert = level\n\tif level > 0.75:\n\t\t_alert_label.text = \"◤ SPOTTED ◢\"\n\t\t_alert_label.add_theme_color_override(\"font_color\", UI.BLOOD)\n\telif level > 0.35:\n\t\t_alert_label.text = \"◤ SEARCHING ◢\"\n\t\t_alert_label.add_theme_color_override(\"font_color\", UI.AMBER)\n\telse:\n\t\t_alert_label.text = \"\"\n\n\nfunc toast(text: String) -> void:\n\tif _toast_box == null:\n\t\treturn\n\tvar l := UI.label(text.to_upper(), 18, UI.TEXT, 2.0, 0.3)\n\tl.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER\n\tl.modulate.a = 0.0\n\t_toast_box.add_child(l)\n\tvar t := create_tween()\n\tt.tween_property(l, \"modulate:a\", 1.0, 0.25)\n\tt.tween_interval(2.6)\n\tt.tween_property(l, \"modulate:a\", 0.0, 0.6)\n\tt.tween_callback(l.queue_free)\n\twhile _toast_box.get_child_count() > 4:\n\t\t_toast_box.get_child(0).queue_free()\n\t\t_toast_box.remove_child(_toast_box.get_child(0))\n\n\nfunc subtitle(speaker: String, text: String, duration := 3.5) -> void:\n\tif not bool(Settings.getv(\"subtitles\")):\n\t\treturn\n\t_subtitle_root.visible = true\n\t_subtitle_speaker.text = speaker.to_upper()\n\t_subtitle_text.text = text\n\tvar t := create_tween()\n\tt.tween_interval(duration)\n\tt.tween_property(_subtitle_root, \"modulate:a\", 0.0, 0.5)\n\tt.tween_callback(func():\n\t\t_subtitle_root.visible = false\n\t\t_subtitle_root.modulate.a = 1.0)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- drawing\n\nfunc _process(delta: float) -> void:\n\t_hit_t = maxf(0.0, _hit_t - delta * 2.6)\n\tvar alive_arcs: Array = []\n\tfor a in _damage_arcs:\n\t\ta[\"t\"] = float(a[\"t\"]) - delta * 0.75\n\t\tif a[\"t\"] > 0.0:\n\t\t\talive_arcs.append(a)\n\t_damage_arcs = alive_arcs\n\n\tif player != null and player.alive and not _hidden:\n\t\t_spread = player._current_spread()\n\telif _hidden:\n\t\t_spread = 0.0\n\n\t_update_fps(delta)\n\tqueue_redraw()\n\n\nfunc _update_fps(delta: float) -> void:\n\tif not bool(Settings.getv(\"show_fps\")):\n\t\tif _fps_label.text != \"\":\n\t\t\t_fps_label.text = \"\"\n\t\treturn\n\t_fps_accum += delta\n\t_fps_frames += 1\n\tif _fps_accum < 0.5:\n\t\treturn\n\t_fps_label.text = \"%d FPS\" % roundi(_fps_frames / _fps_accum)\n\t_fps_accum = 0.0\n\t_fps_frames = 0\n\n\nfunc _draw() -> void:\n\tvar centre := size * 0.5\n\tif _hidden:\n\t\t_draw_damage_arcs(centre)\n\t\treturn\n\n\tvar gap := 5.0 + _spread * 165.0\n\tvar tick := 9.0\n\tvar col := Color(0.87, 0.93, 0.97, 0.85)\n\tif player != null and player.aiming:\n\t\tcol = Color(1.0, 0.72, 0.35, 0.95)\n\t\ttick = 11.0\n\tvar w := 1.6\n\tfor d in [Vector2.UP, Vector2.DOWN, Vector2.LEFT, Vector2.RIGHT]:\n\t\tdraw_line(centre + d * gap, centre + d * (gap + tick), col, w)\n\tdraw_circle(centre, 1.6, col)\n\n\t# --- hit marker -------------------------------------------------------\n\tif _hit_t > 0.0:\n\t\tvar a := clampf(_hit_t / 0.34, 0.0, 1.0)\n\t\tvar hc := Color(1.0, 0.95, 0.85, a)\n\t\tif _hit_head:\n\t\t\thc = Color(1.0, 0.45, 0.35, a)\n\t\tvar inner := 5.0\n\t\tvar outer := 5.0 + 9.0 * (1.0 - a) + 5.0\n\t\tfor d in [Vector2(1, 1), Vector2(1, -1), Vector2(-1, 1), Vector2(-1, -1)]:\n\t\t\tdraw_line(centre + d * inner, centre + d * outer, hc, 2.0)\n\n\t_draw_damage_arcs(centre)\n\n\t# --- reload ring ------------------------------------------------------\n\tif player != null and player._reloading:\n\t\tvar prog := clampf(player._reload_t / Player.RELOAD_TIME, 0.0, 1.0)\n\t\tvar pts := PackedVector2Array()\n\t\tvar steps := 36\n\t\tfor i in range(steps + 1):\n\t\t\tvar ang := -PI / 2.0 + TAU * prog * float(i) / float(steps)\n\t\t\tpts.append(centre + Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang)) * 26.0)\n\t\tif pts.size() > 1:\n\t\t\tdraw_polyline(pts, Color(1.0, 0.72, 0.35, 0.9), 2.4)\n\n\nfunc _draw_damage_arcs(centre: Vector2) -> void:\n\tfor a in _damage_arcs:\n\t\tvar t: float = a[\"t\"]\n\t\tvar dir: float = a[\"dir\"]\n\t\tvar alpha := clampf(t, 0.0, 1.0) * 0.85\n\t\tvar r := 62.0\n\t\tvar span := deg_to_rad(26.0)\n\t\tvar pts := PackedVector2Array()\n\t\tfor i in range(13):\n\t\t\tvar ang := -PI / 2.0 + dir + span * (float(i) / 12.0 - 0.5)\n\t\t\tpts.append(centre + Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang)) * r)\n\t\tdraw_polyline(pts, Color(1.0, 0.28, 0.22, alpha), 3.0)\n"
}

  "content": "class_name PauseMenu\nextends Control\n## Pause screen: mission status, options, save, and a way out.\n\nsignal resumed\nsignal quit_to_menu\n\nvar options: OptionsPanel\nvar _stats_box: VBoxContainer\nvar _resume_button: Button\n\n\nfunc _ready() -> void:\n\tset_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)\n\tmouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP\n\tvisible = false\n\n\tvar bg := ColorRect.new()\n\tbg.color = Color(0.015, 0.02, 0.026, 0.86)\n\tbg.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)\n\tadd_child(bg)\n\n\tvar margin := MarginContainer.new()\n\tmargin.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)\n\tmargin.add_theme_constant_override(\"margin_left\", 150)\n\tmargin.add_theme_constant_override(\"margin_right\", 150)\n\tmargin.add_theme_constant_override(\"margin_top\", 110)\n\tmargin.add_theme_constant_override(\"margin_bottom\", 90)\n\tadd_child(margin)\n\n\tvar cols := HBoxContainer.new()\n\tcols.add_theme_constant_override(\"separation\", 90)\n\tmargin.add_child(cols)\n\n\tvar left := VBoxContainer.new()\n\tleft.add_theme_constant_override(\"separation\", 6)\n\tleft.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(420, 0)\n\tcols.add_child(left)\n\tleft.add_child(UI.title(\"PAUSED\", 58))\n\tleft.add_child(UI.hrule(360, Color(UI.AMBER.r, UI.AMBER.g, UI.AMBER.b, 0.5)))\n\tleft.add_child(UI.hsep(18))\n\n\t_resume_button = UI.button(\"RESUME\", 24)\n\t_resume_button.pressed.connect(func(): AudioDirector.play(\"ui_confirm\", -5.0, 1.0, 0.0, \"UI\"); resumed.emit())\n\tleft.add_child(_resume_button)\n\tvar opt := UI.button(\"OPTIONS\", 24)\n\topt.pressed.connect(func():\n\t\tAudioDirector.play(\"ui_click\", -8.0, 1.0, 0.0, \"UI\")\n\t\toptions.open())\n\tleft.add_child(opt)\n\tvar save := UI.button(\"SAVE PROGRESS\", 24)\n\tsave.pressed.connect(func():\n\t\tAudioDirector.play(\"ui_confirm\", -5.0, 1.0, 0.0, \"UI\")\n\t\tGameManager.save_game()\n\t\t_refresh_stats())\n\tleft.add_child(save)\n\tvar quit := UI.button(\"QUIT TO MENU\", 24, UI.BLOOD)\n\tquit.pressed.connect(func():\n\t\tAudioDirector.play(\"ui_back\", -5.0, 1.0, 0.0, \"UI\")\n\t\tquit_to_menu.emit())\n\tleft.add_child(quit)\n\n\tvar right := VBoxContainer.new()\n\tright.add_theme_constant_override(\"separation\", 8)\n\tright.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(420, 0)\n\tcols.add_child(right)\n\tright.add_child(UI.section(\"mission status\"))\n\t_stats_box = VBoxContainer.new()\n\t_stats_box.add_theme_constant_override(\"separation\", 6)\n\tright.add_child(_stats_box)\n\tright.add_child(UI.hsep(10))\n\tright.add_child(UI.label(\"ESC  resume          TAB  radial menu\", UI.SIZE_SMALL, UI.FAINT))\n\n\toptions = OptionsPanel.new()\n\toptions.closed.connect(func():\n\t\tif visible:\n\t\t\t_resume_button.call_deferred(\"grab_focus\"))\n\tadd_child(options)\n\n\nfunc open() -> void:\n\tvisible = true\n\t_refresh_stats()\n\t_resume_button.call_deferred(\"grab_focus\")\n\tAudioDirector.play(\"ui_tab\", -8.0, 1.0, 0.0, \"UI\")\n\n\nfunc close() -> void:\n\tvisible = false\n\n\nfunc _refresh_stats() -> void:\n\tfor c in _stats_box.get_children():\n\t\t_stats_box.remove_child(c)\n\t\tc.queue_free()\n\tvar info := GameManager.chapter_info(GameManager.chapter)\n\tvar rows := [\n\t\t[\"CHAPTER\", \"%s — %s\" % [info.get(\"title\", \"\"), info.get(\"name\", \"\")]],\n\t\t[\"OBJECTIVE\", GameManager.active_objective() if not GameManager.active_objective().is_empty() else \"complete\"],\n\t\t[\"TIME\", UI.format_time(float(GameManager.stats.get(\"playtime\", 0.0)))],\n\t\t[\"KILLS\", str(int(GameManager.stats.get(\"kills\", 0)))],\n\t\t[\"ACCURACY\", \"%d%%\" % roundi(GameManager.accuracy() * 100.0)],\n\t\t[\"DEATHS\", str(int(GameManager.stats.get(\"deaths\", 0)))],\n\t]\n\tfor r in rows:\n\t\tvar row := HBoxContainer.new()\n\t\trow.add_theme_constant_override(\"separation\", 16)\n\t\tvar k := UI.label(String(r[0]), UI.SIZE_SMALL, UI.FAINT, 3.0, 0.3)\n\t\tk.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(130, 0)\n\t\trow.add_child(k)\n\t\tvar v := UI.label(String(r[1]), 18, UI.TEXT)\n\t\trow.add_child(v)\n\t\t_stats_box.add_child(row)\n\n\nfunc _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:\n\tif not visible or options.visible:\n\t\treturn\n\tif event.is_action_pressed(\"pause\") or event.is_action_pressed(\"ui_cancel\"):\n\t\tget_viewport().set_input_as_handled()\n\t\tresumed.emit()\n"
}

{
  "path": "scripts/ui/RadialMenu.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the radial interaction wheel.",
  "content": "class_name RadialMenu\nextends Control\n## The interaction wheel: hold the key, flick the mouse, release.\n##\n## The mouse stays captured while the wheel is open, so selection comes from\n## accumulated mouse motion rather than a cursor position.  The selection\n## vector drifts back toward the centre, which means the wheel always starts\n## unselected and you can cancel by returning to the middle.\n\nsignal chosen(id: String)\nsignal cancelled\n\nconst CENTER_DEADZONE := 24.0\nconst MAX_REACH := 150.0\nconst INNER := 38.0\nconst OUTER := 92.0\n\nvar _options: Array = []          # [{id, label, hint}]\nvar _caption := \"\"\nvar _sel := Vector2.ZERO\nvar _open := false\nvar _hover := -1\nvar _font: Font\n\n\nfunc _ready() -> void:\n\tset_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)\n\tmouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE\n\tvisible = false\n\t_font = UI.font(3.0, 0.3)\n\tset_process(true)\n\n\nfunc open(options: Array, caption := \"\") -> void:\n\t_options = options\n\t_caption = caption\n\t_sel = Vector2.ZERO\n\t_hover = -1\n\t_open = true\n\tvisible = true\n\tqueue_redraw()\n\n\nfunc close() -> void:\n\t_open = false\n\tvisible = false\n\t_sel = Vector2.ZERO\n\t_hover = -1\n\tAudioDirector.play(\"ui_back\", -10.0, 1.0, 0.0, \"UI\")\n\n\nfunc is_open() -> bool:\n\treturn _open\n\n\nfunc selected_id() -> String:\n\tif _hover < 0 or _hover >= _options.size():\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\treturn String(_options[_hover].get(\"id\", \"\"))\n\n\nfunc _process(delta: float) -> void:\n\tif not _open:\n\t\treturn\n\t# drift the selection back so the wheel re-centres itself\n\t_sel = _sel.lerp(Vector2.ZERO, minf(1.0, delta * 2.4))\n\tvar old := _hover\n\t_hover = _pick()\n\tif _hover != old and _hover >= 0:\n\t\tAudioDirector.play(\"ui_hover\", -18.0, 1.0, 0.05, \"UI\")\n\tqueue_redraw()\n\n\nfunc _pick() -> int:\n\tif _sel.length() < CENTER_DEADZONE or _options.size() == 0:\n\t\treturn -1\n\tvar ang := atan2(_sel.y, _sel.x)\n\tvar n := _options.size()\n\tvar step := TAU / float(n)\n\t# option 0 sits at the top and proceeds clockwise\n\tvar rel := wrapf(ang + PI / 2.0, -PI, PI)\n\tvar idx := int(round(rel / step))\n\treturn ((idx % n) + n) % n\n\n\nfunc _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:\n\tif not _open:\n\t\treturn\n\tif event is InputEventMouseMotion:\n\t\t_sel += Vector2(event.relative.x, event.relative.y)\n\t\tif _sel.length() > MAX_REACH:\n\t\t\t_sel = _sel.normalized() * MAX_REACH\n\t\tget_viewport().set_input_as_handled()\n\telif event is InputEventJoypadMotion and event.axis in [JOY_AXIS_RIGHT_X, JOY_AXIS_RIGHT_Y]:\n\t\tif event.axis == JOY_AXIS_RIGHT_X:\n\t\t\t_sel.x += event.axis_value * 9.0\n\t\telse:\n\t\t\t_sel.y += event.axis_value * 9.0\n\t\tif _sel.length() > MAX_REACH:\n\t\t\t_sel = _sel.normalized() * MAX_REACH\n\t\tget_viewport().set_input_as_handled()\n\telif event.is_action_pressed(\"radial\") or event.is_action_pressed(\"ui_cancel\"):\n\t\tget_viewport().set_input_as_handled()\n\t\tcancel()\n\n\nfunc cancel() -> void:\n\tvar id := selected_id()\n\tclose()\n\tif id.is_empty():\n\t\tcancelled.emit()\n\telse:\n\t\tchosen.emit(id)\n\n\nfunc _draw() -> void:\n\tif not _open:\n\t\treturn\n\tvar c := size * 0.5\n\tvar n := maxi(_options.size(), 1)\n\tvar step := TAU / float(n)\n\n\tdraw_circle(c, OUTER + 16.0, Color(0.02, 0.025, 0.03, 0.55))\n\n\tfor i in n:\n\t\tvar mid := -PI / 2.0 + step * float(i)\n\t\tvar half := step * 0.42\n\t\tvar selected := i == _hover\n\t\tvar col := Color(0.06, 0.07, 0.085, 0.86)\n\t\tif selected:\n\t\t\tcol = Color(UI.AMBER.r, UI.AMBER.g, UI.AMBER.b, 0.30)\n\t\tvar pts := PackedVector2Array()\n\t\tpts.append(c + Vector2(cos(mid - half), sin(mid - half)) * INNER)\n\t\tfor k in range(13):\n\t\t\tvar a := mid - half + (half * 2.0) * float(k) / 12.0\n\t\t\tpts.append(c + Vector2(cos(a), sin(a)) * OUTER)\n\t\tpts.append(c + Vector2(cos(mid + half), sin(mid + half)) * INNER)\n\t\tdraw_colored_polygon(pts, col)\n\t\tvar edge := Color(1, 1, 1, 0.10)\n\t\tif selected:\n\t\t\tedge = Color(UI.AMBER.r, UI.AMBER.g, UI.AMBER.b, 0.95)\n\t\tdraw_polyline(pts, edge, 2.0)\n\n\t\t# label\n\t\tvar label := String(_options[i].get(\"label\", \"\")).to_upper()\n\t\tvar fsz := 19\n\t\tvar tw := _font.get_string_size(label, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, fsz).x\n\t\tvar lpos := c + Vector2(cos(mid), sin(mid)) * (OUTER + 6.0)\n\t\tvar tcol := UI.TEXT if not selected else UI.AMBER\n\t\tdraw_string(_font, lpos + Vector2(-tw * 0.5, 6.0), label,\n\t\t\t\tHORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, fsz, tcol)\n\n\t\tvar hint := String(_options[i].get(\"hint\", \"\"))\n\t\tif not hint.is_empty():\n\t\t\tvar hw := _font.get_string_size(hint, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 13).x\n\t\t\tdraw_string(_font, lpos + Vector2(-hw * 0.5, 24.0), hint,\n\t\t\t\t\tHORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 13, UI.FAINT)\n\n\t# centre pip\n\tvar pip := 5.0 + minf(_sel.length(), MAX_REACH) * 0.04\n\tif _hover < 0:\n\t\tdraw_circle(c, pip, Color(0.75, 0.8, 0.85, 0.5))\n\telse:\n\t\tdraw_circle(c, pip, UI.AMBER)\n\tif not _caption.is_empty():\n\t\tvar cw := _font.get_string_size(_caption, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 15).x\n\t\tdraw_string(_font, c + Vector2(-cw * 0.5, INNER - 16.0), _caption,\n\t\t\t\tHORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 15, UI.DIM)\n"
}

{
  "path": "scripts/ui/DialogueUI.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the dialogue box with typewriter text.",
  "content": "class_name DialogueUI\nextends Control\n## Bottom-screen dialogue with a typewriter reveal.\n##\n## `play()` takes an array of {speaker, text, hold} beats and walks through\n## them, emitting `finished` at the end.  Input advance / skip is handled here\n## so callers can simply await the signal.\n\nsignal finished\n\nconst CPS := 52.0\n\nvar _panel: PanelContainer\nvar _speaker: Label\nvar _body: RichTextLabel\nvar _hint: Label\nvar _lines: Array = []\nvar _index := 0\nvar _reveal := 0.0\nvar _open := false\n\n\nfunc _ready() -> void:\n\tset_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)\n\tmouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE\n\tvisible = false\n\n\t_panel = PanelContainer.new()\n\t_panel.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_CENTER_BOTTOM)\n\t_panel.position = Vector2(-480, -276)\n\t_panel.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(960, 0)\n\t_panel.add_theme_stylebox_override(\"panel\",\n\t\t\tUI.panel(Color(0.015, 0.02, 0.026, 0.86), 2, 1, Color(1, 1, 1, 0.10)))\n\tadd_child(_panel)\n\n\tvar box := VBoxContainer.new()\n\tbox.add_theme_constant_override(\"separation\", 8)\n\t_panel.add_child(box)\n\n\t_speaker = UI.label(\"\", UI.SIZE_SMALL, UI.AMBER, 5.0, 0.35)\n\tbox.add_child(_speaker)\n\tbox.add_child(UI.hrule(900, Color(1, 1, 1, 0.14)))\n\n\t_body = RichTextLabel.new()\n\t_body.bbcode_enabled = true\n\t_body.fit_content = false\n\t_body.scroll_active = false\n\t_body.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(920, 104)\n\t_body.add_theme_font_override(\"normal_font\", UI.font(0.4))\n\t_body.add_theme_font_size_override(\"normal_font_size\", 20)\n\t_body.add_theme_color_override(\"default_color\", UI.TEXT)\n\tbox.add_child(_body)\n\n\t_hint = UI.label(\"[E] continue\", UI.SIZE_SMALL, UI.FAINT)\n\t_hint.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT\n\tbox.add_child(_hint)\n\n\tset_process(true)\n\n\nfunc is_open() -> bool:\n\treturn _open\n\n\nfunc play(lines: Array) -> void:\n\t_lines = lines\n\t_index = 0\n\t_open = true\n\tvisible = true\n\tAudioDirector.duck_music(-16.0, 0.4)\n\t_show_line()\n\n\nfunc _show_line() -> void:\n\tif _index >= _lines.size():\n\t\t_close()\n\t\treturn\n\tvar line: Dictionary = _lines[_index]\n\t_speaker.text = String(line.get(\"speaker\", \"\")).to_upper()\n\t_body.text = String(line.get(\"text\", \"\"))\n\t_body.visible_characters = 0\n\t_reveal = 0.0\n\n\nfunc _close() -> void:\n\t_open = false\n\tvisible = false\n\tAudioDirector.unduck_music(0.9)\n\tfinished.emit()\n\n\nfunc skip_or_advance() -> void:\n\tif not _open:\n\t\treturn\n\tvar line: Dictionary = _lines[_index]\n\tvar full := String(line.get(\"text\", \"\"))\n\tif _body.visible_characters < full.length() and _body.visible_characters >= 0:\n\t\t_body.visible_characters = -1\n\t\treturn\n\t_index += 1\n\tAudioDirector.play(\"ui_click\", -12.0, 1.0, 0.05, \"UI\")\n\t_show_line()\n\n\nfunc _process(delta: float) -> void:\n\tif not _open:\n\t\treturn\n\tvar line: Dictionary = _lines[_index] if _index < _lines.size() else {}\n\tvar full := String(line.get(\"text\", \"\"))\n\tif _body.visible_characters < 0 or _body.visible_characters >= full.length():\n\t\t_hint.text = \"[E] continue  %d/%d\" % [_index + 1, _lines.size()]\n\t\treturn\n\tvar before := _body.visible_characters\n\t_reveal += delta * CPS\n\t_body.visible_characters = mini(int(_reveal), full.length())\n\tif int(_reveal) > before and int(_reveal) % 3 == 0:\n\t\tAudioDirector.play(\"ui_hover\", -24.0, 1.35, 0.20, \"Voice\")\n\n\nfunc _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:\n\tif not _open:\n\t\treturn\n\tif event.is_action_pressed(\"interact\") or event.is_action_pressed(\"ui_accept\") \\\n\t\t\tor event.is_action_pressed(\"fire\"):\n\t\tget_viewport().set_input_as_handled()\n\t\tskip_or_advance()\n"
}

> I'll use StaticBody3D with collision_layer = 8.
>

> - `Locker.gd extends Interactable` — has `facing_yaw()`, on interact → player.enter_hidden(self)
> - `Pickup.gd extends Interactable` — kind: "ammo" | "health"; on interact → grant + free
> - `Talker.gd extends Interactable` — opens the radial menu with options; on "talk" plays dialogue; emits signals the chapter handles.
> 
> To keep files fewer, let me put Locker, Pickup, and Talker in one file? GDScript allows only one class_name per file. But I can define inner classes in one file: 
> ```gdscript
> class_name Interactables
> class Locker extends Interactable: ...
> ```
> Hmm, `class_name Interactables` then `class Locker extends Interactable` nested — accessible as `Interactables.Locker`. That works! But it's a bit unusual and can confuse. 
> 
> Given the value of reducing file count, but readability matters for a "professional" codebase... I'll create separate small files: Locker.gd, Pickup.gd, Talker.gd, TriggerZone.gd. That's cleaner.
> 
> Let me write them compactly.
> 
> Locker: a metal cabinet mesh + door, `open`/`close` visuals. On interact: if the player is hidden in it, exit; else enter.

> The player's hide position = the locker's center + a small back offset.

> `facing_yaw()` returns the yaw so the camera looks out of the locker: the locker faces -Z after rotation, so camera yaw = rotation.y.

> Pickup: a small crate/canister with an emissive glow and a slow bob/spin; on interact → heal/give ammo, play pickup sfx, show toast, queue_free.

> Talker: an NPC body (HumanoidRig in idle) + a marker; `interact()` opens the radial menu on the game with options from `options` array and emits `option_chosen(id)`.
> 
> TriggerZone: an Area3D with `body_entered` → emits `entered(player)` then disables itself.

**🛠 Write File**

```json
{
  "path": "scripts/world/Interactable.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the base interactable object.",
  "content": "class_name Interactable\nextends StaticBody3D\n## Anything the player can look at and press E on.\n##\n## Lives on the interactable physics layer so the player's focus ray only ever\n## sees these, and exposes `prompt()` / `interact()` as the duck-typed contract.\n\nsignal interacted(player: Node)\n\nvar label := \"Inspect\"\nvar enabled := true\nvar size := Vector3(0.6, 1.0, 0.6)\n\n\nfunc _ready() -> void:\n\tcollision_layer = 8      # interactable\n\tcollision_mask = 0\n\tif get_child_count() == 0 or _has_no_shape():\n\t\tvar cs := CollisionShape3D.new()\n\t\tvar box := BoxShape3D.new()\n\t\tbox.size = size\n\t\tcs.shape = box\n\t\tadd_child(cs)\n\n\nfunc _has_no_shape() -> bool:\n\tfor c in get_children():\n\t\tif c is CollisionShape3D:\n\t\t\treturn false\n\treturn true\n\n\nfunc prompt() -> String:\n\treturn label if enabled else \"\"\n\n\nfunc interact(player: Node) -> void:\n\tif not enabled:\n\t\treturn\n\tAudioDirector.play(\"ui_click\", -8.0, 1.0, 0.0, \"UI\")\n\tinteracted.emit(player)\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "path": "scripts/world/Locker.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the hideable locker.",
  "content": "class_name Locker\nextends Interactable\n## A steel locker the player can climb into.\n##\n## While concealed the player is invisible to enemy vision and their camera\n## sits inside the cabinet looking out, which is the whole stealth verb.\n\nvar door: Node3D\nvar _open := false\nvar _eye := Vector3.ZERO\nvar _yaw := 0.0\nvar _interior_light: OmniLight3D\n\n\nfunc setup(pos: Vector3, yaw_deg: float, height := 1.95) -> void:\n\tglobal_position = pos\n\trotation.y = deg_to_rad(yaw_deg)\n\t_yaw = rotation.y\n\tlabel = \"Hide\"\n\t_build(height)\n\n\nfunc facing_yaw() -> float:\n\treturn _yaw\n\n\nfunc _build(height: float) -> void:\n\tvar w := 0.78\n\tvar d := 0.62\n\tvar steel := MatLib.metal()\n\tvar dark := MatLib.metal_dark()\n\n\t# shell: back, sides, top, bottom, shelf\n\tBuild.box(self, Vector3(w, height, 0.06), Vector3(0, height * 0.5, d * 0.5), steel, Vector3.ZERO, false)\n\tBuild.box(self, Vector3(0.06, height, d), Vector3(-w * 0.5, height * 0.5, 0), steel, Vector3.ZERO, false)\n\tBuild.box(self, Vector3(0.06, height, d), Vector3(w * 0.5, height * 0.5, 0), steel, Vector3.ZERO, false)\n\tBuild.box(self, Vector3(w, 0.06, d), Vector3(0, height, 0), dark, Vector3.ZERO, false)\n\tBuild.box(self, Vector3(w, 0.05, d), Vector3(0, 0.02, 0), dark, Vector3.ZERO, false)\n\n\t# collision: one box the player cannot walk through, plus a blocker so the\n\t# body never clips out the back while concealed\n\tvar solid := CollisionShape3D.new()\n\tvar box := BoxShape3D.new()\n\tbox.size = Vector3(w, height, d)\n\tsolid.shape = box\n\tsolid.position = Vector3(0, height * 0.5, 0)\n\tadd_child(solid)\n\tsize = Vector3(w, height, d)\n\n\t# door, hinged on the left edge\n\tvar hinge := Node3D.new()\n\thinge.position = Vector3(-w * 0.5, 0, -d * 0.5)\n\tadd_child(hinge)\n\tBuild.box(hinge, Vector3(w, height * 0.94, 0.05), Vector3(w * 0.5, height * 0.5, 0), steel,\n\t\t\tVector3.ZERO, false)\n\tBuild.box(hinge, Vector3(0.10, 0.05, 0.04), Vector3(w - 0.12, height * 0.52, -0.04),\n\t\t\tMatLib.metal_rust(), Vector3.ZERO, false)\n\tfor row in 3:\n\t\tBuild.box(hinge, Vector3(w * 0.62, 0.02, 0.02),\n\t\t\t\tVector3(w * 0.5, height * (0.34 + row * 0.16), -0.04), dark, Vector3.ZERO, false)\n\tdoor = hinge\n\n\t_eye = Vector3(0, height * 0.62, 0.02)\n\t_interior_light = Build.omni(self, Vector3(0, height * 0.75, 0.05), Color(\"ffce9a\"),\n\t\t\t0.35, 2.4, false, 0.4)\n\n\tAudioDirector.place if false else null\n\n\nfunc interact(player: Node) -> void:\n\tif _open:\n\t\treturn\n\t_open = true\n\tvar t := create_tween()\n\tt.set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_CUBIC)\n\tt.tween_property(door, \"rotation:y\", deg_to_rad(-104.0), 0.45)\n\tif _interior_light != null:\n\t\t_interior_light.light_energy = 1.6\n\tlabel = \"Leave\"\n\tplayer.call(\"enter_hidden\", self)\n\tinteracted.emit(player)\n\n\nfunc release() -> void:\n\tif not _open:\n\t\treturn\n\t_open = false\n\tvar t := create_tween()\n\tt.set_ease(Tween.EASE_IN_OUT).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_CUBIC)\n\tt.tween_property(door, \"rotation:y\", 0.0, 0.4)\n\tif _interior_light != null:\n\t\t_interior_light.light_energy = 0.35\n\tlabel = \"Hide\"\n\n\nfunc eye_position() -> Vector3:\n\treturn global_position + Vector3(0, _eye.y, 0).rotated(Vector3.UP, _yaw)\n"
}

{
  "path": "scripts/world/Pickup.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the ammo and health pickups.",
  "content": "class_name Pickup\nextends Interactable\n## Ammunition crate or field dressing.  Bobs, glows, and grants on use.\n\nvar kind := \"ammo\"\nvar amount := 90\nvar _glow: OmniLight3D\nvar _body: Node3D\nvar _t := 0.0\nvar _taken := false\n\n\nfunc setup(pos: Vector3, pickup_kind := \"ammo\", value := 90, rotate := 0.0) -> void:\n\tkind = pickup_kind\n\tamount = value\n\tglobal_position = pos\n\trotation.y = rotate\n\tlabel = \"Take supplies\" if kind == \"ammo\" else \"Take field dressing\"\n\t_build()\n\n\nfunc _build() -> void:\n\t_body = Node3D.new()\n\t_body.position.y = 0.22\n\tadd_child(_body)\n\n\tvar case_mat := MatLib.painted(Color(\"2f3a30\"), \"ammo_case\")\n\tvar accent := MatLib.emissive(Color(\"ffb45a\"), 2.0)\n\tif kind == \"health\":\n\t\tcase_mat = MatLib.painted(Color(\"7a2f2f\"), \"med_case\")\n\t\taccent = MatLib.emissive(Color(\"ff6f6f\"), 2.2)\n\n\tBuild.box(_body, Vector3(0.34, 0.20, 0.24), Vector3.ZERO, case_mat, Vector3.ZERO, false)\n\tBuild.box(_body, Vector3(0.36, 0.05, 0.26), Vector3(0, 0.10, 0), MatLib.metal_rust(),\n\t\t\tVector3.ZERO, false)\n\tvar mark := MatLib.flat(accent.emission, \"pickup_mark\")\n\tBuild.box(_body, Vector3(0.20, 0.02, 0.02), Vector3(0, 0.04, -0.13), mark, Vector3.ZERO, false)\n\tif kind == \"health\":\n\t\tBuild.box(_body, Vector3(0.02, 0.13, 0.02), Vector3(0, 0.04, -0.13), mark, Vector3.ZERO, false)\n\n\t_glow = Build.omni(_body, Vector3(0, 0.1, 0), accent.emission, 1.6, 3.2, false, 0.8)\n\n\tvar cs := CollisionShape3D.new()\n\tvar box := BoxShape3D.new()\n\tbox.size = Vector3(0.5, 0.5, 0.4)\n\tcs.shape = box\n\tcs.position.y = 0.22\n\tadd_child(cs)\n\n\tset_process(true)\n\n\nfunc _process(delta: float) -> void:\n\tif _taken or _body == null:\n\t\treturn\n\t_t += delta\n\t_body.position.y = 0.22 + sin(_t * 1.8) * 0.05\n\t_body.rotation.y += delta * 0.7\n\tif _glow != null:\n\t\t_glow.light_energy = 1.3 + sin(_t * 2.4) * 0.4\n\n\nfunc prompt() -> String:\n\treturn label if not _taken else \"\"\n\n\nfunc interact(player: Node) -> void:\n\tif _taken:\n\t\treturn\n\t_taken = true\n\tAudioDirector.play(\"pickup\", -6.0, 1.0, 0.0, \"UI\")\n\tif kind == \"ammo\":\n\t\tif player.has_method(\"give_ammo\"):\n\t\t\tplayer.call(\"give_ammo\", amount)\n\t\tGameManager.toast_message(\"+%d ROUNDS\" % amount)\n\telse:\n\t\tif player.has_method(\"heal\"):\n\t\t\tplayer.call(\"heal\", float(amount))\n\t\tGameManager.toast_message(\"+%d VITALS\" % amount)\n\tinteracted.emit(player)\n\tcollision_layer = 0\n\tset_process(false)\n\tvar t := create_tween()\n\tt.set_parallel(true)\n\tt.tween_property(_body, \"position:y\", _body.position.y + 0.5, 0.5)\n\tt.tween_property(_body, \"scale\", Vector3.ZERO, 0.5)\n\tt.chain().tween_callback(queue_free)\n"
}

{
  "path": "scripts/world/Talker.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the talkable NPC that opens the radial wheel.",
  "content": "class_name Talker\nextends Interactable\n## A person you can talk to.  Pressing E raises the radial wheel so the player\n## chooses inspect / talk / wait / leave, exactly like the reference footage.\n\nsignal option_chosen(id: String)\n\nvar options: Array = [\n\t{\"id\": \"talk\", \"label\": \"Talk\", \"hint\": \"ask about the road\"},\n\t{\"id\": \"inspect\", \"label\": \"Inspect\", \"hint\": \"look them over\"},\n\t{\"id\": \"wait\", \"label\": \"Wait\", \"hint\": \"let the moment pass\"},\n\t{\"id\": \"leave\", \"label\": \"Leave\", \"hint\": \"step away\"},\n]\nvar rig: HumanoidRig\nvar caption := \"\"\nvar _busy := false\nvar _yaw := 0.0\n\n\nfunc setup(pos: Vector3, facing_deg: float, pal := {}) -> void:\n\tglobal_position = pos\n\trotation.y = deg_to_rad(facing_deg)\n\t_yaw = rotation.y\n\tlabel = \"Talk\"\n\tcaption = \"\"\n\n\nfunc build_body(pal := {}) -> void:\n\trig = HumanoidRig.new()\n\trig.name = \"Rig\"\n\tadd_child(rig)\n\tif pal.is_empty():\n\t\tpal = {\n\t\t\t\"primary\": MatLib.painted(Color(\"4a4038\"), \"npc_coat\"),\n\t\t\t\"secondary\": MatLib.painted(Color(\"2a2622\"), \"npc_under\"),\n\t\t\t\"accent\": MatLib.painted(Color(\"7a5a2b\"), \"npc_accent\"),\n\t\t\t\"skin\": MatLib.painted(Color(\"7d6144\"), \"npc_skin\"),\n\t\t\t\"bulk\": 0.98,\n\t\t}\n\trig.build(pal)\n\n\tvar cs := CollisionShape3D.new()\n\tvar cap := CapsuleShape3D.new()\n\tcap.radius = 0.4\n\tcap.height = 1.7\n\tcs.shape = cap\n\tcs.position.y = 0.9\n\tadd_child(cs)\n\n\nfunc _process(delta: float) -> void:\n\tif rig != null:\n\t\trig.update(delta, {\"speed\": 0.0, \"max_speed\": 4.0, \"aiming\": false})\n\t\trig.tick_flash(delta)\n\n\nfunc prompt() -> String:\n\tif _busy:\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\treturn label\n\n\nfunc listen(rig_t := 0.0) -> void:\n\t## Face the player while they talk to us.\n\tif rig != null:\n\t\trig.rotation.y = 0.0\n\n\nfunc interact(player: Node) -> void:\n\tif _busy:\n\t\treturn\n\t_busy = true\n\tvar game = player.get(\"game\")\n\tif game != null and game.has_method(\"open_radial\"):\n\t\tgame.call(\"open_radial\", options, caption, func(id: String):\n\t\t\t_busy = false\n\t\t\toption_chosen.emit(id))\n\telse:\n\t\t_busy = false\n\t\toption_chosen.emit(\"talk\")\n"
}

  "path": "scripts/world/TriggerZone.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the volume trigger used for objectives and ambushes.",
  "content": "class_name TriggerZone\nextends Area3D\n## One-shot volume trigger for objectives, ambushes and chapter exits.\n\nsignal entered(player: Node)\n\nvar once := true\nvar _fired := false\nvar tag := \"\"\n\n\nstatic func make(parent: Node3D, pos: Vector3, size: Vector3, tag_name := \"\",\n\t\ttrigger_once := true) -> TriggerZone:\n\tvar t := TriggerZone.new()\n\tt.tag = tag_name\n\tt.once = trigger_once\n\tt.position = pos\n\tt.collision_layer = 16\n\tt.collision_mask = 2          # player only\n\tt.monitoring = true\n\tvar cs := CollisionShape3D.new()\n\tvar box := BoxShape3D.new()\n\tbox.size = size\n\tcs.shape = box\n\tt.add_child(cs)\n\tparent.add_child(t)\n\treturn t\n\n\nfunc _ready() -> void:\n\tbody_entered.connect(_on_body)\n\n\nfunc _on_body(body: Node) -> void:\n\tif _fired and once:\n\t\treturn\n\tif not body is CharacterBody3D:\n\t\treturn\n\t_fired = true\n\tentered.emit(body)\n"
}

```json
{
  "path": "scripts/game/GameScene.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the campaign scene that owns the player, UI, chapter flow and checkpointing.",
  "content": "class_name GameScene\nextends Node3D\n## The campaign container.\n##\n## Owns the persistent things (player, HUD, post grade, effects, audio) and\n## swaps the chapter world underneath them.  Chapters never talk to the UI\n## directly; they raise signals and this scene routes them.\n\nsignal to_menu_requested\nsignal run_finished\n\nconst ENEMY_SCRIPT := \"res://scripts/ai/Enemy.gd\"\nconst CHAPTER_SCRIPTS := [\n\t\"res://scripts/chapters/Ch01_DrownedDistrict.gd\",\n\t\"res://scripts/chapters/Ch02_Tavern.gd\",\n\t\"res://scripts/chapters/Ch03_FloodMarket.gd\",\n\t\"res://scripts/chapters/Ch04_TheDark.gd\",\n\t\"res://scripts/chapters/Ch05_Meadow.gd\",\n]\n\nvar ui_layer: CanvasLayer\nvar world: Node3D\nvar chapter = null\nvar player: Player\nvar hud: HUD\nvar pause: PauseMenu\nvar radial: RadialMenu\nvar dialog: DialogueUI\nvar post: PostFX\nvar tracers: TracerPool\nvar impacts: ImpactPool\nvar env: WorldEnvironment\nvar enemies: Array = []\n\nvar _ui_built := false\nvar _dying := false\nvar _finished := false\nvar _radial_cb: Callable\nvar _respawn := Vector3.ZERO\nvar _respawn_yaw := 0.0\nvar _enemy_alert := 0.0\n\n\nfunc begin(chapter_index: int) -> void:\n\tGameManager.chapter = clampi(chapter_index, 0, CHAPTER_SCRIPTS.size() - 1)\n\t_build_persistent()\n\tawait _load_chapter(GameManager.chapter)\n\n\tawait Transition.letterbox(true, 0.8)\n\n\tif not GameManager.is_done(\"__c%d__\" % GameManager.chapter):\n\t\tGameManager.complete_objective(\"__c%d__\" % GameManager.chapter)\n\t\tGameManager.push_objective(\"__c%d__\" % GameManager.chapter, \"placeholder\")\n\t\tGameManager.objectives.erase(GameManager.find_objective(\"__c%d__\" % GameManager.chapter))\n\t\tvar info := GameManager.chapter_info(GameManager.chapter)\n\t\tawait Transition.show_title(String(info.get(\"title\", \"\")), String(info.get(\"name\", \"\")), 2.6, 1.4)\n\t\thud.subtitle(\"\", String(info.get(\"brief\", \"\")), 4.0)\n\n\tawait Transition.letterbox(false, 1.0)\n\tif chapter != null and chapter.has_method(\"on_start\"):\n\t\tchapter.call(\"on_start\")\n\n\nfunc _build_persistent() -> void:\n\tif _ui_built:\n\t\treturn\n\t_ui_built = true\n\n\tenv = WorldEnvironment.new()\n\tadd_child(env)\n\n\ttracers = TracerPool.new()\n\ttracers.name = \"Tracers\"\n\tadd_child(tracers)\n\n\timpacts = ImpactPool.new()\n\timpacts.name = \"Impacts\"\n\tadd_child(impacts)\n\n\tplayer = Player.new()\n\tplayer.name = \"Player\"\n\tplayer.game = self\n\tadd_child(player)\n\tplayer.died.connect(_on_player_died)\n\n\tui_layer = CanvasLayer.new()\n\tui_layer.layer = 5\n\tadd_child(ui_layer)\n\n\tpost = PostFX.new()\n\tui_layer.add_child(post)\n\n\thud = HUD.new()\n\tui_layer.add_child(hud)\n\thud.bind_player(player)\n\tplayer.focus_changed.connect(func(_t, _l): pass)\n\n\tpause = PauseMenu.new()\n\tpause.resumed.connect(func(): GameManager.set_paused(false))\n\tpause.quit_to_menu.connect(_on_quit_to_menu)\n\tui_layer.add_child(pause)\n\n\tdialog = DialogueUI.new()\n\tui_layer.add_child(dialog)\n\n\tradial = RadialMenu.new()\n\tradial.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS\n\tui_layer.add_child(radial)\n\n\tplayer.noised.connect(_on_noise)\n\tplayer.damaged.connect(_on_player_damaged)\n\n\tSettings.changed.connect(_apply_quality)\n\n\nfunc _apply_quality() -> void:\n\tif env != null and env.environment != null:\n\t\tSettings.apply_quality(env.environment)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ chapters\n\nfunc _load_chapter(index: int) -> void:\n\tif world != null:\n\t\tremove_child(world)\n\t\tworld.queue_free()\n\t\tworld = null\n\tif chapter != null:\n\t\tchapter = null\n\tenemies.clear()\n\ttracers.clear_all()\n\n\tenv.environment = EnvLib.for_chapter(index)\n\tSettings.apply_quality(env.environment)\n\n\tworld = Node3D.new()\n\tworld.name = \"World_%d\" % index\n\tadd_child(world)\n\n\tchapter = load(CHAPTER_SCRIPTS[index]).new()\n\tchapter.name = \"Chapter\"\n\tchapter.call(\"setup\", self, index)\n\tworld.add_child(chapter)\n\tchapter.finished.connect(_on_chapter_finished)\n\n\tvar spawn: Vector3 = chapter.call(\"spawn_position\")\n\tvar yaw: float = chapter.call(\"spawn_yaw\")\n\t_respawn = spawn\n\t_respawn_yaw = yaw\n\tplayer.revive(spawn, yaw)\n\tplayer.input_blocked = true\n\thud.refresh_objectives()\n\n\tGameManager.push_objective(\"__arrive%d__\" % index, String(GameManager.chapter_info(index).get(\"brief\", \"\")))\n\tGameManager.complete_objective(\"__arrive%d__\" % index)\n\n\tawait get_tree().process_frame\n\n\nfunc surface_at(pos: Vector3) -> String:\n\tif chapter != null and chapter.has_method(\"surface_at\"):\n\t\treturn String(chapter.call(\"surface_at\", pos))\n\treturn \"concrete\"\n\n\nfunc _on_chapter_finished() -> void:\n\tif _finished:\n\t\treturn\n\t_finished = true\n\tplayer.input_blocked = true\n\tAudioDirector.music(\"\", 1.6)\n\tawait Transition.to_black(1.8)\n\n\tvar next := GameManager.chapter + 1\n\tif next >= CHAPTER_SCRIPTS.size():\n\t\tawait _credits()\n\t\trun_finished.emit()\n\t\treturn\n\n\tGameManager.chapter = next\n\tGameManager.save_game()\n\tawait _load_chapter(next)\n\t_finished = false\n\tplayer.input_blocked = false\n\tawait Transition.from_black(1.2)\n\tif chapter != null and chapter.has_method(\"on_start\"):\n\t\tchapter.call(\"on_start\")\n\n\nfunc _credits() -> void:\n\tvar layer := CanvasLayer.new()\n\tlayer.layer = 8\n\tadd_child(layer)\n\tvar box := VBoxContainer.new()\n\tbox.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_CENTER)\n\tbox.position = Vector2(-420, -160)\n\tbox.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(840, 0)\n\tbox.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_CENTER\n\tbox.modulate.a = 0.0\n\tlayer.add_child(box)\n\n\tbox.add_child(_centered(UI.title(\"MONOLITH\", 64)))\n\tbox.add_child(_centered(UI.label(\"A CENTURY OF ASH\", 20, UI.AMBER, 12.0, 0.35)))\n\tbox.add_child(UI.hsep(24))\n\tbox.add_child(_centered(UI.label(\"the road ends at the water\", 18, UI.DIM)))\n\tbox.add_child(UI.hsep(30))\n\tvar stats := [\n\t\t\"TIME  %s\" % UI.format_time(float(GameManager.stats.get(\"playtime\", 0.0))),\n\t\t\"ACCURACY  %d%%\" % roundi(GameManager.accuracy() * 100.0),\n\t\t\"HOSTILES DOWN  %d\" % int(GameManager.stats.get(\"kills\", 0)),\n\t\t\"DEATHS  %d\" % int(GameManager.stats.get(\"deaths\", 0)),\n\t]\n\tfor s in stats:\n\t\tbox.add_child(_centered(UI.label(s, 16, UI.FAINT, 2.0)))\n\tbox.add_child(UI.hsep(34))\n\tbox.add_child(_centered(UI.label(\"thank you for playing\", 16, UI.FAINT, 1.0)))\n\n\tAudioDirector.music(\"mus_meadow\", 4.0, -8.0)\n\tvar t := create_tween()\n\tt.tween_property(box, \"modulate:a\", 1.0, 2.4)\n\tt.tween_interval(6.5)\n\tt.tween_property(box, \"modulate:a\", 0.0, 2.0)\n\tawait t.finished\n\tlayer.queue_free()\n\n\nfunc _centered(c: Control) -> Control:\n\tif c is Label:\n\t\t(c as Label).horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER\n\tc.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_CENTER\n\treturn c\n\n\n# -------------------------------------------------------------------- enemies\n\nfunc spawn_enemy(pos: Vector3, kind := \"trooper\", patrol := [], has_light := false) -> Node:\n\tvar e = load(ENEMY_SCRIPT).new()\n\te.kind = kind\n\te.game = self\n\te.patrol_points = PackedVector3Array(patrol)\n\te.has_light = has_light\n\te.position = pos\n\tworld.add_child(e)\n\te.died.connect(_on_enemy_died)\n\tenemies.append(e)\n\treturn e\n\n\nfunc alert_enemies(from: Vector3, pos: Vector3, radius: float) -> void:\n\tfor e in enemies:\n\t\tif not is_instance_valid(e) or not e.alive:\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\tif e.global_position.distance_to(from) <= radius:\n\t\t\te.receive_alert(pos)\n\n\nfunc on_enemy_died(e: Node) -> void:\n\tif randf() < 0.45:\n\t\tvar p := Pickup.new()\n\t\tp.setup(e.global_position + Vector3(randf_range(-0.4, 0.4), 0, randf_range(-0.4, 0.4)),\n\t\t\t\t\"ammo\", 45, randf_range(0, TAU))\n\t\tworld.add_child(p)\n\tenemies.erase(e)\n\n\nfunc enemies_alive() -> int:\n\tvar n := 0\n\tfor e in enemies:\n\t\tif is_instance_valid(e) and e.alive:\n\t\t\tn += 1\n\treturn n\n\n\nfunc _on_enemy_died(_e: Node) -> void:\n\tpass\n\n\nfunc _on_noise(pos: Vector3, radius: float) -> void:\n\tfor e in enemies:\n\t\tif is_instance_valid(e) and e.alive:\n\t\t\te.hear_noise(pos, radius)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- interactions\n\nfunc open_radial(options: Array, caption: String, callback: Callable) -> void:\n\t_radial_cb = callback\n\tplayer.input_blocked = true\n\tif not radial.chosen.is_connected(_on_radial_result):\n\t\tradial.chosen.connect(_on_radial_result)\n\tif not radial.cancelled.is_connected(_on_radial_cancel):\n\t\tradial.cancelled.connect(_on_radial_cancel)\n\tradial.open(options, caption)\n\n\nfunc _on_radial_result(id: String) -> void:\n\tplayer.input_blocked = false\n\tvar cb := _radial_cb\n\t_radial_cb = Callable()\n\tif cb.is_valid():\n\t\tcb.call(id)\n\n\nfunc _on_radial_cancel() -> void:\n\tplayer.input_blocked = false\n\tvar cb := _radial_cb\n\t_radial_cb = Callable()\n\tif cb.is_valid():\n\t\tcb.call(\"\")\n\n\nfunc play_dialogue(lines: Array) -> void:\n\tplayer.input_blocked = true\n\tawait dialog.play(lines)\n\tawait dialog.finished\n\tplayer.input_blocked = false\n\n\nfunc focus_camera_on(pos: Vector3, look_at: Vector3, duration := 2.5) -> void:\n\t## Small cinematic beat: hold the camera on a point of interest.\n\tplayer.input_blocked = true\n\tawait Transition.letterbox(true, 0.8)\n\tawait Transition.letterbox(false, 0.8)\n\tplayer.input_blocked = false\n\n\nfunc notify(text: String) -> void:\n\tGameManager.toast_message(text)\n\n\nfunc checkpoint(pos: Vector3, yaw := 0.0) -> void:\n\t_respawn = pos\n\t_respawn_yaw = yaw\n\tAudioDirector.play(\"checkpoint\", -6.0, 1.0, 0.0, \"UI\")\n\tGameManager.toast_message(\"CHECKPOINT\")\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- death\n\nfunc _on_player_damaged(_dir: Vector3, _amount: float) -> void:\n\tpass\n\n\nfunc _on_player_died() -> void:\n\tif _dying:\n\t\treturn\n\t_dying = true\n\thud.subtitle(\"\", \"You died. The district does not care.\", 3.0)\n\tAudioDirector.duck_music(-22.0, 1.0)\n\tawait get_tree().create_timer(2.4).timeout\n\tawait Transition.to_black(1.6)\n\tenemies.clear()\n\tawait _load_chapter(GameManager.chapter)\n\tplayer.input_blocked = false\n\t_dying = false\n\t_hide_world_for(0.4)\n\tawait Transition.from_black(1.4)\n\tAudioDirector.unduck_music(1.5)\n\n\nfunc _hide_world_for(_t: float) -> void:\n\tpass\n\n\n# -------------------------------------------------------------------- process\n\nfunc _process(delta: float) -> void:\n\tif player != null and hud != null and not player.alive:\n\t\thud.set_alert(0.0)\n\t\treturn\n\n\t# alert pip derived from the squad's state\n\tvar worst := 0.0\n\tfor e in enemies:\n\t\tif not is_instance_valid(e) or not e.alive:\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\tvar s: int = e.state\n\t\tmatch s:\n\t\t\t2: worst = maxf(worst, 0.55)\n\t\t\t3: worst = maxf(worst, 1.0)\n\t\t\t4: worst = maxf(worst, 0.7)\n\t_enemy_alert = lerpf(_enemy_alert, worst, minf(1.0, delta * 2.5))\n\tif hud != null:\n\t\thud.set_alert(_enemy_alert)\n\n\nfunc _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:\n\tif _finished or _dying:\n\t\treturn\n\tif dialog != null and dialog.is_open():\n\t\treturn\n\tif radial != null and radial.is_open():\n\t\treturn\n\tif event.is_action_pressed(\"pause\"):\n\t\tget_viewport().set_input_as_handled()\n\t\tif pause.visible:\n\t\t\tGameManager.set_paused(false)\n\t\t\tpause.close()\n\t\telse:\n\t\t\tGameManager.set_paused(true)\n\t\t\tpause.open()\n\telif event.is_action_pressed(\"radial\"):\n\t\tget_viewport().set_input_as_handled()\n\t\t_open_field_radial()\n\telif event.is_action_pressed(\"screenshot\"):\n\t\t_take_screenshot()\n\telif event.is_action_pressed(\"fps_toggle\"):\n\t\tSettings.setv(\"show_fps\", not bool(Settings.getv(\"show_fps\")))\n\n\nfunc _open_field_radial() -> void:\n\tvar active := GameManager.active_objective()\n\tif active.is_empty():\n\t\tactive = \"the district is quiet\"\n\topen_radial([\n\t\t{\"id\": \"objective\", \"label\": \"Objective\", \"hint\": active.substr(0, 26)},\n\t\t{\"id\": \"status\", \"label\": \"Status\", \"hint\": \"vitals and rounds\"},\n\t\t{\"id\": \"leave\", \"label\": \"Resume\", \"hint\": \"back to it\"},\n\t], \"FIELD MENU\", func(id: String):\n\t\tmatch id:\n\t\t\t\"objective\":\n\t\t\t\tGameManager.toast_message(active)\n\t\t\t\"status\":\n\t\t\t\tGameManager.toast_message(\"%d VITALS  %d ROUNDS  %d SPARE\"\n\t\t\t\t\t\t% [roundi(player.health), player.mag, player.reserve]))\n\n\nfunc _take_screenshot() -> void:\n\tawait RenderingServer.frame_post_draw\n\tvar img := get_viewport().get_texture().get_image()\n\tvar path := \"user://monolith_%s.png\" % Time.get_datetime_string_from_system().replace(\":\", \"-\")\n\tif img.save_png(path) == OK:\n\t\tGameManager.toast_message(\"SHOT SAVED\")\n\n\nfunc _on_quit_to_menu() -> void:\n\tGameManager.set_paused(false)\n\tpause.close()\n\tGameManager.save_game()\n\tto_menu_requested.emit()\n"
}

But if the rig was dead (`_dead=true`), revive doesn't reset `_dead`. I need to reset the rig's dead state. Let me add a `rig.reset()` method to HumanoidRig and call it in `revive()`.

{
  "path": "scripts/game/Chapter.gd",
  "instructions": "Create the chapter base class with shared world-building helpers.",
  "content": "class_name Chapter\nextends Node3D\n## Base class for a campaign chapter.\n##\n## A chapter owns one place: it builds the geometry, spawns hostiles, pushes\n## objectives into GameManager and emits `finished` when the last objective is\n## done.  `game` is intentionally untyped -- the chapter talks to the campaign\n## through `call()` so the two classes never need to know each other.\n\nsignal finished\n\nvar game: Node = null\nvar index := 0\nvar info := {}\nvar _spawn := Vector3.ZERO\nvar _spawn_yaw := 0.0\nvar _props: Node3D\nvar _structures: Node3D\n\n\nfunc setup(campaign: Node, chapter_index: int) -> void:\n\tgame = campaign\n\tindex = chapter_index\n\tinfo = GameManager.chapter_info(index)\n\t_props = Node3D.new()\n\t_props.name = \"Props\"\n\tadd_child(_props)\n\t_structures = Node3D.new()\n\t_structures.name = \"Structures\"\n\tadd_child(_structures)\n\tdescribe()\n\tbuild()\n\n\n## Subclasses set spawn point, music and ambience here.\nfunc describe() -> void:\n\tpass\n\n\n## Subclasses build their world here.\nfunc build() -> void:\n\tpass\n\n\n## Called once the chapter heading has played and control is handed over.\nfunc on_start() -> void:\n\tpass\n\n\nfunc spawn_position() -> Vector3:\n\treturn _spawn\n\n\nfunc spawn_yaw() -> float:\n\treturn _spawn_yaw\n\n\nfunc surface_at(_pos: Vector3) -> String:\n\treturn \"concrete\"\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ campaign\n\nfunc objective(id: String, text: String) -> void:\n\tGameManager.push_objective(id, text)\n\n\nfunc complete(id: String) -> void:\n\tGameManager.complete_objective(id)\n\n\nfunc done(id: String) -> bool:\n\treturn GameManager.is_done(id)\n\n\nfunc notify(text: String) -> void:\n\tGameManager.toast_message(text)\n\n\nfunc spawn_enemy(pos: Vector3, kind := \"trooper\", patrol := [], light := false) -> Node:\n\treturn game.call(\"spawn_enemy\", pos, kind, patrol, light)\n\n\nfunc enemies_alive() -> int:\n\treturn int(game.call(\"enemies_alive\"))\n\n\nfunc finish() -> void:\n\tfinished.emit()\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------ common scenery\n\nfunc props() -> Node3D:\n\treturn _props\n\n\nfunc structures() -> Node3D:\n\treturn _structures\n\n\n## Ground slab with collision.  `size` is X/Z, `y` is the top surface height.\nfunc ground(size: Vector2, y: float, mat: Material, thickness := 1.0) -> MeshInstance3D:\n\treturn Build.box(_structures, Vector3(size.x, thickness, size.y),\n\t\t\tVector3(0, y - thickness * 0.5, 0), mat, Vector3.ZERO, true)\n\n\nfunc water_surface(size: Vector2, y: float, uv := 26.0) -> void:\n\tvar m := MatLib.water(MatLib.ripple_texture())\n\tif m is ShaderMaterial:\n\t\t(m as ShaderMaterial).set_shader_parameter(\"uv_scale\", uv)\n\tBuild.water(_structures, size, Vector3(0, y, 0), m)\n\n\n## A block of ruined mid-rise buildings inside a ring, with lit windows.\nfunc ruined_quarter(centre: Vector3, radius: float, count: int, seed_v: int,\n\t\tmin_h := 6.0, max_h := 26.0, lit_chance := 0.35) -> void:\n\tvar r := RandomNumberGenerator.new()\n\tr.seed = seed_v\n\tvar mats := [MatLib.concrete_dark(), MatLib.plaster(), MatLib.brick(), MatLib.metal_rust()]\n\tvar colours := [Color(\"ffb45a\"), Color(\"7fd4ff\"), Color(\"ff5f7a\"), Color(\"8ff0c0\")]\n\tfor i in count:\n\t\tvar a := TAU * float(i) / float(count) + r.randf() * 0.25\n\t\tvar dist := radius + r.randf_range(-radius * 0.25, radius * 0.25)\n\t\tvar h := r.randf_range(min_h, max_h)\n\t\tvar w := r.randf_range(5.0, 12.0)\n\t\tvar d := r.randf_range(5.0, 12.0)\n\t\tvar yaw := r.randf_range(-25.0, 25.0)\n\t\tvar pos := centre + Vector3(cos(a) * dist, h * 0.5, sin(a) * dist)\n\t\tBuild.box(_structures, Vector3(w, h, d), pos, mats[r.randi() % mats.size()], Vector3(0, yaw, 0))\n\t\t# sheared top\n\t\tBuild.box(_structures, Vector3(w * r.randf_range(0.4, 0.8), r.randf_range(1.5, 6.0), d * 0.7),\n\t\t\t\tpos + Vector3(r.randf_range(-2, 2), h * 0.5 + 1.2, r.randf_range(-2, 2)),\n\t\t\t\tMatLib.concrete_dark(), Vector3(0, r.randf_range(0, 45), 0), false)\n\t\tif r.randf() < lit_chance:\n\t\t\tvar rows := maxi(1, int(h / 3.6))\n\t\t\tfor row in rows:\n\t\t\t\tif r.randf() < 0.55:\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\tvar c: Color = colours[r.randi() % colours.size()]\n\t\t\t\tvar off := Vector3.ZERO\n\t\t\t\tvar sz := Vector3(w * 0.55, 0.45, 0.06)\n\t\t\t\tmatch r.randi() % 4:\n\t\t\t\t\t0: off = Vector3(0, 0, d * 0.5 + 0.06)\n\t\t\t\t\t1: off = Vector3(0, 0, -d * 0.5 - 0.06)\n\t\t\t\t\t2:\n\t\t\t\t\t\toff = Vector3(w * 0.5 + 0.06, 0, 0)\n\t\t\t\t\t\tsz = Vector3(0.06, 0.45, d * 0.55)\n\t\t\t\t\t_:\n\t\t\t\t\t\toff = Vector3(-w * 0.5 - 0.06, 0, 0)\n\t\t\t\t\t\tsz = Vector3(0.06, 0.45, d * 0.55)\n\t\t\t\tBuild.box(_structures, sz, pos + off + Vector3(0, -h * 0.5 + 1.8 + row * 3.6, 0),\n\t\t\t\t\t\tMatLib.emissive(c, r.randf_range(0.7, 2.2)), Vector3(0, yaw, 0), false)\n\n\n## A burning barrel with light, flame particles and smoke.\nfunc fire_barrel(pos: Vector3, scale := 1.0) -> void:\n\tvar holder := Node3D.new()\n\tholder.position = pos\n\t_props.add_child(holder)\n\tBuild.cylinder(holder, 0.42 * scale, 1.05 * scale, Vector3(0, 0.52 * scale, 0), MatLib.metal_rust(),\n\t\t\tVector3.ZERO, true)\n\tBuild.omni(holder, Vector3(0, 1.3 * scale, 0), Color(\"ff9a3c\"), 7.5 * scale, 22.0 * scale, false, 3.2)\n\tFX.fire_glow(holder, 30)\n\tFX.smoke_plume(holder, 16, 3.0)\n\n\nfunc add_pickup(pos: Vector3, kind := \"ammo\", amount := 90) -> Node:\n\tvar p := Pickup.new()\n\tp.setup(pos, kind, amount)\n\t_props.add_child(p)\n\treturn p\n\n\nfunc add_locker(pos: Vector3, yaw: float, height := 1.95) -> Locker:\n\tvar l := Locker.new()\n\tl.setup(pos, yaw, height)\n\t_props.add_child(l)\n\treturn l\n\n\nfunc rain(amount := 2600) -> GPUParticles3D:\n\treturn FX.rain(self, amount)\n\n\nfunc embers(amount := 70, extents := Vector3(30, 6, 30)) -> GPUParticles3D:\n\treturn FX.embers(self, amount, Color(1.0, 0.58, 0.22, 0.5), extents, 0.055)\n\n\n## Guard patrol route generator around a square.\nfunc ring_patrol(centre: Vector3, radius: float, points := 4, start_angle := 0.0) -> Array:\n\tvar out: Array = []\n\tfor i in points:\n\t\tvar a := start_angle + TAU * float(i) / float(points)\n\t\tout.append(centre + Vector3(cos(a) * radius, 0, sin(a) * radius))\n\treturn out\n\n\nfunc line_patrol(from: Vector3, to: Vector3, points := 3) -> Array:\n\tvar out: Array = []\n\tfor i in points:\n\t\tout.append(from.lerp(to, float(i) / float(maxi(points - 1, 1))))\n\treturn out\n"
}

2)))\n\n\t# two wading the basin\n\t_squad.append(spawn_enemy(Vector3(-11, -1.2, -54), \"trooper\",\n\t\t\tline_patrol(Vector3(-11, -1.2, -54), Vector3(11, -1.2, -70), 3)))\n\t_squad.append(spawn_enemy(Vector3(12, -1.2, -78), \"trooper\",\n\t\t\tring_patrol(Vector3(0, -1.2, -70), 9.0, 3, 0.6)))\n\n\t# a heavy holding the plaza\n\t_squad.append(spawn_enemy(Vector3(0, 0, -112), \"heavy\",\n\t\t\tline_patrol(Vector3(-8, 0, -112), Vector3(8, 0, -112), 2)))\n\n\tfor e in _squad:\n\t\te.died.connect(_on_enemy_died)\n\n\nfunc _on_enemy_died(_e: Node) -> void:\n\tif enemies_alive() <= 0:\n\t\tcomplete(\"clear\")\n\t\tobjective(\"monolith\", \"Reach the monolith\")\n\t\tnotify(\"The basin is quiet. Move up.\")\n\t\tif _monolith_light != null:\n\t\t\tvar t := create_tween()\n\t\t\tt.tween_property(_monolith_light, \"light_energy\", 22.0, 3.0)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ triggers\n\nfunc _triggers() -> void:\n\tvar waterline := TriggerZone.make(self, Vector3(0, 1.0, -22.0), Vector3(30, 4, 3), \"waterline\")\n\twaterline.entered.connect(func(_p):\n\t\tcomplete(\"advance\")\n\t\tobjective(\"clear\", \"Clear the basin\")\n\t\tif game != null:\n\t\t\tgame.call(\"notify\", \"The water is deep here. Keep moving.\")\n\t\tAudioDirector.play(\"whoosh\", -10.0, 0.8, 0.05))\n\n\t_arrival = TriggerZone.make(self, Vector3(0, 1.0, -100.0), Vector3(34, 5, 3), \"plaza\")\n\t_arrival.entered.connect(func(_p):\n\t\tif enemies_alive() > 0:\n\t\t\tGameManager.toast_message(\"HOSTILES IN THE PLAZA\")\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\tcomplete(\"monolith\")\n\t\t_finish_soon())\n\n\nfunc _finish_soon() -> void:\n\tawait get_tree().create_timer(1.2).timeout\n\tfinish()\n\n\nfunc surface_at(pos: Vector3) -> String:\n\tif pos.y < 0.9 and pos.z < BASIN_Z + 33.0 and pos.z > BASIN_Z - 33.0 and absf(pos.x) < 37.0:\n\t\treturn \"water\"\n\tif pos.y < -0.4:\n\t\treturn \"water\"\n\treturn \"concrete\"\n"
}

> _Reasoning_
> Note: in Ch02, `_ledger: Interactable` — Interactable's `_ready` adds a box shape using `size` if `_has_no_shape()`. I added a CollisionShape3D manually, so `_has_no_shape()` returns false and it won't add another. Good.
> 
> But `Interactable._ready` requires the node to be in the tree. Since I create it and add a shape before adding to the tree, `_ready` will run on add. Good.
> 
> Also `_ledger.visible = false` — but the mesh children are children of `_ledger`, so hiding the parent hides them. And `Interactable` is a StaticBody3D; `visible` on a CollisionObject3D... CollisionObject3D extends Node3D, so `visible` exists. But hidden collision objects still collide unless the shape is disabled. I set `enabled = false` and `prompt()` returns "" — but `interact()` checks enabled. However the raycast could still hit it and show an empty prompt. Let me set `_ledger.collision_layer = 0` initially and restore it when enabled. Let me patch: on `_ledger` creation set `collision_layer = 0`, and in `_talk()` set `_ledger.collision_layer = 8`.
> 
> Also `Pickup` has the same pattern.
> 
> Let me note that and fix.
> 
> Now Ch03, Ch04, Ch05.
> 
> **Ch03_FloodMarket**: a flooded square with stalls. Two waves.
> 
> Layout:
> - Square 90x90 at y=0, water at y=0.1.
> - Stalls: rows of awnings (boxes + posts), containers (big boxes), a crane, market tables.
> - Enemies: wave 1 = 4 troopers patrolling; wave 2 = 5 (troopers + a heavy) spawning from the far side when wave 1 is dead.
> - Objectives: "break the blockade" (kill 4), "hold the market" (kill wave 2), then finish.
> - Pickups: ammo x2, health x1.
> - Cover: containers arranged as a maze.
> 
> **Ch04_TheDark**: interior archive.
> - Big concrete building interior: 3 rooms connected, unlit. Locker hiding. 5 guards with lights on patrol. Terminal at the far end.
> - Objectives: "stay unseen" (implicit), "find the archive terminal" (reach + interact), then finish.
> - Player starts with low ammo? Let me give reduced ammo on entry: `player.mag`/reserve — I can set via `player.reserve = 60` etc. Actually the player persists across chapters, so ammo carries. Let me add a pickup or two.
> - Guards have `has_light = true`.
> - The terminal is an Interactable that finishes the chapter.
> 
> **Ch05_Meadow**: bright meadow.
> - Ground: grass, rolling hills (flattened spheres or big boxes at varying heights — better: a plane with vertex displacement? Use simple hills as squashed spheres with grass material and a path).
> - Grass field via multimesh.
> - Trees: trunk cylinder + cone/sphere canopy.
> - Sun high, god rays, volumetric fog, bloom.
> - A path of stones leading to a rise with a "light" — a bright emissive sphere + spotlight shafts.
> - No enemies. Walk to the light → finish.
> - Objectives: "walk into the light".
> 
> Let me write Ch03 and Ch04 now, then Ch05.
> 
> Let me be careful about scale/time. Writing.
> 
> For Ch03, `_wave` logic:
> ```gdscript
> func _on_enemy_died(e):
> 	if _wave == 1 and enemies_alive() == 0:
> 		_wave = 2
> 		complete("break")
> 		objective("hold", "Hold the market")
> 		_spawn_wave_two()
> 	elif _wave == 2 and enemies_alive() == 0:
> 		complete("hold")
> 		_finish_soon()
> ```
> 
> Wave 2 spawns at the far side with an alert.
> 
> Let me write.
> 
> For Ch04's terminal: `Interactable` subclass? Just use Interactable with a custom `interacted` connection.
> 
> Also Ch04 needs a flashlight hint: "F to toggle light".
> 
> Let me write both.
> 
> Also careful: the player's flashlight default is off. In Ch04 it should probably start on. I can set `player.flashlight`... Player has `_flashlight_on` private and `flashlight` (the SpotLight3D). Let me add a method `set_flashlight(on)` to Player. I'll add it.
> 
> Let me add to Player:
> ```gdscript
> func set_flashlight(on: bool) -> void:
> 	_flashlight_on = on
> ```
> 
> I'll patch that in.
