a := TAU * float(i) / 8.0\n\t\tBuild.cylinder(props(), 0.5, 2.4 + fmod(float(i), 3.0),\n\t\t\t\tVector3(cos(a) * 30.0, 1.2, sin(a) * 30.0 - 6.0), MatLib.metal_rust(),\n\t\t\t\tVector3(90, rad_to_deg(a), 0), true)\n\tadd_pickup(Vector3(-11, 0, 14), \"ammo\", 120)\n\tadd_pickup(Vector3(21, 0, -10), \"ammo\", 120)\n\tadd_pickup(Vector3(0, 0, -28), \"health\", 55)\n\n\nfunc _stalls() -> void:\n\tvar canvas := [Color(\"8a3a3a\"), Color(\"2f6a5a\"), Color(\"6a5a8a\"), Color(\"8a7a3a\")]\n\tfor i in 7:\n\t\tvar x := -27.0 + float(i) * 9.0\n\t\tvar z := -24.0 if i % 2 == 0 else 22.0\n\t\tvar c: Color = canvas[i % canvas.size()]\n\t\t# posts\n\t\tfor dx in [-2.2, 2.2]:\n\t\t\tfor dz in [-1.6, 1.6]:\n\t\t\t\tBuild.cylinder(props(), 0.07, 2.4, Vector3(x + dx, 1.2, z + dz), MatLib.metal_dark())\n\t\t# awning\n\t\tBuild.box(props(), Vector3(5.0, 0.12, 3.8), Vector3(x, 2.5, z),\n\t\t\t\tMatLib.fabric(c), Vector3(0, 0, 3.0), false)\n\t\t# table with stock\n\t\tBuild.box(props(), Vector3(3.6, 0.12, 1.6), Vector3(x, 0.95, z), MatLib.wood(),\n\t\t\t\tVector3.ZERO, true)\n\t\tfor k in 4:\n\t\t\tBuild.box(props(), Vector3(0.5, 0.34, 0.5),\n\t\t\t\t\tVector3(x - 1.3 + float(k) * 0.9, 1.18, z + (0.2 if k % 2 == 0 else -0.2)),\n\t\t\t\t\tMatLib.wood_dark(), Vector3(0, float(k) * 22.0, 0), false)\n\t\tBuild.omni(props(), Vector3(x, 2.2, z), Color(\"ffbb77\"), 2.4, 8.0, false, 1.2)\n\n\t# gantry crane descending over the square\n\t_crane = Node3D.new()\n\t_crane.position = Vector3(30, 0, -34)\n\tprops().add_child(_crane)\n\tBuild.box(_crane, Vector3(1.2, 18, 1.2), Vector3(0, 9, 0), MatLib.metal_rust(), Vector3.ZERO, true)\n\tBuild.box(_crane, Vector3(1.2, 18, 1.2), Vector3(-12, 9, 0), MatLib.metal_rust(), Vector3.ZERO, true)\n\tBuild.box(_crane, Vector3(14, 1.0, 1.0), Vector3(-6, 18.5, 0), MatLib.metal_rust(), Vector3.ZERO, true)\n\tBuild.box(_crane, Vector3(2.0, 2.0, 2.0), Vector3(-6, 16.0, 0), MatLib.metal_dark(), Vector3.ZERO, false)\n\n\nfunc _atmosphere() -> void:\n\train(2400)\n\tembers(60, Vector3(46, 6, 46))\n\tBuild.sun(self, Vector3(-11, 148, 0), Color(\"ff9c52\"), 1.15, 3.2)\n\tfire_barrel(Vector3(-20, 0, 26))\n\tfire_barrel(Vector3(24, 0, -30))\n\tfire_barrel(Vector3(-4, 0, 40))\n\tfor i in 4:\n\t\tBuild.spot(props(), Vector3(-30.0 + float(i) * 20.0, 8.0, -40.0), Vector3(-26, 180, 0),\n\t\t\t\tColor(\"ffd0a0\"), 3.0, 40.0, 34.0, true, 2.0)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- enemies\n\nfunc _spawn_wave_one() -> void:\n\t_wave_one = [\n\t\tspawn_enemy(Vector3(-18, 0, -4), \"trooper\", ring_patrol(Vector3(-18, 0, -4), 8.0, 3)),\n\t\tspawn_enemy(Vector3(16, 0, -4), \"trooper\", ring_patrol(Vector3(16, 0, -4), 8.0, 3, 1.0)),\n\t\tspawn_enemy(Vector3(0, 0, -20), \"trooper\", line_patrol(Vector3(-10, 0, -20), Vector3(10, 0, -20), 3)),\n\t\tspawn_enemy(Vector3(0, 0, 12), \"heavy\", line_patrol(Vector3(-6, 0, 12), Vector3(6, 0, 12), 2)),\n\t]\n\tfor e in _wave_one:\n\t\te.died.connect(_on_enemy_died)\n\n\nfunc _spawn_wave_two() -> void:\n\tvar entries := [\n\t\tVector3(-34, 0, -30), Vector3(34, 0, -30), Vector3(-24, 0, -38),\n\t\tVector3(24, 0, -38), Vector3(0, 0, -40),\n\t]\n\tvar kinds := [\"trooper\", \"trooper\", \"trooper\", \"trooper\", \"heavy\"]\n\tfor i in entries.size():\n\t\tvar e := spawn_enemy(entries[i], kinds[i], [])\n\t\te.died.connect(_on_enemy_died)\n\t\t_wave_two.append(e)\n\t# they know roughly where the player is\n\tif game != null and game.player != null:\n\t\tfor e in _wave_two:\n\t\t\te.receive_alert(game.player.global_position)\n\tAudioDirector.play(\"enemy_alert\", -4.0, 0.9, 0.0, \"Voice\")\n\tGameManager.toast_message(\"CONTACT FROM THE FAR PIER\")\n\n\nfunc _on_enemy_died(_e: Node) -> void:\n\tif enemies_alive() > 0:\n\t\treturn\n\tif _wave == 1:\n\t\t_wave = 2\n\t\tcomplete(\"break\")\n\t\tobjective(\"hold\", \"Hold the market\")\n\t\tnotify(\"That was the picket. The rest are coming.\")\n\t\tawait get_tree().create_timer(3.0).timeout\n\t\t_spawn_wave_two()\n\telif _wave == 2:\n\t\tcomplete(\"hold\")\n\t\tnotify(\"The market is yours. The way in is open.\")\n\t\tawait get_tree().create_timer(2.0).timeout\n\t\tfinish()\n\n\nfunc surface_at(pos: Vector3) -> String:\n\tif pos.y < 0.9:\n\t\treturn \"water\"\n\treturn \"concrete\"\n"
}

> _Reasoning_
> Bugs to fix:
> 1. Ch04: `Build.painted if false else null` — remove that garbage line in the columns loop.
> 2. Ch04 `_rooms()`: `walls` array where each entry is `[Vector3, float, float]` — I destructure `w[0]` and `w[1]` which is `10.0` as w2. But I wrote `[Vector3(-11.0, 0, 12.0), 10.0, 0.0]` so w[1] = 10.0. OK. Fine but sloppy; keep.
> 3. Ch02: ledger collision_layer fix.
> 4. Player: add `set_flashlight`.
> 
> Also Ch04's `game.player` — `game` is a Node; `game.player` dynamic access works. But `var p = game.player` then `p.reserve = ...` — dynamic. Fine. However `p.set_flashlight(false)` — call method dynamically; needs `set_flashlight` to exist.
> 
> Let me fix these, then write Ch05, then run a full smoke test.
> 
> Also Ch04 `_guards[0].hear_range` — Enemy has `hear_range` var. Good.
> 
> Let me make the fixes and write Ch05.
> 
> **Ch05_Meadow**:
> - Bright meadow. Ground: grass, rolling hills.
> - Layout: a path from the treeline to a rise; the "light" at the end.
> - Rolling hills: create a few large flattened spheres with the meadow material at various positions to suggest hills, plus a flat ground.
>   Better: create gentle terrain using a PlaneMesh with vertex displacement? I'd need a shader. Let me use big flattened spheres (radius 18, squash y 0.12) at the sides to make hills, and a flat ground. Combined with grass multimesh and trees, this reads as a meadow.
> - Trees: trunk + 3 canopy spheres at random positions around the edges.
> - Grass: `Build.grass_field` multimesh 3000 blades across the area. Also flower dots (small emissive-ish quads).
> - Path: a series of flat stone slabs.
> - The light: a bright emissive sphere high on the rise + a big spotlight + shafts + particles (motes).
> - Audio: mus_meadow, amb_meadow.
> - Objectives: "Walk into the light".
> - Trigger at the light → dialogue/credits → finish.
> 
> Let me write it.
> 
> Also birds? Skip.
> 
> Let me write Ch05 and the fixes.

  "instructions": "Create chapter 5: the sunlit meadow epilogue.",
  "content": "extends Chapter\n## CHAPTER V -- THE MEADOW\n##\n## The release.  After four chapters of rain and concrete the palette flips\n## completely: full sun, volumetric god rays, no hostiles, and a walk uphill\n## toward the light.\n\nconst PATH_END := -120.0\n\nvar _light_node: Node3D\nvar _bloom: OmniLight3D\nvar _motes: GPUParticles3D\n\n\nfunc describe() -> void:\n\t_spawn = Vector3(0, 0.3, 34)\n\t_spawn_yaw = 0.0\n\tGameManager.set_objectives([\n\t\t{\"id\": \"walk\", \"text\": \"Walk into the light\"},\n\t])\n\n\nfunc build() -> void:\n\t_ground()\n\t_hills()\n\t_grass()\n\t_trees()\n\t_path()\n\t_the_light()\n\tset_process(true)\n\n\nfunc _ground() -> void:\n\tground(Vector2(210, 200), 0.0, MatLib.meadow(), 2.0)\n\n\nfunc _hills() -> void:\n\t# Flattened spheres read as rolling ground once the grass field covers them.\n\tvar r := RandomNumberGenerator.new()\n\tr.seed = 5150\n\tfor i in 9:\n\t\tvar a := TAU * float(i) / 9.0 + 0.3\n\t\tvar dist := 62.0 + r.randf_range(-10.0, 16.0)\n\t\tvar radius := r.randf_range(14.0, 26.0)\n\t\tBuild.sphere(structures(), radius, Vector3(cos(a) * dist, -radius * 0.86, sin(a) * dist),\n\t\t\t\tMatLib.meadow(), Vector3(1.0, 0.30, 1.0), false)\n\t# the rise the player walks up to\n\tBuild.sphere(structures(), 34.0, Vector3(0, -28.0, PATH_END - 14.0), MatLib.meadow(),\n\t\t\tVector3(1.0, 0.34, 1.0), false)\n\t# a walkable ramp so the rise is climbable\n\tBuild.ramp(structures(), 16.0, 26.0, 2.6, Vector3(0, 0, PATH_END + 14.0), MatLib.meadow())\n\n\nfunc _grass() -> void:\n\tBuild.grass_field(self, Vector3(0, 0, -20), Vector3(80, 0, 80), 2600, 7, 0.55)\n\tBuild.grass_field(self, Vector3(0, 0, -100), Vector3(40, 0, 30), 900, 8, 0.42)\n\t# wildflowers\n\tvar r := RandomNumberGenerator.new()\n\tr.seed = 313\n\tvar flowers := [\n\t\tMatLib.flat(Color(\"ffd85a\"), \"flower_a\"),\n\t\tMatLib.flat(Color(\"ffffff\"), \"flower_b\"),\n\t\tMatLib.flat(Color(\"d07ad0\"), \"flower_c\"),\n\t]\n\tfor i in 240:\n\t\tvar p := Vector3(r.randf_range(-70, 70), r.randf_range(0.16, 0.34), r.randf_range(20, -150))\n\t\tBuild.quad(self, Vector2(0.12, 0.12), p, flowers[r.randi() % flowers.size()],\n\t\t\t\tVector3(0, r.randf_range(0, 90), 0))\n\n\nfunc _trees() -> void:\n\tvar r := RandomNumberGenerator.new()\n\tr.seed = 606\n\tvar bark := MatLib.wood_dark()\n\tvar leaf := MatLib.flat(Color(\"4f7a35\"), \"leaf\", 1.0)\n\tleaf.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_PER_PIXEL\n\tleaf.albedo_color = Color(\"547f38\")\n\tfor i in 34:\n\t\tvar a := r.randf_range(0.0, TAU)\n\t\tvar dist := r.randf_range(52.0, 96.0)\n\t\tvar p := Vector3(cos(a) * dist, 0, sin(a) * dist - 30.0)\n\t\tvar h := r.randf_range(6.0, 13.0)\n\t\tvar t := Node3D.new()\n\t\tt.position = p\n\t\tstructures().add_child(t)\n\t\tBuild.cylinder(t, 0.34, h, Vector3(0, h * 0.5, 0), bark, Vector3.ZERO, true)\n\t\tfor k in 3:\n\t\t\tvar off := Vector3(r.randf_range(-1.8, 1.8), h * 0.82 + float(k) * 1.5, r.randf_range(-1.8, 1.8))\n\t\t\tBuild.sphere(t, r.randf_range(2.2, 3.6), off, leaf, Vector3(1.0, 0.82, 1.0), false)\n\n\nfunc _path() -> void:\n\tvar stone := MatLib.concrete()\n\tfor i in 30:\n\t\tvar z := 30.0 - float(i) * 5.0\n\t\tvar x := sin(float(i) * 0.55) * 2.4\n\t\tBuild.box(props(), Vector3(2.6, 0.10, 2.0), Vector3(x, 0.05, z), stone,\n\t\t\t\tVector3(0, float(i) * 11.0, 0), false)\n\t# a marker stone at the end of the path\n\tBuild.box(props(), Vector3(1.4, 3.0, 0.6), Vector3(0, 1.5, PATH_END + 12.0), MatLib.concrete_dark(),\n\t\t\tVector3(0, 6.0, 0), true)\n\n\nfunc _the_light() -> void:\n\t_light_node = Node3D.new()\n\t_light_node.position = Vector3(0, 0, PATH_END - 6.0)\n\tstructures().add_child(_light_node)\n\n\t# a standing ring of pale stone around the light\n\tfor i in 10:\n\t\tvar a := TAU * float(i) / 10.0\n\t\tBuild.box(_light_node, Vector3(0.8, 4.4, 0.8),\n\t\t\t\tVector3(cos(a) * 7.0, 2.2, sin(a) * 7.0), MatLib.concrete(),\n\t\t\t\tVector3(0, rad_to_deg(a), 0), true)\n\n\tvar core := MatLib.emissive(Color(\"fff4d8\"), 12.0)\n\tBuild.sphere(_light_node, 1.5, Vector3(0, 4.6, 0), core, Vector3.ONE, false)\n\t_bloom = Build.omni(_light_node, Vector3(0, 4.6, 0), Color(\"fff0cf\"), 26.0, 70.0, false, 6.0)\n\n\t# god rays through the gap\n\tfor i in 7:\n\t\tvar a := TAU * float(i) / 7.0\n\t\tBuild.shaft(_light_node, Vector2(4.0, 26.0),\n\t\t\t\tVector3(cos(a) * 2.4, 12.0, sin(a) * 2.4), Vector3(-16.0, rad_to_deg(a), 0),\n\t\t\t\tColor(1.0, 0.95, 0.82), 0.055)\n\tBuild.spot(_light_node, Vector3(0, 12.0, 0), Vector3(-88, 0, 0), Color(\"fff2d6\"), 9.0, 60.0, 44.0,\n\t\t\tfalse, 6.0)\n\n\t_motes = FX.dust(self, 320, Vector3(16, 8, 16), 0.035, Color(1.0, 0.96, 0.85, 0.55))\n\t_motes.position = Vector3(0, 4.0, PATH_END - 6.0)\n\n\tBuild.sun(self, Vector3(-34, 24, 0), Color(\"fff2d8\"), 2.5, 1.4)\n\n\tvar arrive := TriggerZone.make(_light_node, Vector3(0, 2.0, 2.0), Vector3(12, 6, 6), \"light\")\n\tarrive.entered.connect(_on_arrive)\n\n\nfunc _on_arrive(_p: Node) -> void:\n\tif done(\"walk\"):\n\t\treturn\n\tcomplete(\"walk\")\n\tif game != null:\n\t\tawait game.call(\"play_dialogue\", [\n\t\t\t{\"speaker\": \"You\", \"text\": \"The record said there was a door. It did not say it opened onto this.\"},\n\t\t\t{\"speaker\": \"\", \"text\": \"Someone has been looking after it. There are tools by the wall, and the wall is straight.\"},\n\t\t\t{\"speaker\": \"\", \"text\": \"Down the hill, in the wet city, the monolith finishes another century of rain.\"},\n\t\t])\n\tawait get_tree().create_timer(1.2).timeout\n\tfinish()\n\n\nfunc surface_at(_pos: Vector3) -> String:\n\treturn \"grass\"\n\n\nfunc _process(delta: float) -> void:\n\tif _bloom != null:\n\t\t_bloom.light_energy = 26.0 + sin(Time.get_ticks_msec() / 1400.0) * 3.0\n\tif _motes != null and game != null and game.player != null:\n\t\t_motes.position = Vector3(game.player.global_position.x, 4.0, game.player.global_position.z)\n"
}

      func _open_field_radial() -> void:
      	var active := GameManager.active_objective()
      	if active.is_empty():
      		active = "the district is quiet"
      	open_radial([
      		{"id": "objective", "label": "Objective", "hint": active.substr(0, 26)},
      		{"id": "status", "label": "Status", "hint": "vitals and rounds"},
      		{"id": "leave", "label": "Resume", "hint": "back to it"},
      	], "FIELD MENU", func(id: String):
      		match id:
      			"objective":
      				GameManager.toast_message(active)
      			"status":
      				GameManager.toast_message("%d VITALS  %d ROUNDS  %d SPARE"
      						% [roundi(player.health), player.mag, player.reserve]))
      
      
      func _take_screenshot() -> void:
      	await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
      	var img := get_viewport().get_texture().get_image()
      	var path := "user://monolith_%s.png" % Time.get_datetime_string_from_system().replace(":", "-")
      	if img.save_png(path) == OK:
      		GameManager.toast_message("SHOT SAVED")
      
      
      func _on_quit_to_menu() -> void:
      	GameManager.set_paused(false)
      	pause.close()
      	GameManager.save_game()

> _Reasoning_
> CameraRig.gd lines 95-96: 
> ```gdscript
> 		for side in [-0.42, 0.0, 0.42]:
> 			var from := pivot + basis.x * side
> 			var to := from + back * target_arm
> ```
> `side` is a Variant from the untyped array → `pivot + basis.x * side` has no inferred type. Fix: type the loop var `for side: float in [...]` or declare `var from: Vector3 = ...`.
> 
> Let me fix.
> 
> Also check for the same pattern elsewhere. In `Enemy._steer` I use `for a in range(...)` then `deg_to_rad(float(a))` — fine. In `impact` I use `for k in range` — fine.
> 
> Let me fix CameraRig.
